---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Tick
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

---@module Features.Economy.EconomyFormulas
local EconomyFormulas = require("Features/Economy/EconomyFormulas")

---@module Features.Economy.ResearchPlanner
local ResearchPlanner = require("Features/Economy/ResearchPlanner")

---@module Features.Economy.FocusPlanner
local FocusPlanner = require("Features/Economy/FocusPlanner")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatModel
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")

---@module Features.Supply.SupplyModel
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

-- Constants.
local CONSUMER_GOODS_ITEM = "Consumer Goods"
local FULL_HEALTH = 100
local TIER = "E"

-- State.
EconomyPlanner.lastSummary = nil

---Equipment demand of our damaged units, by item (TroopCommon.GetTroopConsumption on the live Model).
---@param world table
---@param healPerTick number
---@return table item -> { demandPerTick, remainingNeed, units }
local function equipmentDemand(world, healPerTick)
	local TroopCommon = GameModules.get("TroopCommon")
	local demand = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		local itemName = unit.replenishSuppliesName

		if itemName ~= nil and unit.health < FULL_HEALTH and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, unit.tile) then
			local consumption = TroopCommon.GetTroopConsumption(unit.model, world.meInstance)
			local ticksToFull = math.ceil((FULL_HEALTH - unit.health) / healPerTick)
			local entry = demand[itemName]

			if entry == nil then
				entry = { demandPerTick = 0, remainingNeed = 0, units = 0 }
				demand[itemName] = entry
			end

			entry.demandPerTick += consumption
			entry.remainingNeed += consumption * ticksToFull
			entry.units += 1
		end
	end

	return demand
end

---Per-tick draw of our units in contact with the enemy, by equipment item.
---@param world table
---@param analysis table?
---@return table item -> consumption per tick
local function frontDraw(world, analysis)
	local draw = {}

	if analysis == nil then
		return draw
	end

	local TroopCommon = GameModules.get("TroopCommon")

	for _, unit in pairs(analysis.contact) do
		local itemName = unit.replenishSuppliesName

		if itemName ~= nil then
			draw[itemName] = (draw[itemName] or 0) + TroopCommon.GetTroopConsumption(unit.model, world.meInstance)
		end
	end

	return draw
end

---1. Production split.
---@param world table
---@param country Instance
---@param analysis table? the front analysis of the same cycle
---@return table facts
local function planProduction(world, country, analysis)
	local items = CountryState.productionItems(country)
	local atWar = next(world.enemies) ~= nil
	local totalFactories = Resolver.requireAttribute(country, "factories", "number")
	local productivity = CountryState.folderNumber(country, "Techs", "factoryProductivity")
	local replenishSpeed = CountryState.folderNumber(country, "Techs", "replenishSpeedModifier")
	local secondsPerDay = GameModules.get("Utils").secondsPerDay
	local troopInfo = GameModules.get("ArmyCommon").troopNameToInfo

	-- Healing speed per tick of the units in play (all troop types share `replenish` in the table,
	-- but it is read per troop to stay exact).
	local healPerTick = nil
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		local info = troopInfo[unit.troopName]

		if info ~= nil and info.replenish ~= nil then
			local perTick = Formula.evaluate("heal.perTick", { replenish = info.replenish, secondsPerDay = secondsPerDay, replenishSpeedModifier = replenishSpeed })

			if healPerTick == nil or perTick < healPerTick then
				healPerTick = perTick
			end
		end
	end

	if totalFactories <= 0 then
		return { skipped = true, totalFactories = totalFactories }
	end

	-- Without units there is nothing to heal.
	local demand = {}
	if healPerTick ~= nil then
		demand = equipmentDemand(world, healPerTick)
	end
	local draw = frontDraw(world, analysis)
	local desired = {}
	local assigned = 0

	if atWar then
		for _, item in ipairs(items) do
			local need = demand[item.name]
			local itemDraw = draw[item.name]
			local factories = 0

			if need ~= nil then
				factories = Formula.evaluate("econ.equipmentFactories", {
					stock = item.stock,
					remainingNeed = need.remainingNeed,
					demandPerTick = need.demandPerTick,
					outputCoef = item.outputCoef,
					factoryProductivity = productivity,
				})
			end

			if itemDraw ~= nil then
				factories = math.max(factories, Formula.evaluate("econ.readinessFactories", {
					stock = item.stock,
					frontDrawPerTick = itemDraw,
					outputCoef = item.outputCoef,
					factoryProductivity = productivity,
				}))
			end

			if factories > 0 then
				desired[item.name] = math.min(factories, totalFactories - assigned)
				assigned += desired[item.name]
			end
		end
	elseif next(demand) ~= nil then
		Logger.info("Features.Economy.EconomyPlanner", "peaceHealingUnfunded", {
			demand = demand,
			reason = "user rule: at peace every factory makes Consumer Goods; damaged units heal only from existing stock",
		})
	end

	desired[CONSUMER_GOODS_ITEM] = totalFactories - assigned

	local changes = {}

	for _, item in ipairs(items) do
		local wanted = desired[item.name] or 0

		if wanted ~= item.factories then
			local weight = wanted / totalFactories

			if weight ~= item.factoriesValue then
				table.insert(changes, { item = item.name, from = item.factories, to = wanted, weight = weight })

				ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
					remote = "SetProductionPercentage",
					args = { item.instance, weight },
					key = "production:" .. item.name .. ":" .. tostring(weight),
					reason = string.format("%s: %d -> %d factories", item.name, item.factories, wanted),
					tier = TIER,
				})
			end
		end
	end

	return { atWar = atWar, totalFactories = totalFactories, demand = demand, frontDraw = draw, desired = desired, changes = changes, healPerTick = healPerTick }
end

---2. Training and manpower.
---@param world table
---@param country Instance
---@param budget table { money }
---@return table facts
local function planTraining(world, country, budget)
	local classes = CountryState.armyClasses(country)
	local counterDemand = {}

	for className in pairs(classes) do
		counterDemand[className] = 0

		for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
			if Formula.evaluate("combat.counter", { attackerClass = className, defenderClass = enemy.class }) > 1 then
				counterDemand[className] += 1
			end
		end
	end

	local ranked = {}
	for className, classState in pairs(classes) do
		table.insert(ranked, {
			id = className,
			score = counterDemand[className] - classState.count,
			facts = { demand = counterDemand[className], count = classState.count, target = classState.target, hasManpower = classState.hasManpower },
			state = classState,
		})
	end

	table.sort(ranked, function(first, second)
		if first.score ~= second.score then
			return first.score > second.score
		end

		return first.id < second.id
	end)

	Formula.recordChoice("E.trainingPriority", ranked, "counter demand minus our count (combat.counter > 1 against enemy units at war)")

	local raised = {}
	local manpowerWanted = false
	local treasury = CountryState.treasury(country)
	local divisionSize = GameModules.get("ArmyCommon").divisionSize

	for _, entry in ipairs(ranked) do
		local classState = entry.state
		local pipelineIdle = classState.target <= classState.count

		if entry.score > 0 and pipelineIdle then
			if not classState.hasManpower and treasury.manpower < divisionSize then
				manpowerWanted = true
			else
				local cost = Formula.evaluate("econ.trainingCost", {
					target = classState.target,
					count = classState.count,
					costIncrease = classState.costIncrease,
					baseCost = classState.baseCost,
				})

				if budget.money >= cost then
					budget.money -= cost
					table.insert(raised, { class = entry.id, cost = cost })

					ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
						remote = "TryIncreaseTarget",
						args = { classState.folder },
						key = "target:" .. entry.id .. ":" .. tostring(classState.target),
						reason = string.format("%s counters %d enemy units, we have %d", entry.id, counterDemand[entry.id], classState.count),
						tier = TIER,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local manpowerBought = nil
	if manpowerWanted then
		local army = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Army")
		local price = Formula.evaluate("econ.manpowerPrice", {
			divisions = treasury.divisions,
			manpower = treasury.manpower,
			divisionSize = divisionSize,
			bonusCoef = Resolver.requireAttribute(army, "bonusCoef", "number"),
			bonusBasePrice = Resolver.requireAttribute(army, "bonusBasePrice", "number"),
		})

		if budget.money >= price then
			budget.money -= price
			manpowerBought = price

			ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
				remote = "TryBuyManpower",
				args = {},
				key = "manpower:" .. tostring(treasury.divisions) .. ":" .. tostring(math.floor(treasury.manpower)),
				reason = "training of a needed class is blocked by manpower",
				tier = TIER,
			})
		end
	end

	return { counterDemand = counterDemand, raised = raised, manpowerWanted = manpowerWanted, manpowerPrice = manpowerBought }
end

---4. One city defence where it turns an unsafe city tile safe.
---@param world table
---@param country Instance
---@param analysis table?
---@param budget table
---@return table? facts
local function planDefense(world, country, analysis, budget)
	if analysis == nil then
		return nil
	end

	local techs = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Techs")
	local strengthModifier = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "cityDefensesStrengthModifier", "number")
	local cities = {}

	for _, city in ipairs(CountryState.cities(country)) do
		cities[city.tileName] = city
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do
		local city = cities[entry.tile]

		if city ~= nil and city.defenses + city.defenseBuildQueue < city.maxDefenses then
			local hypothetical = CombatModel.placedOn(entry.unit, entry.tile)
			hypothetical.defense = entry.unit.defense + strengthModifier
			local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, entry.tile, hypothetical)

			if not threat.breaks then
				local price = Formula.evaluate("econ.defensePrice", {
					cityDefensesPrice = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "cityDefensesPrice", "number"),
					defenses = city.defenses,
					queue = city.defenseBuildQueue,
					cityDefensesPriceMultiplier = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "cityDefensesPriceMultiplier", "number"),
				})

				if budget.money >= price then
					budget.money -= price

					ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
						remote = "PurchaseDefense",
						args = { city.folder },
						key = "defense:" .. city.name .. ":" .. tostring(city.defenses + city.defenseBuildQueue),
						reason = string.format("one more defence holds %s against the adjacent stack (ratio %.2f -> %.2f)", entry.tile, entry.threat.ratio, threat.ratio),
						tier = TIER,
					})

					return { city = city.name, price = price, ratioBefore = entry.threat.ratio, ratioAfter = threat.ratio }
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

---5. One factory in the cheapest city when it pays back before the round ends.
---@param country Instance
---@param budget table
---@return table? facts
local function planFactory(country, budget)
	local period = Tick.periodSeconds()

	if period == nil then
		return nil
	end

	local techs = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Techs")
	local economy = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Economy")
	local mapModel = Resolver.requireChild(workspace, "Map")
	local cities = CountryState.cities(country)
	local treasury = CountryState.treasury(country)

	local allQueued = 0
	for _, city in ipairs(cities) do
		allQueued += city.factoryBuildQueue
	end

	local best = nil
	for _, city in ipairs(cities) do
		if city.factories + city.factoryBuildQueue < city.maxFactories then
			local price = Formula.evaluate("econ.factoryPrice", {
				factoryPrice = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "factoryPrice", "number"),
				cityFactories = city.factories,
				cityQueue = city.factoryBuildQueue,
				factoryPriceMultiplier = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "factoryPriceMultiplier", "number"),
				countryFactories = treasury.factories,
				allQueued = allQueued,
				factoryTotalPriceMultiplier = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "factoryTotalPriceMultiplier", "number"),
			})

			if best == nil or price < best.price then
				best = { city = city, price = price }
			end
		end
	end

	if best == nil then
		return nil
	end

	local payback = Formula.evaluate("econ.factoryPaybackTicks", {
		price = best.price,
		consumerGoodsTaxIncome = Resolver.requireAttribute(economy, "consumerGoodsTaxIncome", "number"),
	})

	local ticksLeft = Formula.evaluate("econ.roundTicksLeft", {
		startTime = Resolver.requireAttribute(mapModel, "startTime", "number"),
		maxGameLength = Resolver.requireAttribute(workspace, "maxGameLength", "number"),
		now = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
		tickPeriod = period,
	})

	local manpowerPrice = Resolver.requireAttribute(techs, "factoryManpowerPrice", "number")

	if payback >= ticksLeft or budget.money < best.price or treasury.manpower < manpowerPrice then
		return { city = best.city.name, price = best.price, paybackTicks = payback, ticksLeft = ticksLeft, bought = false }
	end

	budget.money -= best.price

	ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
		remote = "PurchaseFactory",
		args = { best.city.folder },
		key = "factory:" .. best.city.name .. ":" .. tostring(best.city.factories + best.city.factoryBuildQueue),
		reason = string.format("cheapest factory (%.0f) pays back in %.0f of %.0f ticks left", best.price, payback, ticksLeft),
		tier = TIER,
	})

	return { city = best.city.name, price = best.price, paybackTicks = payback, ticksLeft = ticksLeft, bought = true }
end

---Run the economy for one tick.
---@param world table
---@param analysis table? the front analysis of the same cycle
function EconomyPlanner.run(world, analysis)
	local country = world.meInstance
	local treasury = CountryState.treasury(country)
	local budget = { money = treasury.money }

	local summary = { money = treasury.money }

	if Settings.get("economy.enabled") then
		summary.production = planProduction(world, country, analysis)
		summary.training = planTraining(world, country, budget)
	end

	if Settings.get("research.enabled") then
		summary.research = ResearchPlanner.run(world, country, budget)
	end

	if Settings.get("focus.enabled") then
		summary.focus = FocusPlanner.run(world, country)
	end

	if Settings.get("economy.enabled") then
		summary.defense = planDefense(world, country, analysis, budget)

		if budget.money > (summary.research and summary.research.reservedMoney or 0) then
			local spendable = { money = budget.money - (summary.research and summary.research.reservedMoney or 0) }
			summary.factory = planFactory(country, spendable)
			budget.money = spendable.money + (summary.research and summary.research.reservedMoney or 0)
		end
	end

	summary.moneyLeftInPlan = budget.money
	EconomyPlanner.lastSummary = summary

	Logger.info("Features.Economy.EconomyPlanner", "planned", summary)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Economy.EconomyPlanner", EconomyPlanner)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Economy/FocusPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Economy/FocusPlanner

	Runs one national focus at a time (a second one needs a gamepass, economy.md §4). A focus is a
	candidate when it is not complete, not started, every required focus is complete (the client
	does not enforce this; the AI does), and its PP cost leaves enough political power for the
	user's diplomacy: the largest war-justification cost of our country (Diplomacy SameJustPP /
	NeutralJustPP / OppositeJustPP, T0) stays in reserve so the user can always justify a war.

	Ranking (DESIGN §7.5): combat effects first, then manpower and healing, then economy, then the
	rest, classified from the focus's replicated effect text (effectLines, T3); then the shortest
	duration, then the name. Ideology changes are never made (they reset focuses and are political).
]]

local FocusPlanner = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Utility.Serializer
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

---@module Game.CountryState
local CountryState = require("Game/CountryState")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

-- Constants.
local COMPLETE = 1
local TIER = "F"
local JUSTIFICATION_COST_ATTRIBUTES = { "SameJustPP", "NeutralJustPP", "OppositeJustPP" }
-- Effect-text classes, most wanted first (text fragments of the game's effectLines, T3).
-- The array position is the rank; anything unmatched ranks after the last class.
local EFFECT_CLASSES = {
	{ name = "combat", fragments = { "attack", "defense", "defence", "division", "infantry", "tank", "anti-tank", "experience" } },
	{ name = "manpower", fragments = { "manpower", "healing", "replenish" } },
	{ name = "economy", fragments = { "income", "tax", "factor", "production", "research", "consumer" } },
}
local RANK_REST = #EFFECT_CLASSES + 1

---Class of a focus from its effect text.
---@param effectLinesText string?
---@return number, string
local function classify(effectLinesText)
	if effectLinesText == nil then
		return RANK_REST, "no effectLines"
	end

	local lowered = string.lower(effectLinesText)

	for rank, effectClass in ipairs(EFFECT_CLASSES) do
		for _, fragment in ipairs(effectClass.fragments) do
			if string.find(lowered, fragment, 1, true) then
				return rank, effectClass.name .. " ('" .. fragment .. "')"
			end
		end
	end

	return RANK_REST, "no class fragment"
end

---Plan the focus for one tick.
---@param world table
---@param country Instance
---@return table facts
function FocusPlanner.run(world, country)
	local state = CountryState.focuses(country)

	if #state.current > 0 then
		return { running = state.current }
	end

	local diplomacy = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Diplomacy")
	local reserve = 0
	for _, attributeName in ipairs(JUSTIFICATION_COST_ATTRIBUTES) do
		reserve = math.max(reserve, Resolver.requireAttribute(diplomacy, attributeName, "number"))
	end

	local politicalPower = Resolver.requireAttribute(country, "politicalPower", "number")
	local ranked = {}

	for focusName, focus in pairs(state.focuses) do
		if focus.progress < COMPLETE and not focus.purchased then
			local unlocked = true

			for _, requiredName in ipairs(focus.required) do
				local requiredFocus = state.focuses[requiredName]

				if requiredFocus == nil or requiredFocus.progress < COMPLETE then
					unlocked = false
				end
			end

			if unlocked and politicalPower - focus.ppCost >= reserve then
				local rank, why = classify(focus.effectLines)

				table.insert(ranked, {
					id = focusName,
					score = rank,
					facts = { class = why, ppCost = focus.ppCost, duration = focus.duration },
					focus = focus,
				})
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(ranked, function(first, second)
		if first.score ~= second.score then
			return first.score < second.score
		end

		if first.focus.duration ~= second.focus.duration then
			return first.focus.duration < second.focus.duration
		end

		return first.id < second.id
	end)

	if #ranked == 0 then
		return { chosen = nil, politicalPower = politicalPower, reserve = reserve }
	end

	Formula.recordChoice("F.nextFocus", ranked, "effect class, then shortest duration; PP above the justification reserve")

	local chosen = ranked[1]

	ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
		remote = "TryStartFocus",
		args = { chosen.focus.instance },
		key = "focus:" .. chosen.id,
		reason = string.format("%s (%s), %d PP of %.1f, reserve %d for the user's diplomacy", chosen.id, chosen.facts.class, chosen.focus.ppCost, politicalPower, reserve),
		tier = TIER,
	})

	Logger.debug("Features.Economy.FocusPlanner", "effectLines", { focus = chosen.id, text = chosen.focus.effectLines and Serializer.toJsonSafe(chosen.focus.effectLines, {}) or nil })

	return { chosen = chosen.id, politicalPower = politicalPower, reserve = reserve }
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Economy.FocusPlanner", FocusPlanner)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Economy/ResearchPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Economy/ResearchPlanner

	Keeps the research slots busy (DESIGN §7.4). A tech is a candidate when it is not complete, not
	in a slot, and every tech it requires is complete (the client does not enforce prerequisites;
	the AI checks them). Candidates are ranked by the TechCatalog tier (unit levels of fielded
	classes, healing speed, attack/defence boosts of fielded classes, economy, other), then by the
	shortest duration, then by price, then by name.

	Paying and starting are two remotes: Research.TryPurchaseTech (money, sets `purchased`) and
	Research.StartResearch (into the first free slot). StartResearch is sent only while a slot is
	free, because with both slots full the server fires OnAllSlotsOccupied and the game opens a
	gamepass prompt (economy.md §4). When the wanted tech is not affordable yet, its price is
	reserved so lower tiers (factories) do not spend it.
]]

local ResearchPlanner = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Game.CountryState
local CountryState = require("Game/CountryState")

---@module Features.Economy.TechCatalog
local TechCatalog = require("Features/Economy/TechCatalog")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

-- Constants.
local COMPLETE = 1
local TIER = "R"

---Classes we field (units on the map) plus classes we can train (Army.Classes folders).
---@param world table
---@param country Instance
---@return table set
local function fieldedClasses(world, country)
	local classes = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		classes[unit.class] = true
	end

	for className in pairs(CountryState.armyClasses(country)) do
		classes[className] = true
	end

	return classes
end

---Plan research for one tick.
---@param world table
---@param country Instance
---@param budget table { money } decreased by what this planner commits
---@return table facts { chosen?, reservedMoney }
function ResearchPlanner.run(world, country, budget)
	local slots = CountryState.researchSlots(country)
	local freeSlot = nil
	local inSlot = {}

	local slotNames = {}
	for slotName in pairs(slots) do
		table.insert(slotNames, slotName)
	end
	table.sort(slotNames)

	for _, slotName in ipairs(slotNames) do
		local techName = slots[slotName]

		if techName == false then
			if freeSlot == nil then
				freeSlot = slotName
			end
		else
			inSlot[techName] = true
		end
	end

	if freeSlot == nil then
		return { freeSlot = false, reservedMoney = 0 }
	end

	local techs = CountryState.techs(country)
	local fielded = fieldedClasses(world, country)
	local ranked = {}

	for techName, tech in pairs(techs) do
		local entry = TechCatalog.entry(techName)

		if entry ~= nil and tech.progress < COMPLETE and not inSlot[techName] then
			local unlocked = true
			local missing = nil

			for _, requiredName in ipairs(entry.required) do
				local requiredTech = techs[requiredName]

				if requiredTech == nil or requiredTech.progress < COMPLETE then
					unlocked = false
					missing = requiredName
				end
			end

			local tier = entry.tier
			if entry.class ~= nil and not fielded[entry.class] then
				-- A level or boost of a class we neither field nor train helps nothing yet.
				tier = TechCatalog.TIERS.other
			end

			if unlocked then
				table.insert(ranked, {
					id = techName,
					score = tier,
					facts = { tier = tier, duration = tech.duration, price = tech.price, purchased = tech.purchased, evidence = entry.evidence },
					tech = tech,
				})
			else
				Logger.trace("Features.Economy.ResearchPlanner", "locked", { tech = techName, missing = missing })
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(ranked, function(first, second)
		if first.score ~= second.score then
			return first.score < second.score
		end

		if first.tech.duration ~= second.tech.duration then
			return first.tech.duration < second.tech.duration
		end

		if first.tech.price ~= second.tech.price then
			return first.tech.price < second.tech.price
		end

		return first.id < second.id
	end)

	if #ranked == 0 then
		return { freeSlot = freeSlot, chosen = nil, reservedMoney = 0 }
	end

	Formula.recordChoice("R.nextTech", ranked, "TechCatalog tier, then shortest duration, then price")

	local chosen = ranked[1]

	if chosen.tech.purchased then
		ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
			remote = "StartResearch",
			args = { chosen.tech.instance },
			key = "start:" .. chosen.id,
			reason = string.format("slot %s is free; %s is paid", freeSlot, chosen.id),
			tier = TIER,
		})

		return { freeSlot = freeSlot, chosen = chosen.id, action = "start", reservedMoney = 0 }
	end

	if budget.money >= chosen.tech.price then
		budget.money -= chosen.tech.price

		ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
			remote = "TryPurchaseTech",
			args = { chosen.tech.instance },
			key = "buy:" .. chosen.id,
			reason = string.format("slot %s is free; best candidate %s (tier %d)", freeSlot, chosen.id, chosen.score),
			tier = TIER,
		})

		return { freeSlot = freeSlot, chosen = chosen.id, action = "purchase", price = chosen.tech.price, reservedMoney = 0 }
	end

	return { freeSlot = freeSlot, chosen = chosen.id, action = "saving", price = chosen.tech.price, reservedMoney = chosen.tech.price }
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Economy.ResearchPlanner", ResearchPlanner)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Economy/TechCatalog"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Economy/TechCatalog

	What each research does, derived from the game's own research catalogue
	(ReplicatedStorage.Common.ResearchCommon.techs, T3) rather than from a hand-written list:
	- a unit-level tech's description embeds ArmyCommon.GetTroopBaseStatsRTF(<troop name>) (for
	  example "Tank Parts II" -> Tanks2), so the troop it unlocks is found by matching that exact text;
	  completing it replaces every Model of the class with the new level (ground-combat.md §2);
	- a boost tech is a key of ArmyCommon.upgrades, whose attribute names (TanksAttackBoost,
	  InfantryDefenseBoost, ...) name the class and the stat;
	- the remaining techs are classified from their description text: healing speed, factory limits
	  and manpower. The rule that matched is kept as evidence; a tech no rule matches is "other".
]]

local TechCatalog = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

-- Constants.
-- Research priority tiers (DESIGN §7.4), most important first.
local TIER_UNIT_LEVEL = 1
local TIER_HEALING = 2
local TIER_BOOST = 3
local TIER_ECONOMY = 4
local TIER_OTHER = 5
-- Description fragments (T3 text of ResearchCommon descriptions) for the text-classified tiers.
local HEALING_FRAGMENT = "healing speed"
local ECONOMY_FRAGMENTS = { "factory limit", "manpower" }
-- T3 TroopCommon.GetTroopClass: class = troop name without digits.
local DIGITS_PATTERN = "%d"
local CLASS_NAMES = { "AntiTank", "Infantry", "Tanks" }

TechCatalog.TIERS = { unitLevel = TIER_UNIT_LEVEL, healing = TIER_HEALING, boost = TIER_BOOST, economy = TIER_ECONOMY, other = TIER_OTHER }

-- State.
TechCatalog.entries = nil

---Build the catalogue from the game modules.
---@return table techName -> { tier, class?, troopName?, stat?, required, evidence }
function TechCatalog.build()
	local ResearchCommon = GameModules.get("ResearchCommon")
	local ArmyCommon = GameModules.get("ArmyCommon")

	local statsTextByTroop = {}
	for troopName in pairs(ArmyCommon.troopNameToInfo) do
		statsTextByTroop[troopName] = ArmyCommon.GetTroopBaseStatsRTF(troopName)
	end

	local entries = {}
	local counts = {}

	for techName, tech in pairs(ResearchCommon.techs) do
		local description = type(tech.description) == "string" and tech.description or ""
		local entry = { tier = TIER_OTHER, required = tech.required or {}, evidence = "no rule matched" }

		for troopName, statsText in pairs(statsTextByTroop) do
			if statsText ~= "" and string.find(description, statsText, 1, true) then
				entry.tier = TIER_UNIT_LEVEL
				entry.troopName = troopName
				entry.class = string.gsub(troopName, DIGITS_PATTERN, "")
				entry.evidence = "description embeds GetTroopBaseStatsRTF(" .. troopName .. ")"
			end
		end

		if entry.tier == TIER_OTHER and ArmyCommon.upgrades[techName] ~= nil then
			for attributeName in pairs(ArmyCommon.upgrades[techName]) do
				for _, className in ipairs(CLASS_NAMES) do
					local statPart = string.match(attributeName, "^" .. className .. "(%a+)Boost$")

					if statPart == "Attack" or statPart == "Defense" then
						entry.tier = TIER_BOOST
						entry.class = className
						entry.stat = statPart
						entry.evidence = "ArmyCommon.upgrades." .. attributeName
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if entry.tier == TIER_OTHER then
			local lowered = string.lower(description)

			if string.find(lowered, HEALING_FRAGMENT, 1, true) then
				entry.tier = TIER_HEALING
				entry.evidence = "description mentions '" .. HEALING_FRAGMENT .. "'"
			else
				for _, fragment in ipairs(ECONOMY_FRAGMENTS) do
					if entry.tier == TIER_OTHER and string.find(lowered, fragment, 1, true) then
						entry.tier = TIER_ECONOMY
						entry.evidence = "description mentions '" .. fragment .. "'"
					end
				end
			end
		end

		entries[techName] = entry
		counts[entry.tier] = (counts[entry.tier] or 0) + 1
	end

	TechCatalog.entries = entries

	Logger.info("Features.Economy.TechCatalog", "built", { techs = counts })

	return entries
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Economy.TechCatalog", TechCatalog)

-- Hot-path functions, installed after instrumentation: they run thousands of times per planning
-- cycle (profiled in the first live run: 1.1 M trace records in 78 s). Failures still raise with a
-- named message, which the traced caller logs with its traceback.

---The catalogue entry of a tech (building the catalogue on first use).
---@param techName string
---@return table?
function TechCatalog.entry(techName)
	if TechCatalog.entries == nil then
		TechCatalog.build()
	end

	return TechCatalog.entries[techName]
end

return TechCatalog


]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/AttackPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/AttackPlanner

	Attack tiers of the front plan (DESIGN §5.4). Every attack is launched as a stack whose stacked
	ratio is >= 1 without a tie (Tactics.winningStack): the formula's own break-even, recorded as a
	91-97 % win rate against 16-54 % below it (players.md §5.3).
	- A1 relief: beat the enemy units that threaten one of our unsafe tiles (called from T1). An
	  attacked attacker switches to Defending and stops its own attack (ground-combat.md §3.7).
	- A0 enemy capitals: a unit entering an enemy's own capital annexes the whole country
	  (24/24 recorded annexations). Empty capital: the earliest arrival walks in. Held capital: a
	  winning stack attacks, and when the defender would retreat its free exits are occupied first so
	  it dies and our attacker moves in by itself. Out of reach: units are staged on the capital's
	  free neighbours until the staged stack would win. The puppet prompt is never answered
	  (diplomacy is the user's; unanswered means annexation).
	- A2 kills: defenders that die when beaten (dieOnNextRetreat, or no free own or friendly
	  neighbour; flagged defenders that were attacked died 32/34 and 18/20), cut-off units, surround
	  kills (occupy every exit before the defender breaks) and retreat chases (entering a retreating
	  unit's destination first kills it, 19/19).
	- A3 cuts and A4 captures: walk-ins into empty enemy tiles ranked by how many enemy units they
	  cut off from supply, then cities; plain winning attacks whose attackers stay safe afterwards.
	- Review: our running attacks stay together; one that no longer wins is recorded once
	  (Army.HaltTroop does not stop an attack, so it runs to the 20 floor unless other tiers join it).
]]

local AttackPlanner = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

---@module Game.Arrows
local Arrows = require("Game/Arrows")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatModel
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")

---@module Features.Movement.Pathing
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")

---@module Features.Supply.SupplyModel
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")

---@module Features.Front.FrontAnalyzer
local FrontAnalyzer = require("Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer")

---@module Features.Front.Tactics
local Tactics = require("Features/Front/Tactics")

-- Constants.
local TIER_RELIEF = "A1"
local TIER_CAPITAL = "A0"
local TIER_KILL = "A2"
local TIER_CUT = "A3"
local TIER_CAPTURE = "A4"
local TIER_REVIEW = "AR"
-- T1: a tile touching two of our tiles survives the loss of either; one touch is a single point of
-- failure for its supply (supply rule, war-and-loop.md §3.13).
local BROAD_FRONT_NEIGHBOURS = 2
local NOT_TARGETABLE_STATES = { Retreating = true, Dead = true }
-- T5: path[1] is the unit's own tile; the route it walks starts at the second entry.
local FIRST_ROUTE_TILE = 2

-- State.
AttackPlanner.reportedLosing = {}

---Our available unflagged units next to a tile that may attack a unit of the given owner from there.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param tileName string
---@param targetOwner string
---@return table
local function strikers(world, plan, tileName, targetOwner)
	local units = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(Tactics.availableNeighbours(world, plan, tileName)) do
		if not unit.flagged and Tactics.mayAttackFromHot(world, unit.tile, targetOwner) then
			table.insert(units, unit)
		end
	end

	return units
end

---Assign the new attackers of a stack.
---@param plan table
---@param stack table from Tactics.winningStack
---@param targetTile string
---@param purpose string
---@param tier string
---@param reason string
local function launch(plan, stack, targetTile, purpose, tier, reason)
	for _, entry in ipairs(stack.attackers) do
		plan:assign(entry.unit, { entry.unit.tile, targetTile }, purpose, tier, reason, {
			ratio = stack.result.ratio,
			breakSeconds = stack.result.breakSeconds,
			stackSize = #stack.all,
		})
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(stack.all) do
		if entry.existing and plan:isAvailable(entry.unit) then
			plan:hold(entry.unit, purpose, tier, "already attacking in a winning stack")
		end
	end
end

---Cover every free exit of a defender with a walk-in that lands before the stack breaks it.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param defender table
---@param stack table
---@param purpose string
---@param tier string
---@return boolean covered, table facts
local function surround(world, plan, defender, stack, purpose, tier)
	local exits = FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, defender)
	local breakSeconds = stack.result.breakSeconds
	local used = {}

	for _, entry in ipairs(stack.all) do
		used[entry.unit.id] = true
	end

	if #exits == 0 then
		return true, { exits = 0 }
	end

	if breakSeconds == nil then
		return false, { reason = "no break time" }
	end

	local ourMovement = world.paths.movement[world.me] or Ownership.movementCountries(world.me)
	world.paths.movement[world.me] = ourMovement

	local chosen = {}

	for _, exitName in ipairs(exits) do
		local owner = world.tileOwner[exitName]

		if owner == nil or not ourMovement[owner] or not plan:isFreeDestination(exitName) then
			return false, { reason = "exit not enterable", exit = exitName, owner = owner }
		end

		local best = nil
		for _, unit in ipairs(Tactics.availableNeighbours(world, plan, exitName)) do
			if not used[unit.id] and not unit.flagged then
				local hop = Pathing.hopSeconds(world.paths, unit, unit.tile, exitName)
				local placed = CombatModel.placedOn(unit, exitName)
				local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, exitName, placed)

				if hop < breakSeconds and not threat.breaks and (best == nil or hop < best.hop) then
					best = { unit = unit, hop = hop, threatRatio = threat.ratio }
				end
			end
		end

		if best == nil then
			return false, { reason = "no unit reaches the exit before the break", exit = exitName, breakSeconds = breakSeconds }
		end

		used[best.unit.id] = true
		table.insert(chosen, { exit = exitName, choice = best })
	end

	for _, item in ipairs(chosen) do
		plan:assign(item.choice.unit, { item.choice.unit.tile, item.exit }, purpose, tier, "occupy the defender's exit so it dies when beaten", {
			defenderTile = defender.tile,
			hopSeconds = item.choice.hop,
			breakSeconds = breakSeconds,
			threatRatio = item.choice.threatRatio,
		})
	end

	return true, { exits = #exits }
end

---A1: beat the units threatening one unsafe tile of ours.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param entry table unsafe entry from FrontAnalyzer
---@return table { remainingThreat = engagement or nil, facts }
function AttackPlanner.relieve(world, plan, entry)
	local defender = entry.unit
	local ordered = table.clone(entry.threat.attackers)

	-- Units already attacking the tile first, then the heaviest hitters.
	local damageByUnit = {}
	for index, attackerEntry in ipairs(entry.threat.attackers) do
		damageByUnit[attackerEntry.unit.id] = entry.threat.damages and entry.threat.damages[index] or 0
	end

	table.sort(ordered, function(first, second)
		local firstAttacking = first.unit.state == "Attacking" and first.unit.targetTile == entry.tile
		local secondAttacking = second.unit.state == "Attacking" and second.unit.targetTile == entry.tile

		if firstAttacking ~= secondAttacking then
			return firstAttacking
		end

		if damageByUnit[first.unit.id] ~= damageByUnit[second.unit.id] then
			return damageByUnit[first.unit.id] > damageByUnit[second.unit.id]
		end

		return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
	end)

	local relieved = {}
	local remainingThreat = nil
	local facts = { attackers = #ordered, relieved = 0 }

	for _, attackerEntry in ipairs(ordered) do
		local enemy = attackerEntry.unit
		local stack = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, enemy.tile, strikers(world, plan, enemy.tile, enemy.owner))

		if stack ~= nil then
			launch(plan, stack, enemy.tile, "relief", TIER_RELIEF, "beat a unit threatening " .. entry.tile)
			relieved[enemy.id] = true
			facts.relieved += 1

			local remaining = {}
			for _, other in ipairs(entry.threat.attackers) do
				if not relieved[other.unit.id] then
					table.insert(remaining, other)
				end
			end

			if #remaining == 0 then
				remainingThreat = { ratio = 0, breaks = false }
				break
			end

			remainingThreat = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, remaining, defender, entry.tile, "defender")

			if not remainingThreat.breaks then
				break
			end
		end
	end

	facts.remainingRatio = remainingThreat and remainingThreat.ratio or entry.threat.ratio

	return { remainingThreat = remainingThreat, facts = facts }
end

---A0: take enemy capitals.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
function AttackPlanner.capitals(world, plan)
	local enemyNames = {}
	for enemyName in pairs(world.enemies) do
		table.insert(enemyNames, enemyName)
	end

	-- Server-AI countries first: they rarely react (15-16 %) and fall fastest (players.md §10.14).
	table.sort(enemyNames, function(first, second)
		local firstAI = Ownership.isAI(first)
		local secondAI = Ownership.isAI(second)

		if firstAI ~= secondAI then
			return firstAI
		end

		return first < second
	end)

	for _, enemyName in ipairs(enemyNames) do
		local capitalTile = world.enemyCapitals[enemyName]

		if capitalTile ~= nil and world.tileOwner[capitalTile] == enemyName then
			local occupant = world.unitsByTile[capitalTile]

			if occupant == nil then
				local incumbent = nil
				for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
					if unit.targetTile == capitalTile then
						incumbent = unit
					end
				end

				if incumbent ~= nil then
					if plan:isAvailable(incumbent) then
						plan:hold(incumbent, "capitalWalkIn", TIER_CAPITAL, "already entering the empty enemy capital")
					end
				else
					local candidates = {}
					for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
						if not unit.flagged then
							table.insert(candidates, unit)
						end
					end

					local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, candidates, capitalTile, nil)
					plan:decide(TIER_CAPITAL, "walkIn:" .. enemyName, ranked, "earliest arrival at the empty enemy capital")

					if best ~= nil then
						plan:assign(best.unit, best.path, "capitalWalkIn", TIER_CAPITAL, enemyName .. "'s capital is empty: entering it annexes the country", { seconds = best.seconds, enemy = enemyName })
					end
				end
			elseif world.enemies[occupant.owner] and not NOT_TARGETABLE_STATES[occupant.state] then
				local stack, stackFacts = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, capitalTile, strikers(world, plan, capitalTile, occupant.owner))

				if stack ~= nil then
					local dies = FrontAnalyzer.diesWhenBeaten(world, plan, occupant)
					local surroundFacts = nil

					if not dies then
						local _, facts = surround(world, plan, occupant, stack, "capitalSurround", TIER_CAPITAL)
						surroundFacts = facts
					end

					launch(plan, stack, capitalTile, "capitalStrike", TIER_CAPITAL, "beat the defender of " .. enemyName .. "'s capital")

					local logStrike = Logger.info
					if #stack.attackers == 0 then
						-- The running strike re-evaluated with no new attacker: detail only.
						logStrike = Logger.debug
					end

					logStrike("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "capitalStrike", {
						enemy = enemyName,
						tile = capitalTile,
						defender = occupant.troopName,
						ratio = stack.result.ratio,
						breakSeconds = stack.result.breakSeconds,
						defenderDiesWhenBeaten = dies,
						surround = surroundFacts,
					})
				else
					AttackPlanner.approachCapital(world, plan, enemyName, capitalTile, occupant, stackFacts)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

---Stage units on the free neighbours of a held enemy capital until the staged stack would win.
---Staging is not a reaction, so it is planned on tick cycles only; units already walking to a
---staging tile of this capital count at their destination, so each tick adds only what is missing.
---One distance field (the client's path metric) from the staging tiles serves every candidate.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param enemyName string
---@param capitalTile string
---@param defender table
---@param adjacentFacts table
function AttackPlanner.approachCapital(world, plan, enemyName, capitalTile, defender, adjacentFacts)
	local entries = Tactics.existingAttackers(world, capitalTile)
	local inStack = {}

	for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
		inStack[entry.unit.id] = true
	end

	for _, unit in ipairs(strikers(world, plan, capitalTile, defender.owner)) do
		table.insert(entries, { unit = unit, fromTile = unit.tile, adjacent = true })
		inStack[unit.id] = true
	end

	for unitId, commitment in pairs(plan.commitments) do
		local unit = world.unitsById[unitId]

		if unit ~= nil and not inStack[unitId] and commitment.purpose == "capitalApproach" and commitment.facts ~= nil and commitment.facts.capitalTile == capitalTile then
			table.insert(entries, { unit = CombatModel.placedOn(unit, commitment.destination), fromTile = commitment.destination, committed = true })
			inStack[unitId] = true
		end
	end

	-- Combat cycles only keep an approach in progress together: units next to the capital wait for
	-- the ones still walking there; new staging is planned on the next tick.
	if world.cycleKind ~= "tick" then
		local approachRunning = false
		for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
			if entry.committed then
				approachRunning = true
			end
		end

		if approachRunning then
			for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
				if entry.adjacent and plan:isAvailable(entry.unit) then
					plan:hold(entry.unit, "capitalStack", TIER_CAPITAL, "waits for the staged units to strike together")
				end
			end
		end

		return
	end

	local result = nil
	if #entries > 0 then
		result = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, entries, defender, capitalTile, "attacker")
	end

	local ourMovement = world.paths.movement[world.me] or Ownership.movementCountries(world.me)
	world.paths.movement[world.me] = ourMovement

	local staging = {}
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[capitalTile].landNeighbours) do
		local owner = world.tileOwner[neighbourName]

		if owner ~= nil and ourMovement[owner] and plan:isFreeDestination(neighbourName) and world.unitsByTile[neighbourName] == nil then
			table.insert(staging, neighbourName)
		end
	end

	local planned = {}

	if (result == nil or not result.breaks or result.tie) and #staging > 0 then
		local field = Pathing.distanceField(staging, function(tileName)
			local owner = world.tileOwner[tileName]
			return owner ~= nil and ourMovement[owner] and world.unitsByTile[tileName] == nil and plan.locks[tileName] == nil and plan.claims[tileName] == nil
		end)

		local routes = {}
		for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
			if not unit.flagged and not inStack[unit.id] then
				local firstStep = nil
				local firstDistance = math.huge

				for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].landNeighbours) do
					local neighbourDistance = field.distance[neighbourName]

					if neighbourDistance ~= nil then
						local total = neighbourDistance + MapGraph.edgeLength(unit.tile, neighbourName)
						if total < firstDistance then
							firstDistance = total
							firstStep = neighbourName
						end
					end
				end

				if firstStep ~= nil then
					local path = { unit.tile, firstStep }
					local step = firstStep

					while field.towardSource[step] ~= nil do
						step = field.towardSource[step]
						table.insert(path, step)
					end

					table.insert(routes, { unit = unit, path = path, studs = firstDistance })
				end
			end
		end

		table.sort(routes, function(first, second)
			if first.studs ~= second.studs then
				return first.studs < second.studs
			end

			return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
		end)

		-- A moving unit that is attacked stops and defends where it stands, so every tile of a route
		-- (the staging tile included) must hold against the enemy units next to it (second live run:
		-- single units sent 13-21 tiles deep into Russia). Checked closest first, only until the
		-- staged stack wins.
		local function routeIsSafe(route)
			for index = FIRST_ROUTE_TILE, #route.path do
				local tileName = route.path[index]
				local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, tileName, CombatModel.placedOn(route.unit, tileName))

				if threat.breaks then
					return false
				end
			end

			return true
		end

		local taken = {}
		for _, route in ipairs(routes) do
			local stagingTile = route.path[#route.path]

			if not taken[stagingTile] and #route.path <= Pathing.MAX_PATH_TILES and routeIsSafe(route) then
				taken[stagingTile] = true
				table.insert(planned, { unit = route.unit, path = route.path, staging = stagingTile, studs = route.studs })
				table.insert(entries, { unit = CombatModel.placedOn(route.unit, stagingTile), fromTile = stagingTile })

				result = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, entries, defender, capitalTile, "attacker")

				if result.breaks and not result.tie then
					break
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if result ~= nil and result.breaks and not result.tie then
		for _, item in ipairs(planned) do
			plan:assign(item.unit, item.path, "capitalApproach", TIER_CAPITAL, "stage next to " .. enemyName .. "'s capital", {
				capitalTile = capitalTile,
				staging = item.staging,
				studs = item.studs,
				stagedRatio = result.ratio,
			})
		end

		for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
			if entry.adjacent and plan:isAvailable(entry.unit) then
				plan:hold(entry.unit, "capitalStack", TIER_CAPITAL, "waits for the staged units to strike together")
			end
		end
	else
		Logger.debug("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "capitalOutOfReach", {
			enemy = enemyName,
			tile = capitalTile,
			defender = defender.troopName,
			adjacentFacts = adjacentFacts,
			stagingTiles = #staging,
			stackSize = #entries,
			bestStagedRatio = result and result.ratio or 0,
		})
	end
end

---A2: kills.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
function AttackPlanner.kills(world, plan)
	local targets = {}

	for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
		if not NOT_TARGETABLE_STATES[enemy.state] then
			local dies, why = FrontAnalyzer.diesWhenBeaten(world, plan, enemy)
			local cut = enemy.noSupply and not SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, enemy.tile)

			if dies or cut then
				table.insert(targets, { enemy = enemy, dies = dies, why = why, cut = cut })
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(targets, function(first, second)
		if first.dies ~= second.dies then
			return first.dies
		end

		if first.enemy.health ~= second.enemy.health then
			return first.enemy.health < second.enemy.health
		end

		return first.enemy.id < second.enemy.id
	end)

	local targeted = {}

	for _, target in ipairs(targets) do
		local enemy = target.enemy
		local stack = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, enemy.tile, strikers(world, plan, enemy.tile, enemy.owner))

		if stack ~= nil and #stack.attackers > 0 then
			local purpose = "kill"
			if not target.dies then
				purpose = "cutOffAttack"
			end

			launch(plan, stack, enemy.tile, purpose, TIER_KILL, target.why)
			targeted[enemy.id] = true
		end
	end

	-- Surround kills: occupy every exit of a defender we can beat, before it breaks.
	for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
		if not targeted[enemy.id] and not NOT_TARGETABLE_STATES[enemy.state] and not enemy.flagged then
			local strikeUnits = strikers(world, plan, enemy.tile, enemy.owner)

			if #strikeUnits > 0 then
				local stack = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, enemy.tile, strikeUnits)

				if stack ~= nil and #stack.attackers > 0 then
					local exits = FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, enemy)

					if #exits > 0 then
						local covered, facts = surround(world, plan, enemy, stack, "surround", TIER_KILL)

						if covered then
							launch(plan, stack, enemy.tile, "surroundKill", TIER_KILL, "exits occupied: the beaten defender dies")
							targeted[enemy.id] = true

							Logger.info("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "surroundKill", {
								tile = enemy.tile,
								troop = enemy.troopName,
								ratio = stack.result.ratio,
								breakSeconds = stack.result.breakSeconds,
								exits = facts.exits,
							})
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	-- Chase: enter a retreating enemy's destination before it arrives.
	local ourMovement = world.paths.movement[world.me] or Ownership.movementCountries(world.me)
	world.paths.movement[world.me] = ourMovement

	for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
		if enemy.state == "Retreating" and enemy.targetTile ~= nil then
			local landing = enemy.targetTile
			local owner = world.tileOwner[landing]

			if owner ~= nil and ourMovement[owner] and world.unitsByTile[landing] == nil and plan:isFreeDestination(landing) then
				local endTime = Arrows.endTime("RetreatArrow", enemy.tile, landing)

				if endTime ~= nil then
					local remaining = endTime - world.serverTime
					local candidates = {}

					for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
						if not unit.flagged then
							table.insert(candidates, unit)
						end
					end

					local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, candidates, landing, remaining)

					if best ~= nil and best.seconds < remaining then
						plan:decide(TIER_KILL, "chase:" .. landing, ranked, "arrives before the retreating unit lands")
						plan:assign(best.unit, best.path, "chase", TIER_KILL, "entering a retreat destination first kills the retreating unit", {
							seconds = best.seconds,
							retreatRemainingSeconds = remaining,
							troop = enemy.troopName,
						})
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

---Our land tiles next to a tile, counting tiles this plan's walk-ins will capture.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param tileName string
---@return number
local function ownNeighbourCount(world, plan, tileName)
	local count = 0

	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		local claimant = plan.claims[neighbourName]
		-- Claims come from this plan's moves and from moves of ours still walking (commitments).
		local claimedByOurMove = claimant ~= nil and ((plan.byUnit[claimant] ~= nil and not plan.byUnit[claimant].isAttack) or plan.commitments[claimant] ~= nil)

		if world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me or claimedByOurMove then
			count += 1
		end
	end

	return count
end

---A3 and A4: walk-ins and plain attacks.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
function AttackPlanner.captures(world, plan)
	local walkIns = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
		if not unit.flagged and unit.state ~= "Attacking" then
			for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].landNeighbours) do
				if Tactics.isEnemyTile(world, neighbourName) and world.unitsByTile[neighbourName] == nil and plan:isFreeDestination(neighbourName) then
					local owner = world.tileOwner[neighbourName]
					local cut = SupplyModel.unitsCutByLosing(world, owner, neighbourName)

					table.insert(walkIns, {
						unit = unit,
						tile = neighbourName,
						cutUnits = #cut,
						isCity = MapGraph.tiles[neighbourName].cityName ~= nil,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(walkIns, function(first, second)
		if first.cutUnits ~= second.cutUnits then
			return first.cutUnits > second.cutUnits
		end

		if first.isCity ~= second.isCity then
			return first.isCity
		end

		if first.tile ~= second.tile then
			return first.tile < second.tile
		end

		return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
	end)

	for _, candidate in ipairs(walkIns) do
		local unit = candidate.unit

		if plan:isAvailable(unit) and plan:isFreeDestination(candidate.tile) then
			local placed = CombatModel.placedOn(unit, candidate.tile)
			local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, candidate.tile, placed)
			local originExposed = #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, unit.tile) > 0

			-- A captured tile keeps supply only through our land next to it; a tile touching one of
			-- our tiles is cut off by a single enemy capture behind it (Germany run: 1-tile tentacles
			-- up to 7 tiles deep were cut, fought at x0.6 and two units died). Advances therefore
			-- broaden the front: the new tile must touch at least two of our tiles, unless it is a
			-- supply source itself (an enemy port city becomes ours with its port).
			local broad = ownNeighbourCount(world, plan, candidate.tile) >= BROAD_FRONT_NEIGHBOURS
			local cityFolder = nil
			local becomesSource = false

			if candidate.isCity then
				cityFolder = GameModules.get("MapData").tileNameToCity[candidate.tile]
			end

			if cityFolder ~= nil then
				local ownerCountry = Ownership.country(world.tileOwner[candidate.tile])
				local cities = ownerCountry and ownerCountry:FindFirstChild("Cities")
				local folder = cities and cities:FindFirstChild(cityFolder.name)
				becomesSource = folder ~= nil and folder:GetAttribute("isPort") == true
			end

			if not threat.breaks and not originExposed and (broad or becomesSource) then
				local tier = TIER_CAPTURE
				local purpose = "walkIn"
				if candidate.cutUnits > 0 then
					tier = TIER_CUT
					purpose = "cutWalkIn"
				end

				plan:assign(unit, { unit.tile, candidate.tile }, purpose, tier, "capture an empty enemy tile", {
					cutUnits = candidate.cutUnits,
					isCity = candidate.isCity,
					threatRatio = threat.ratio,
				})
			end
		end
	end

	-- Plain attacks: winning stacks whose attackers stay safe afterwards.
	local candidates = {}
	for _, enemy in ipairs(world.enemyUnits) do
		if not NOT_TARGETABLE_STATES[enemy.state] then
			table.insert(candidates, enemy)
		end
	end

	table.sort(candidates, function(first, second)
		if first.health ~= second.health then
			return first.health < second.health
		end

		return first.id < second.id
	end)

	for _, enemy in ipairs(candidates) do
		local strikeUnits = strikers(world, plan, enemy.tile, enemy.owner)

		if #strikeUnits > 0 then
			local stack = Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, enemy.tile, strikeUnits)

			if stack ~= nil and #stack.attackers > 0 then
				local allSafe = true
				local safetyFacts = {}

				for index, entry in ipairs(stack.all) do
					local healthAfter = stack.result.healthsAtBreak and stack.result.healthsAtBreak[index] or entry.unit.health
					local safe, facts = Tactics.safeAfterFight(world, entry, healthAfter, enemy.tile)
					table.insert(safetyFacts, facts)

					if not safe then
						allSafe = false
					end
				end

				if allSafe then
					launch(plan, stack, enemy.tile, "attack", TIER_CAPTURE, "winning attack; every attacker stays safe")
				else
					Logger.debug("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "attackUnsafeAfter", { tile = enemy.tile, ratio = stack.result.ratio, safety = safetyFacts })
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

---True when our running attack keeps an enemy unit from breaking one of our units: the enemy is
---Defending because we attack it (an attacked unit cannot start its own attack), and with it added
---to the stack next to one of our tiles, that tile's defender would break while today it does not.
---@param world table
---@param enemy table
---@return boolean, string?
local function pinsAThreat(world, enemy)
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[enemy.tile].landNeighbours) do
		local ours = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]

		if ours ~= nil and ours.owner == world.me then
			local current = CombatModel.potentialAttackers(world, neighbourName, world.enemies)
			local withEnemy = table.clone(current)
			local alreadyIn = false

			for _, entry in ipairs(current) do
				if entry.unit.id == enemy.id then
					alreadyIn = true
				end
			end

			if not alreadyIn then
				table.insert(withEnemy, { unit = enemy, fromTile = enemy.tile })

				local before = #current > 0 and CombatModel.engage(world.combat, current, ours, neighbourName, "defender") or { breaks = false }
				local after = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, withEnemy, ours, neighbourName, "defender")

				if after.breaks and not before.breaks then
					return true, neighbourName
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return false, nil
end

---Review our running attacks that no tier of this plan kept: continue the ones that still win or
---pin a threat, halt the rest (an attacker otherwise fights on to the 20 floor).
---@param world table
---@param plan table
function AttackPlanner.reviewOngoing(world, plan)
	local reviewed = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if unit.state == "Attacking" and unit.targetTile ~= nil and plan:isUnassigned(unit) and not reviewed[unit.targetTile] then
			local targetTile = unit.targetTile
			reviewed[targetTile] = true

			local defender = world.unitsByTile[targetTile]
			local entries = Tactics.existingAttackers(world, targetTile)

			if defender ~= nil and #entries > 0 then
				local result = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, entries, defender, targetTile, "attacker")
				local pinning, pinnedTile = pinsAThreat(world, defender)

				-- Army.HaltTroop does not stop an attack (second live run: 50 halts, every attacker kept
				-- attacking), so a losing attack runs on to the 20 floor; the other tiers may join it
				-- (winningStack counts running attackers). The review keeps winning and pinning attacks
				-- together and records the losing ones once per target.
				local losing = not (result.breaks and not result.tie) and not pinning

				for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
					if entry.unit.owner == world.me and plan:isUnassigned(entry.unit) then
						if losing then
							plan:hold(entry.unit, "attackLosing", TIER_REVIEW, "the running attack no longer wins; nothing in the game stops it")
						elseif pinning then
							plan:hold(entry.unit, "attackPin", TIER_REVIEW, "the attack keeps the enemy from breaking " .. tostring(pinnedTile))
						else
							plan:hold(entry.unit, "attackContinue", TIER_REVIEW, "the running attack still wins")
						end
					end
				end

				if losing and AttackPlanner.reportedLosing[targetTile] == nil then
					AttackPlanner.reportedLosing[targetTile] = true
					Logger.info("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", "attackLosing", { target = targetTile, ratio = result.ratio, attackers = #entries })
				elseif not losing then
					AttackPlanner.reportedLosing[targetTile] = nil
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.AttackPlanner", AttackPlanner)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/DefensePlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/DefensePlanner

	Defence tiers of the front plan (DESIGN §5.3):
	- T0 capital: the capital always holds our unit; a free safe neighbour is kept as its retreat lane
	  (a beaten defender with no free own or friendly neighbour dies, 3/3 capital deaths were
	  encircled capitals); while enemies are near, an unflagged reserve stands on another neighbour to
	  re-enter (re-entry saved Budapest and Ankara); a flagged defender is rotated out while no enemy
	  is next to the capital.
	- T1 unsafe tiles, most severe consequence first: relief attacks that beat the threatening units,
	  else a replacement whose threat on the tile does not break it and that wins the walk-in race,
	  else the loss is accepted and logged with its reason.
	- T2 lanes: every front defender keeps a free exit; a unit that took the last one steps away.
	- T3 rotation: flagged units (dieOnNextRetreat) next to an enemy move to a safe, supplied tile and
	  rest (healing needs Idle); flagged units out of reach are held so no tier uses them.
	- T4 gaps and reserves: our empty tiles next to an enemy are covered by units that arrive before
	  the enemy could walk in; every other free unit moves toward the tiles that need support.
]]

local DefensePlanner = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatModel
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")

---@module Features.Movement.Pathing
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")

---@module Features.Supply.SupplyModel
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")

---@module Features.Front.FrontAnalyzer
local FrontAnalyzer = require("Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer")

---@module Features.Front.Tactics
local Tactics = require("Features/Front/Tactics")

---@module Features.Front.WorldView
local WorldView = require("Features/Front/WorldView")

-- Constants.
local TIER_CAPITAL = "T0"
local TIER_UNSAFE = "T1"
local TIER_LANES = "T2"
local TIER_ROTATION = "T3"
local TIER_RESERVES = "T4"
local STATES_THAT_CANNOT_MOVE = { Defending = true, Retreating = true, Dead = true }

-- State.
DefensePlanner.trappedReported = {}
DefensePlanner.unguardedReported = false

---True when any enemy unit stands next to the tile or next to one of its land neighbours.
---@param world table
---@param tileName string
---@return boolean
local function enemyNearZone(world, tileName)
	if #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, tileName) > 0 then
		return true
	end

	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		if world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, neighbourName) > 0 then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

---A tile where one of our units can rest: ours, free, supplied, no enemy unit next to it.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param tileName string
---@return boolean
local function isRestTile(world, plan, tileName)
	return world.tileOwner[tileName] == world.me
		and plan:isFreeDestination(tileName)
		and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, tileName) == 0
		and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, tileName)
end

---T0: guard our capital.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
function DefensePlanner.capital(world, plan)
	local capitalTile = world.capitalTile

	if capitalTile == nil then
		Logger.error("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "capitalUnknown", { reason = world.capitalReason })
		return
	end

	local occupant = world.unitsByTile[capitalTile]
	local zoneThreatened = enemyNearZone(world, capitalTile)

	if occupant ~= nil then
		DefensePlanner.unguardedReported = false
	end

	if occupant ~= nil and occupant.owner == world.me then
		if occupant.flagged and not zoneThreatened and plan:isAvailable(occupant) then
			-- Rotate a flagged defender out while nothing can attack the capital (DESIGN §5.3 T0).
			local replacements = {}
			for _, unit in ipairs(Tactics.availableNeighbours(world, plan, capitalTile)) do
				if not unit.flagged and unit.id ~= occupant.id and unit.owner == world.me then
					table.insert(replacements, unit)
				end
			end

			local restPath = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, occupant, function(tileName)
				return isRestTile(world, plan, tileName)
			end, true)

			if #replacements > 0 and restPath ~= nil then
				local replacement = replacements[1]
				plan:assign(occupant, restPath, "capitalRotateOut", TIER_CAPITAL, "flagged capital defender rests while no enemy is near", { health = occupant.health })
				plan:assign(replacement, { replacement.tile, capitalTile }, "capitalRotateIn", TIER_CAPITAL, "unflagged neighbour takes the capital", { health = replacement.health, from = replacement.tile })
			else
				plan:hold(occupant, "capitalHold", TIER_CAPITAL, "flagged, but no replacement or rest tile")
				Logger.warn("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "flaggedCapitalDefender", { tile = capitalTile, replacements = #replacements, restPath = restPath ~= nil })
			end
		else
			plan:hold(occupant, "capitalHold", TIER_CAPITAL, "the capital is never left empty")
		end
	elseif occupant == nil then
		local incumbent = nil
		for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
			if unit.targetTile == capitalTile and plan.byUnit[unit.id] == nil then
				incumbent = unit
			end
		end

		if incumbent ~= nil then
			plan:hold(incumbent, "capitalGuard", TIER_CAPITAL, "already entering the empty capital")
		else
			local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, plan:availableUnits(), capitalTile, nil)
			local purpose = "capitalGuard"

			-- An empty capital falls to the first enemy that walks in, so when no free unit can reach it,
			-- a unit under the user's manual order is taken (logged: it overrides the user).
			if best == nil then
				local userUnits = {}
				for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
					if plan.blockedUnits[unit.id] and plan.byUnit[unit.id] == nil and not STATES_THAT_CANNOT_MOVE[unit.state] then
						table.insert(userUnits, unit)
					end
				end

				best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, userUnits, capitalTile, nil)
				purpose = "capitalGuardOverridesUser"
			end

			plan:decide(TIER_CAPITAL, purpose, ranked, "earliest arrival at our empty capital")

			if best ~= nil then
				DefensePlanner.unguardedReported = false
				plan:assign(best.unit, best.path, purpose, TIER_CAPITAL, "our capital is empty", { seconds = best.seconds })

				if purpose == "capitalGuardOverridesUser" then
					Logger.warn("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "capitalGuardOverridesUser", { unit = best.unit.id, tile = best.unit.tile, seconds = best.seconds })
				end
			elseif not DefensePlanner.unguardedReported then
				-- Reported once per episode; the plan retries every cycle.
				DefensePlanner.unguardedReported = true
				Logger.error("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "capitalUnguarded", { tile = capitalTile, reason = "no unit of ours can reach it" })
			end
		end
	else
		Logger.error("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "capitalHeldByOther", { tile = capitalTile, owner = occupant.owner, troop = occupant.troopName })
	end

	-- Lane: one free neighbour, not next to an enemy, stays empty for a beaten defender.
	local laneTile = nil
	local capitalAsUnit = { owner = world.me, tile = capitalTile }

	for _, exitName in ipairs(FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, capitalAsUnit)) do
		if laneTile == nil and plan.locks[exitName] == nil and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, exitName) == 0 then
			laneTile = exitName
		end
	end

	if laneTile == nil then
		for _, exitName in ipairs(FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, capitalAsUnit)) do
			if laneTile == nil and plan.locks[exitName] == nil then
				laneTile = exitName
			end
		end
	end

	if laneTile ~= nil then
		plan:lock(laneTile, "lane:" .. capitalTile)
	elseif zoneThreatened then
		-- Free one: move a neighbour that is not itself holding a front tile.
		local cleared = false

		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[capitalTile].landNeighbours) do
			local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]

			if not cleared and unit ~= nil and plan:isAvailable(unit) and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, neighbourName) == 0 then
				local path = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit, function(tileName)
					return tileName ~= capitalTile and isRestTile(world, plan, tileName)
				end, true)

				if path ~= nil then
					plan:assign(unit, path, "capitalLaneClear", TIER_CAPITAL, "the capital had no free neighbour", { from = neighbourName })
					plan:lock(neighbourName, "lane:" .. capitalTile)
					cleared = true
				end
			end
		end

		if not cleared then
			Logger.warn("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "capitalWithoutLane", { tile = capitalTile, reason = "every neighbour is enemy-held or held by a unit that cannot move" })
		end
	end

	-- Reserve: an unflagged unit next to the capital while enemies are near.
	if zoneThreatened then
		local hasReserve = false

		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[capitalTile].landNeighbours) do
			local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]

			if unit ~= nil and unit.owner == world.me and not unit.flagged and not STATES_THAT_CANNOT_MOVE[unit.state] then
				hasReserve = true

				if plan:isAvailable(unit) then
					plan:hold(unit, "capitalReserve", TIER_CAPITAL, "re-enters the capital if its defender leaves")
				end

				break
			end
		end

		if not hasReserve then
			local bestPlacement = nil
			local bestRanked = {}

			for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[capitalTile].landNeighbours) do
				if world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me and plan:isFreeDestination(neighbourName) then
					local candidates = {}
					for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
						if not unit.flagged then
							table.insert(candidates, unit)
						end
					end

					local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, candidates, neighbourName, bestPlacement and bestPlacement.seconds or nil)

					if best ~= nil and (bestPlacement == nil or best.seconds < bestPlacement.seconds) then
						bestPlacement = best
						bestRanked = ranked
					end
				end
			end

			plan:decide(TIER_CAPITAL, "capitalReserve", bestRanked, "earliest unflagged arrival next to the capital")

			if bestPlacement ~= nil then
				plan:assign(bestPlacement.unit, bestPlacement.path, "capitalReserve", TIER_CAPITAL, "enemies near the capital and no reserve next to it", { seconds = bestPlacement.seconds })
			end
		end
	end
end

---T1: unsafe tiles.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param analysis table
---@param attackPlanner table Features/Front/AttackPlanner (relief attacks)
function DefensePlanner.unsafe(world, plan, analysis, attackPlanner)
	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do
		local defender = entry.unit
		local tileName = entry.tile
		local fixed = false

		-- (b) Relief: beat the units that threaten the tile.
		local relieved = attackPlanner.relieve(world, plan, entry)

		if relieved.remainingThreat ~= nil and not relieved.remainingThreat.breaks then
			fixed = true
		end

		-- (a) Replacement by a unit that holds the tile and wins the walk-in race.
		if not fixed and plan:isAvailable(defender) then
			local enemyEntry = FrontAnalyzer.enemyEntrySeconds(world, tileName)
			local lanes = FrontAnalyzer.safeLanes(world, plan, defender)
			local ranked = {}

			if #lanes > 0 then
				for _, candidate in ipairs(Tactics.availableNeighbours(world, plan, tileName)) do
					if candidate.id ~= defender.id and candidate.owner == world.me and not candidate.flagged and world.tileOwner[candidate.tile] == world.me then
						local placed = CombatModel.placedOn(candidate, tileName)
						local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, tileName, placed)
						local hop = Pathing.hopSeconds(world.paths, candidate, candidate.tile, tileName)
						local originExposed = #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, candidate.tile) > 0

						local qualifies = not threat.breaks and (enemyEntry == nil or hop < enemyEntry) and not originExposed

						table.insert(ranked, {
							id = candidate.id,
							score = threat.ratio,
							facts = { troop = candidate.troopName, from = candidate.tile, threatRatio = threat.ratio, hopSeconds = hop, enemyEntrySeconds = enemyEntry, originExposed = originExposed, qualifies = qualifies },
							unit = candidate,
							qualifies = qualifies,
						})
					end
				end
			end

			table.sort(ranked, function(first, second)
				if first.qualifies ~= second.qualifies then
					return first.qualifies
				end

				if first.score ~= second.score then
					return first.score < second.score
				end

				return first.id < second.id
			end)

			plan:decide(TIER_UNSAFE, "replacement:" .. tileName, ranked, "lowest threat ratio among replacements that win the walk-in race")

			local best = ranked[1]
			if best ~= nil and best.qualifies then
				plan:assign(defender, { tileName, lanes[1] }, "swapOut", TIER_UNSAFE, "a better defender takes the tile", { threatRatio = entry.threat.ratio, lane = lanes[1] })
				plan:assign(best.unit, { best.unit.tile, tileName }, "swapIn", TIER_UNSAFE, "holds the tile against the adjacent stack", best.facts)
				fixed = true
			end
		end

		if not fixed then
			local dies, why = FrontAnalyzer.diesWhenBeaten(world, plan, defender)

			Logger.debug("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "unsafeAccepted", {
				tile = tileName,
				troop = defender.troopName,
				health = defender.health,
				threatRatio = entry.threat.ratio,
				breakSeconds = entry.threat.breakSeconds,
				consequence = entry.consequence,
				diesWhenBeaten = dies,
				why = why,
				relief = relieved.facts,
			})
		end
	end
end

---T2: keep a free exit behind every front defender.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param analysis table
function DefensePlanner.lanes(world, plan, analysis)
	local contactTiles = {}
	for tileName in pairs(analysis.contact) do
		table.insert(contactTiles, tileName)
	end
	table.sort(contactTiles)

	for _, tileName in ipairs(contactTiles) do
		local defender = analysis.contact[tileName]
		local exits = FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, defender)

		if #exits > 0 then
			local chosen = nil
			for _, exitName in ipairs(exits) do
				if chosen == nil and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, exitName) == 0 and plan.locks[exitName] == nil then
					chosen = exitName
				end
			end

			if chosen == nil then
				chosen = exits[1]
			end

			plan:lock(chosen, "lane:" .. tileName)
		else
			local cleared = false

			for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
				local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]

				if not cleared and unit ~= nil and plan:isAvailable(unit) and analysis.contact[neighbourName] == nil then
					local path = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit, function(candidateTile)
						return candidateTile ~= neighbourName and isRestTile(world, plan, candidateTile)
					end, true)

					if path ~= nil then
						plan:assign(unit, path, "laneClear", TIER_LANES, "the front defender next to it had no free exit", { defenderTile = tileName, from = neighbourName })
						plan:lock(neighbourName, "lane:" .. tileName)
						cleared = true
					end
				end
			end

			if not cleared then
				Logger.debug("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "noLane", { tile = tileName, troop = defender.troopName })
			end
		end
	end
end

---T3: rest flagged units, and bring cut-off units back to supplied land.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
function DefensePlanner.rotation(world, plan)
	-- A unit on our land that the supply pass cannot reach loses x noSupplyCombatCoef, x0.3 speed
	-- and all healing at the second missed pass (user rule U1); it walks back to the nearest
	-- supplied tile of ours before that happens.
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if not unit.flagged and plan:isAvailable(unit) and world.tileOwner[unit.tile] == world.me and not SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, unit.tile) then
			local isSuppliedFree = function(tileName)
				return world.tileOwner[tileName] == world.me and plan:isFreeDestination(tileName) and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, tileName)
			end

			local path = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit, isSuppliedFree, true)
			if path == nil then
				path = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit, isSuppliedFree, false)
			end

			if path ~= nil then
