				plan:assign(unit, path, "resupply", TIER_ROTATION, "cut off from supply: back to supplied land", { health = unit.health, noSupply = unit.noSupply })
			else
				Logger.debug("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "cutOffNoRoute", { unit = unit.id, tile = unit.tile })
			end
		end
	end

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if unit.flagged and plan:isAvailable(unit) then
			local exposure = #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, unit.tile)

			if exposure > 0 then
				local isRest = function(tileName)
					return isRestTile(world, plan, tileName)
				end

				-- Staying next to an enemy means death at the first beating, so a risky route beats no
				-- route (Germany run: two flagged units with no safe route were held and killed).
				local path = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit, isRest, true)
				local how = "safe route"

				if path == nil then
					path = Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit, isRest, false)
					how = "route past enemy units"
				end

				if path == nil then
					local bestTile = nil
					local bestExposure = exposure

					for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].landNeighbours) do
						local owner = world.tileOwner[neighbourName]
						local neighbourExposure = #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, neighbourName)

						if owner ~= nil and WorldView.friendsOf(world, world.me)[owner] and plan:isFreeDestination(neighbourName) and neighbourExposure < bestExposure then
							bestTile = neighbourName
							bestExposure = neighbourExposure
						end
					end

					if bestTile ~= nil then
						path = { unit.tile, bestTile }
						how = string.format("one tile to fewer enemy neighbours (%d -> %d)", exposure, bestExposure)
					end
				end

				if path ~= nil then
					plan:assign(unit, path, "rest", TIER_ROTATION, "flagged unit next to an enemy dies when beaten", { health = unit.health, how = how })
				else
					plan:hold(unit, "rest", TIER_ROTATION, "flagged, no safe tile reachable")

					-- Once per unit and tile: the same trapped unit is re-planned every cycle.
					if DefensePlanner.trappedReported[unit.id] ~= unit.tile then
						DefensePlanner.trappedReported[unit.id] = unit.tile
						Logger.warn("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "flaggedTrapped", { unit = unit.id, tile = unit.tile, troop = unit.troopName, health = unit.health })
					end
				end
			else
				plan:hold(unit, "rest", TIER_ROTATION, "flagged: heals only while Idle")
			end
		end
	end
end

---T4a: cover our empty tiles the enemy could walk into, with units that arrive first.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param analysis table
function DefensePlanner.gaps(world, plan, analysis)
	for _, gap in ipairs(analysis.gaps) do
		if plan:isFreeDestination(gap.tile) then
			local candidates = {}
			for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
				if not unit.flagged and analysis.contact[unit.tile] == nil then
					table.insert(candidates, unit)
				end
			end

			if #candidates == 0 then
				break
			end

			local best, ranked = Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, candidates, gap.tile, gap.enemyEntrySeconds)

			if best ~= nil then
				local placed = CombatModel.placedOn(best.unit, gap.tile)
				local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, gap.tile, placed)

				if not threat.breaks then
					plan:decide(TIER_RESERVES, "coverGap:" .. gap.tile, ranked, "earliest arrival before the enemy could walk in")
					plan:assign(best.unit, best.path, "coverGap", TIER_RESERVES, "our empty tile next to an enemy", {
						seconds = best.seconds,
						enemyEntrySeconds = gap.enemyEntrySeconds,
						threatRatio = threat.ratio,
						consequence = gap.consequence,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

---T4b: stage every other free unit toward the tiles that need support (tick cycles only).
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param analysis table
function DefensePlanner.stage(world, plan, analysis)
	-- Support tiles: free tiles of ours next to our threatened or contact tiles, else our border.
	local supportTiles = {}
	local seen = {}

	local function addSupportAround(tileName)
		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
			if not seen[neighbourName] and world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == world.me and plan:isFreeDestination(neighbourName) and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, neighbourName) == 0 then
				seen[neighbourName] = true
				table.insert(supportTiles, neighbourName)
			end
		end
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do
		addSupportAround(entry.tile)
	end

	if #supportTiles == 0 then
		local contactTiles = {}
		for tileName in pairs(analysis.contact) do
			table.insert(contactTiles, tileName)
		end
		table.sort(contactTiles)

		for _, tileName in ipairs(contactTiles) do
			addSupportAround(tileName)
		end
	end

	if #supportTiles == 0 then
		for tileName in pairs(world.ourTiles) do
			if not seen[tileName] and plan:isFreeDestination(tileName) then
				for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
					if not seen[tileName] and Tactics.isEnemyTile(world, neighbourName) then
						seen[tileName] = true
						table.insert(supportTiles, tileName)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		table.sort(supportTiles)
	end

	if #supportTiles == 0 then
		return
	end

	local assigned = 0
	local rounds = 0

	-- Rounds: one distance field per round; every mover follows it to its nearest support tile, the
	-- closest mover per tile wins it, and the losers try again next round with the tiles left.
	while #supportTiles > 0 do
		-- A unit already next to a contact tile is in a support position; moving it between two such
		-- tiles gains nothing (second live run: one unit bounced between 6445 and 6493 eight times).
		local movers = {}
		for _, unit in ipairs(plan:availableUnits()) do
			if not unit.flagged and analysis.contact[unit.tile] == nil and unit.state == "Idle" then
				-- On the front edge (next to a contact tile or to enemy land) a unit is where the front
				-- needs it; staging it back undoes the advance that put it there (Germany run: one unit
				-- alternated between walk-ins and staging moves 11 times).
				local inPosition = false
				for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].landNeighbours) do
					if analysis.contact[neighbourName] ~= nil or Tactics.isEnemyTile(world, neighbourName) then
						inPosition = true
					end
				end

				if not inPosition then
					table.insert(movers, unit)
				end
			end
		end

		if #movers == 0 then
			break
		end

		rounds += 1

		local field = Pathing.distanceField(supportTiles, function(tileName)
			return world.tileOwner[tileName] == world.me and world.unitsByTile[tileName] == nil and plan.locks[tileName] == nil and plan.claims[tileName] == nil
		end)

		local routes = {}

		for _, unit in ipairs(movers) do
			local firstStep = nil
			local firstDistance = math.huge

			for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].landNeighbours) do
				local neighbourDistance = field.distance[neighbourName]

				if neighbourDistance ~= nil then
					local total = neighbourDistance + MapGraph.edgeLength(unit.tile, neighbourName)
					if total < firstDistance then
						firstDistance = total
						firstStep = neighbourName
					end
				end
			end

			if firstStep ~= nil then
				local path = { unit.tile, firstStep }
				local step = firstStep

				while field.towardSource[step] ~= nil do
					step = field.towardSource[step]
					table.insert(path, step)
				end

				path = Tactics.truncatePath(path)
				table.insert(routes, { unit = unit, path = path, destination = path[#path], studs = firstDistance })
			end
		end

		table.sort(routes, function(first, second)
			if first.studs ~= second.studs then
				return first.studs < second.studs
			end

			return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
		end)

		local takenThisRound = {}
		local assignedThisRound = 0

		for _, route in ipairs(routes) do
			if not takenThisRound[route.destination] then
				takenThisRound[route.destination] = true
				plan:assign(route.unit, route.path, "reserve", TIER_RESERVES, "free unit moves to support the front", { destination = route.destination, studs = route.studs })
				assigned += 1
				assignedThisRound += 1
			end
		end

		if assignedThisRound == 0 then
			break
		end

		local remaining = {}
		for _, tileName in ipairs(supportTiles) do
			if not takenThisRound[tileName] then
				table.insert(remaining, tileName)
			end
		end
		supportTiles = remaining
	end

	Logger.debug("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", "reservesStaged", { assigned = assigned, rounds = rounds, supportTilesLeft = #supportTiles })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.DefensePlanner", DefensePlanner)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer

	Reads the front for one cycle (DESIGN §5.1-5.2):
	- contact tiles: our occupied tiles with an enemy unit next to them;
	- threat on each contact tile: the worst stack of adjacent enemy units against its defender
	  (CombatModel.threat, pessimistic by construction); a threat that breaks the defender makes the
	  tile unsafe;
	- gaps: our empty tiles next to an enemy unit, with the soonest an enemy could walk in;
	- consequence of losing a tile, as a lexicographic class: our capital, a capital neighbour, a city,
	  a tile whose loss cuts our units off from supply, any other tile.
	Exits and lanes (free neighbour tiles a beaten unit can retreat onto) are computed here for both
	sides, because they decide whether a beaten defender retreats or dies (user rule U2).
]]

local FrontAnalyzer = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatModel
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")

---@module Features.Movement.Pathing
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")

---@module Features.Supply.SupplyModel
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")

---@module Features.Front.WorldView
local WorldView = require("Features/Front/WorldView")

-- Constants.
-- Lexicographic consequence classes (DESIGN §5.3 tier 1 order), most severe first.
local CONSEQUENCE_CAPITAL = 0
local CONSEQUENCE_CAPITAL_NEIGHBOUR = 1
local CONSEQUENCE_CITY = 2
local CONSEQUENCE_SUPPLY = 3
local CONSEQUENCE_OTHER = 4
local STATES_THAT_CANNOT_MOVE = { Defending = true, Retreating = true, Dead = true }
-- Log only: how many of the most urgent gaps the analysis record lists in full.
local LOGGED_GAPS = 3

FrontAnalyzer.CONSEQUENCE = {
	capital = CONSEQUENCE_CAPITAL,
	capitalNeighbour = CONSEQUENCE_CAPITAL_NEIGHBOUR,
	city = CONSEQUENCE_CITY,
	supply = CONSEQUENCE_SUPPLY,
	other = CONSEQUENCE_OTHER,
}

---Consequence class of losing one of our tiles.
---@param world table
---@param tileName string
---@return number, table? units cut when the class is supply
local function consequenceOf(world, tileName)
	if tileName == world.capitalTile then
		return CONSEQUENCE_CAPITAL, nil
	end

	if world.capitalTile ~= nil then
		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[world.capitalTile].landNeighbours) do
			if neighbourName == tileName then
				return CONSEQUENCE_CAPITAL_NEIGHBOUR, nil
			end
		end
	end

	if MapGraph.tiles[tileName].cityName ~= nil then
		return CONSEQUENCE_CITY, nil
	end

	local cut = SupplyModel.unitsCutByLosing(world, world.me, tileName)
	if #cut > 0 then
		return CONSEQUENCE_SUPPLY, cut
	end

	return CONSEQUENCE_OTHER, nil
end

---Analyse the front.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@return table
function FrontAnalyzer.analyze(world, plan)
	local analysis = {
		contact = {},
		threats = {},
		unsafe = {},
		gaps = {},
		consequence = {},
	}

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		local adjacentEnemies = FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, unit.tile)

		if #adjacentEnemies > 0 then
			analysis.contact[unit.tile] = unit

			local threat = CombatModel.threat(world.combat, world, unit.tile, unit)
			analysis.threats[unit.tile] = threat

			if threat.breaks then
				local consequence, cutUnits = consequenceOf(world, unit.tile)
				analysis.consequence[unit.tile] = consequence

				table.insert(analysis.unsafe, {
					tile = unit.tile,
					unit = unit,
					threat = threat,
					consequence = consequence,
					cutUnits = cutUnits and #cutUnits or 0,
					flagged = unit.flagged,
				})
			end
		end
	end

	-- Most severe consequence first, then the strongest threat, then the tile name for a stable order.
	table.sort(analysis.unsafe, function(first, second)
		if first.consequence ~= second.consequence then
			return first.consequence < second.consequence
		end

		if first.threat.ratio ~= second.threat.ratio then
			return first.threat.ratio > second.threat.ratio
		end

		return first.tile < second.tile
	end)

	for tileName in pairs(world.ourTiles) do
		if world.unitsByTile[tileName] == nil then
			local entrySeconds, entrant = FrontAnalyzer.enemyEntrySeconds(world, tileName)

			if entrySeconds ~= nil then
				local consequence = consequenceOf(world, tileName)

				table.insert(analysis.gaps, {
					tile = tileName,
					enemyEntrySeconds = entrySeconds,
					enemyUnitId = entrant.id,
					consequence = consequence,
				})
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(analysis.gaps, function(first, second)
		if first.consequence ~= second.consequence then
			return first.consequence < second.consequence
		end

		if first.enemyEntrySeconds ~= second.enemyEntrySeconds then
			return first.enemyEntrySeconds < second.enemyEntrySeconds
		end

		return first.tile < second.tile
	end)

	local unsafeSummary = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do
		table.insert(unsafeSummary, {
			tile = entry.tile,
			troop = entry.unit.troopName,
			health = entry.unit.health,
			flagged = entry.flagged,
			ratio = entry.threat.ratio,
			breakSeconds = entry.threat.breakSeconds,
			attackers = #entry.threat.attackers,
			consequence = entry.consequence,
		})
	end

	local contactCount = 0
	for _ in pairs(analysis.contact) do
		contactCount += 1
	end

	Logger.debug("Features.Front.FrontAnalyzer", "analysis", {
		contactTiles = contactCount,
		unsafe = unsafeSummary,
		gaps = #analysis.gaps,
		firstGaps = { table.unpack(analysis.gaps, 1, math.min(LOGGED_GAPS, #analysis.gaps)) },
	})

	return analysis
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.FrontAnalyzer", FrontAnalyzer)

-- Hot-path helpers, installed after instrumentation.

---Enemy units on land tiles next to a tile.
---@param world table
---@param tileName string
---@return table array of snapshots
function FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, tileName)
	local found = {}

	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]

		if unit ~= nil and world.enemies[unit.owner] then
			table.insert(found, unit)
		end
	end

	return found
end

---Soonest an adjacent enemy unit able to move could enter a tile (one hop), and that unit.
---@param world table
---@param tileName string
---@return number?, table?
function FrontAnalyzer.enemyEntrySeconds(world, tileName)
	local best = nil
	local bestUnit = nil

	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]

		if unit ~= nil and world.enemies[unit.owner] and not STATES_THAT_CANNOT_MOVE[unit.state] then
			local seconds = Pathing.hopSeconds(world.paths, unit, neighbourName, tileName)

			if best == nil or seconds < best then
				best = seconds
				bestUnit = unit
			end
		end
	end

	return best, bestUnit
end

---Free exits of a unit: neighbour land tiles owned by its country or a friend of it, holding no
---live unit and not claimed by our plan. A beaten defender without an exit dies (user rule U2;
---war-and-loop.md §3.14: 0 unflagged defenders with a free exit died).
---@param world table
---@param plan table?
---@param unit table
---@return table array of tile names
function FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, unit)
	local friends = WorldView.friendsOf(world, unit.owner)
	local exits = {}

	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].landNeighbours) do
		local owner = world.tileOwner[neighbourName]

		local free = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName] == nil or (plan ~= nil and plan.vacating[neighbourName] == true and unit.owner == world.me)

		if owner ~= nil and friends[owner] and free then
			local claimed = plan ~= nil and (plan.claims[neighbourName] ~= nil)

			if not claimed then
				table.insert(exits, neighbourName)
			end
		end
	end

	return exits
end

---Safe lanes of one of our units: exits that no enemy unit is next to.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param unit table
---@return table array of tile names
function FrontAnalyzer.safeLanes(world, plan, unit)
	local lanes = {}

	for _, exitName in ipairs(FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, unit)) do
		if plan.locks[exitName] == nil or plan.locks[exitName] == "lane:" .. unit.tile then
			if #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, exitName) == 0 then
				table.insert(lanes, exitName)
			end
		end
	end

	return lanes
end

---True when a beaten defender would die instead of retreating: flagged, or no exit.
---@param world table
---@param plan table?
---@param unit table
---@return boolean, string
function FrontAnalyzer.diesWhenBeaten(world, plan, unit)
	if unit.flagged or unit.liveFlag then
		return true, "carries dieOnNextRetreat"
	end

	if #FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, unit) == 0 then
		return true, "no free own or friendly neighbour"
	end

	return false, "has a free exit"
end

return FrontAnalyzer

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/FrontPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/FrontPlanner

	Runs the front tiers in their fixed order (DESIGN §5). Each tier only uses the units and tiles
	earlier tiers left free, so the order is the policy:
	  retreat locks -> T0 capital -> T1 unsafe tiles (A1 relief inside) -> T2 lanes -> T3 rotation
	  -> A0 enemy capitals -> A2 kills -> A3/A4 cuts and captures -> review of running attacks
	  -> T4a gaps -> T4b reserve staging (tick cycles only).
	Each tier is profiled; the plan summary is logged at the end of the cycle.
]]

local FrontPlanner = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Profiler
local Profiler = require("Utility/Profiler")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

---@module Features.Front.Plan
local Plan = require("Features/Front/Plan")

---@module Features.Front.FrontAnalyzer
local FrontAnalyzer = require("Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer")

---@module Features.Front.RetreatManager
local RetreatManager = require("Features/Front/RetreatManager")

---@module Features.Front.DefensePlanner
local DefensePlanner = require("Features/Front/DefensePlanner")

---@module Features.Front.AttackPlanner
local AttackPlanner = require("Features/Front/AttackPlanner")

---@module Features.Supply.SupplyModel
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")

---Lock our spawn tile: new units appear on Army.priorityCity only while its tile is free
---(ground-commands.md §3.6), so no tier may park a unit there.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
local function lockSpawnTile(world, plan)
	local army = world.meInstance:FindFirstChild("Army")
	local cityName = army and army:GetAttribute("priorityCity")

	if type(cityName) ~= "string" or cityName == "" then
		return
	end

	local cities = world.meInstance:FindFirstChild("Cities")
	local cityFolder = cities and cities:FindFirstChild(cityName)
	local tileName = cityFolder and cityFolder:GetAttribute("tileName")

	if type(tileName) == "string" then
		plan:lock(tileName, "spawn:" .. cityName)
	end
end

---Plan the front for one cycle. A combat cycle (an instant reaction) skips reserve staging, the
---only tier that is not a reaction to the event; a tick cycle runs every tier.
---@param world table
---@param blockedUnits table set of unit ids the plan must not touch
---@param commitments table unit id -> order of ours in progress (OrderExecutor.commitments)
---@param kind string "tick" | "combat"
---@return table plan, table analysis
function FrontPlanner.plan(world, blockedUnits, commitments, kind)
	local plan = Plan.new(world, blockedUnits, commitments)
	local analysis = Profiler.run("Front_Analyze", FrontAnalyzer.analyze, world, plan)

	lockSpawnTile(world, plan)

	plan:beginTier("T0")
	Profiler.run("Front_RetreatLocks", RetreatManager.protect, world, plan, analysis)
	Profiler.run("Front_T0_Capital", DefensePlanner.capital, world, plan)

	plan:beginTier("T1")
	Profiler.run("Front_T1_Unsafe", DefensePlanner.unsafe, world, plan, analysis, AttackPlanner)

	plan:beginTier("T2")
	Profiler.run("Front_T2_Lanes", DefensePlanner.lanes, world, plan, analysis)

	plan:beginTier("T3")
	Profiler.run("Front_T3_Rotation", DefensePlanner.rotation, world, plan)

	if Settings.get("front.attack") then
		plan:beginTier("A0")
		Profiler.run("Front_A0_Capitals", AttackPlanner.capitals, world, plan)

		plan:beginTier("A2")
		Profiler.run("Front_A2_Kills", AttackPlanner.kills, world, plan)

		plan:beginTier("A3")
		Profiler.run("Front_A34_Captures", AttackPlanner.captures, world, plan)
	end

	plan:beginTier("AR")
	Profiler.run("Front_Review", AttackPlanner.reviewOngoing, world, plan)

	plan:beginTier("T4")
	Profiler.run("Front_T4_Gaps", DefensePlanner.gaps, world, plan, analysis)

	if kind == "tick" then
		Profiler.run("Front_T4_Stage", DefensePlanner.stage, world, plan, analysis)
	end

	SupplyModel.logOurPrediction(world)

	Logger.info("Features.Front.FrontPlanner", "planned", {
		cycle = world.cycleLabel,
		kind = kind,
		summary = plan:summary(),
		unsafeTiles = #analysis.unsafe,
		gaps = #analysis.gaps,
	})

	return plan, analysis
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.FrontPlanner", FrontPlanner)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/Plan"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/Plan

	The front plan of one cycle: the orders each tier produced, the units they used, the tiles they
	claimed as destinations, the tiles that must stay empty (retreat lanes, spawn tile), the units to
	halt, and every decision with its chosen and rejected candidates. Tiers run in a fixed order and a
	later tier can only use what earlier tiers left free (DESIGN §5), which this object enforces.
]]

local Plan = {}
Plan.__index = Plan

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

-- Constants.
-- States in which one of our units accepts a new order (DESIGN §5.1: Defending units cannot be moved
-- out, Retreating units are the server's; orders to either were never observed accepted, OQ15).
local ORDERABLE_STATES = { Idle = true, Moving = true, Waiting = true, Attacking = true }
-- The front plan's tier order (FrontPlanner), most important first; relief attacks (A1) are planned
-- inside T1 and share its rank, walk-ins and captures (A3, A4) share one stage.
local TIER_RANK = { T0 = 1, T1 = 2, A1 = 2, T2 = 3, T3 = 4, A0 = 5, A2 = 6, A3 = 7, A4 = 7, AR = 8, T4 = 9 }
-- A tier missing from TIER_RANK ranks after every known tier.
local UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK = math.huge

Plan.TIER_RANK = TIER_RANK

---A new empty plan for a world view.
---@param world table
---@param blockedUnits table set of unit ids the plan must not touch (user overrides)
---@param commitments table unit id -> the order of ours that unit is carrying out (OrderExecutor)
---@return table
function Plan.new(world, blockedUnits, commitments)
	local self = setmetatable({}, Plan)

	self.world = world
	self.blockedUnits = blockedUnits
	self.commitments = commitments
	self.orders = {}
	self.byUnit = {}
	self.claims = {}
	self.vacating = {}
	self.locks = {}
	self.halts = {}
	self.decisionCount = 0
	self.tierCounts = {}
	self.currentTier = nil
	self.currentRank = UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK

	-- A unit walking to a destination under one of our orders already holds that destination.
	for unitId, commitment in pairs(commitments) do
		local unit = world.unitsById[unitId]
		local occupant = world.unitsByTile[commitment.destination]
		local attacksIt = occupant ~= nil and occupant.owner ~= world.me

		if unit ~= nil and not attacksIt then
			self.claims[commitment.destination] = unitId

			-- A unit walking away from its tile under our order is vacating it.
			if unit.tile ~= commitment.destination and (unit.state == "Moving" or unit.state == "Waiting") then
				self.vacating[unit.tile] = true
			end
		end
	end

	return self
end

---Mark the tier whose decisions follow (availability depends on it).
---@param tier string
function Plan:beginTier(tier)
	self.currentTier = tier
	self.currentRank = TIER_RANK[tier] or UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK
end

---Lock a tile: it must stay empty this cycle.
---@param tileName string
---@param reason string
function Plan:lock(tileName, reason)
	if self.locks[tileName] == nil then
		self.locks[tileName] = reason
	end
end

---Record that a unit stays where it is for a purpose (no order is sent).
---@param unit table
---@param purpose string
---@param tier string
---@param reason string
function Plan:hold(unit, purpose, tier, reason)
	self.byUnit[unit.id] = { unitId = unit.id, fromTile = unit.tile, purpose = purpose, tier = tier, reason = reason, hold = true }
	self.tierCounts[tier] = (self.tierCounts[tier] or 0) + 1
end

---Assign an order to a unit.
---@param unit table snapshot
---@param path table array of tile names, the unit's tile first
---@param purpose string
---@param tier string
---@param reason string
---@param facts table the numbers that decided it
---@return table order
function Plan:assign(unit, path, purpose, tier, reason, facts)
	if self.byUnit[unit.id] ~= nil then
		return error(string.format("Plan:assign: unit %s is already assigned (%s)", tostring(unit.id), self.byUnit[unit.id].purpose))
	end

	if path[1] ~= unit.tile then
		return error(string.format("Plan:assign: path of unit %s starts at %s, unit is on %s", tostring(unit.id), tostring(path[1]), unit.tile))
	end

	local destination = path[#path]
	local order = {
		unitId = unit.id,
		unitTroop = unit.troopName,
		fromTile = unit.tile,
		path = path,
		destination = destination,
		purpose = purpose,
		tier = tier,
		reason = reason,
		facts = facts,
		isAttack = self.world.unitsByTile[destination] ~= nil and self.world.unitsByTile[destination].owner ~= self.world.me,
	}

	table.insert(self.orders, order)
	self.byUnit[unit.id] = order
	self.tierCounts[tier] = (self.tierCounts[tier] or 0) + 1

	if not order.isAttack then
		self.claims[destination] = unit.id
		self.vacating[unit.tile] = true
	end

	return order
end

---Halt one of our units (Army.HaltTroop), for example an attack that no longer pays.
---@param unit table
---@param tier string
---@param reason string
---@param facts table
function Plan:halt(unit, tier, reason, facts)
	if self.byUnit[unit.id] ~= nil then
		return error(string.format("Plan:halt: unit %s is already assigned", tostring(unit.id)))
	end

	local entry = { unitId = unit.id, fromTile = unit.tile, purpose = "halt", tier = tier, reason = reason, facts = facts, halt = true }
	table.insert(self.halts, entry)
	self.byUnit[unit.id] = entry
	self.tierCounts[tier] = (self.tierCounts[tier] or 0) + 1
end

---Log one decision: what was chosen, what lost, and the fact that decided it.
---@param tier string
---@param decisionName string
---@param ranked table array of { id, score, facts } best first (may be empty when nothing qualified)
---@param decidingFact string
function Plan:decide(tier, decisionName, ranked, decidingFact)
	self.decisionCount += 1

	if #ranked == 0 then
		Logger.debug("Features.Front.Plan", "noCandidate", { tier = tier, decision = decisionName, reason = decidingFact })
		return
	end

	Formula.recordChoice(tier .. "." .. decisionName, ranked, decidingFact)
end

---Summary for logs and the menu.
---@return table
function Plan:summary()
	local byPurpose = {}

	for _, order in ipairs(self.orders) do
		byPurpose[order.purpose] = (byPurpose[order.purpose] or 0) + 1
	end

	local lockCount = 0
	for _ in pairs(self.locks) do
		lockCount += 1
	end

	return {
		orders = #self.orders,
		halts = #self.halts,
		byPurpose = byPurpose,
		byTier = self.tierCounts,
		locks = lockCount,
		decisions = self.decisionCount,
	}
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.Plan", Plan)

-- Hot-path methods, installed after instrumentation: the tiers call them for every unit and tile
-- they consider; the decisions that follow are logged.

---True when a unit is ours, unassigned, not blocked and in an orderable state.
---@param unit table snapshot
---@return boolean
function Plan:isAvailable(unit)
	if unit.owner ~= self.world.me or self.byUnit[unit.id] ~= nil or self.blockedUnits[unit.id] or ORDERABLE_STATES[unit.state] ~= true then
		return false
	end

	-- A member of a server army group is ordered by the army AI, which overrides manual orders
	-- (fourth live run: two orders sent before the release were never accepted). It becomes ours
	-- once ArmyControl has released it (armies.releaseInherited).
	if unit.army ~= nil then
		return false
	end

	-- A unit carrying out one of our orders keeps it unless a strictly more important tier needs it
	-- (re-ordering restarts the step in flight, ground-commands.md §3.2; the first live run lost
	-- most movement to orders re-planned every cycle). A running attack yields only to the capital.
	local commitment = self.commitments[unit.id]

	if commitment ~= nil then
		if unit.state == "Attacking" then
			return self.currentRank == TIER_RANK.T0
		end

		return self.currentRank < (TIER_RANK[commitment.tier] or UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK)
	end

	return true
end

---True when a unit is ours, untouched by this plan and not blocked (ignores commitments; used by the
---review of running attacks, which may halt any of them).
---@param unit table
---@return boolean
function Plan:isUnassigned(unit)
	return unit.owner == self.world.me and self.byUnit[unit.id] == nil and not self.blockedUnits[unit.id]
end

---Our units still available, in id order.
---@return table
function Plan:availableUnits()
	local units = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(self.world.ourUnits) do
		if self:isAvailable(unit) then
			table.insert(units, unit)
		end
	end

	return units
end

---True when nothing in the plan stops a unit from ending its move on this tile: no live unit that
---stays, no claim by another order, no lock.
---@param tileName string
---@return boolean
function Plan:isFreeDestination(tileName)
	if self.claims[tileName] ~= nil or self.locks[tileName] ~= nil then
		return false
	end

	local occupant = self.world.unitsByTile[tileName]

	return occupant == nil or self.vacating[tileName] == true
end

return Plan

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/RetreatManager"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/RetreatManager

	Keeps retreats alive (DESIGN §5.5, user rule U2):
	- a retreating unit dies if any unit enters its destination first (19/19 and 14/14 recorded), so
	  the destination of each of our retreating units is locked until it lands;
	- a defender predicted to break retreats onto one of its free exits (which one is unknown, OQ7),
	  so every exit of a predicted break is locked: no tier may fill it this cycle.
	After landing the unit is flagged and at 10 health; the rotation tier moves it out of reach.
]]

local RetreatManager = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Features.Front.FrontAnalyzer
local FrontAnalyzer = require("Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer")

---Lock retreat destinations and the exits of predicted breaks.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param analysis table
function RetreatManager.protect(world, plan, analysis)
	local landingLocks = 0
	local exitLocks = 0

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if unit.state == "Retreating" and unit.targetTile ~= nil then
			plan:lock(unit.targetTile, "lane:" .. unit.tile)
			landingLocks += 1
		end
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(analysis.unsafe) do
		local dies = FrontAnalyzer.diesWhenBeaten(world, plan, entry.unit)

		if not dies then
			for _, exitName in ipairs(FrontAnalyzer.exits(world, plan, entry.unit)) do
				plan:lock(exitName, "lane:" .. entry.tile)
				exitLocks += 1
			end
		end
	end

	Logger.debug("Features.Front.RetreatManager", "locked", { landingLocks = landingLocks, exitLocks = exitLocks })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.RetreatManager", RetreatManager)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/Tactics"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/Tactics

	Building blocks shared by the defence and attack tiers:
	- plan-aware path searches (tiles claimed, locked or still occupied are not passed through);
	- the earliest arrival of any available unit at a tile;
	- the smallest stack of adjacent units that beats a defender (stack ratio >= 1 and no tie, the
	  formula's own break-even, DESIGN §5.4; recorded: ratio >= 1 beat the defender 91-97 %);
	- whether an attacker is still safe on its tile after the fight it would win.
]]

local Tactics = {}

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatModel
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")

---@module Features.Movement.Pathing
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")

---@module Features.Front.WorldView
local WorldView = require("Features/Front/WorldView")

---True when our unit on originTile may attack a unit on targetTile (DiplomacyCommon.CanAttackFromCountryTile,
---T3): from a friendly country's tile, that country must be at war with the target's owner.
---@param world table
---@param originTile string
---@param targetOwner string
---@return boolean
function Tactics.mayAttackFrom(world, originTile, targetOwner)
	local originOwner = world.tileOwner[originTile]

	if originOwner == nil or originOwner == world.me then
		return true
	end

	return GameModules.get("DiplomacyCommon").CanAttackFromCountryTile(world.meInstance, originOwner, targetOwner)
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.Tactics", Tactics)

-- Hot path, installed after instrumentation: called for every candidate unit and target; callers
-- log the decisions.

---Plan-aware search for one of our units.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param unit table
---@param maxSeconds number?
---@return table search result (Pathing.search)
function Tactics.search(world, plan, unit, maxSeconds)
	local blocked = {}

	for tileName in pairs(plan.locks) do
		blocked[tileName] = true
	end

	for tileName, claimant in pairs(plan.claims) do
		if claimant ~= unit.id then
			blocked[tileName] = true
		end
	end

	return Pathing.search(world.paths, unit, { maxSeconds = maxSeconds, blocked = blocked })
end

---The available unit that reaches a goal first, with its path and time. The goal must be free for
---the plan (or hold the enemy the move attacks).
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param units table candidate snapshots
---@param goalTile string
---@param maxSeconds number?
---@return table? { unit, path, seconds }, table ranked candidates for the decision log
function Tactics.earliestArrival(world, plan, units, goalTile, maxSeconds)
	local ranked = {}
	local goalPosition = MapGraph.tiles[goalTile].position

	-- Branch and bound: candidates in order of their lower bound (straight distance / fastest speed);
	-- once a lower bound exceeds the best arrival found, no later candidate can win.
	local ordered = {}
	for _, unit in ipairs(units) do
		if unit.tile ~= goalTile then
			local straight = (MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].position - goalPosition).Magnitude
			table.insert(ordered, { unit = unit, lowerBound = straight / Pathing.fastestSpeed(world.paths, unit) })
		end
	end

	table.sort(ordered, function(first, second)
		if first.lowerBound ~= second.lowerBound then
			return first.lowerBound < second.lowerBound
		end

		return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
	end)

	local bestSeconds = maxSeconds or math.huge
	local skipped = 0

	for _, candidate in ipairs(ordered) do
		if candidate.lowerBound > bestSeconds then
			skipped += 1
		else
			local unit = candidate.unit
			local result = Tactics.search(world, plan, unit, bestSeconds)
			local seconds = result.times[goalTile]

			if seconds ~= nil then
				local path = Pathing.pathTo(result, goalTile)

				if path ~= nil then
					table.insert(ranked, { id = unit.id, score = seconds, facts = { tile = unit.tile, troop = unit.troopName, hops = #path - 1, lowerBound = candidate.lowerBound }, unit = unit, path = path })

					if seconds < bestSeconds then
						bestSeconds = seconds
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if skipped > 0 then
		table.insert(ranked, { id = "boundSkipped", score = math.huge, facts = { candidates = skipped, reason = "lower bound above the best arrival" } })
	end

	table.sort(ranked, function(first, second)
		if first.score ~= second.score then
			return first.score < second.score
		end

		return first.id < second.id
	end)

	local best = ranked[1]

	if best == nil or best.unit == nil then
		return nil, ranked
	end

	return { unit = best.unit, path = best.path, seconds = best.score }, ranked
end

---Our units and allied units already attacking a tile (they stay in any stack we add to).
---@param world table
---@param targetTile string
---@return table array of { unit, fromTile }
function Tactics.existingAttackers(world, targetTile)
	local attackers = {}
	local friends = world.friends

	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[targetTile].landNeighbours) do
		local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]

		if unit ~= nil and friends[unit.owner] and unit.state == "Attacking" and unit.targetTile == targetTile then
			table.insert(attackers, { unit = unit, fromTile = neighbourName, existing = true })
		end
	end

	return attackers
end

---Smallest stack of candidate units that beats the defender on targetTile (together with the units
---already attacking it). Candidates are added in order of what each would deliver alone.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param targetTile string
---@param candidates table array of our available units adjacent to targetTile
---@return table? { attackers = new entries, all = every entry, result = engagement }, table evaluation facts
function Tactics.winningStack(world, plan, targetTile, candidates)
	local defender = world.unitsByTile[targetTile]

	if defender == nil then
		return nil, { reason = "no defender" }
	end

	local existing = Tactics.existingAttackers(world, targetTile)
	local scored = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(candidates) do
		if unit.tile ~= targetTile and Tactics.mayAttackFromHot(world, unit.tile, defender.owner) then
			local alone = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, { { unit = unit, fromTile = unit.tile } }, defender, targetTile, "attacker")
			table.insert(scored, { unit = unit, alone = alone.ratio })
		end
	end

	table.sort(scored, function(first, second)
		if first.alone ~= second.alone then
			return first.alone > second.alone
		end

		return first.unit.id < second.unit.id
	end)

	local entries = table.clone(existing)
	local added = {}
	local lastResult = nil

	if #entries > 0 then
		lastResult = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, entries, defender, targetTile, "attacker")

		if lastResult.breaks and not lastResult.tie then
			return { attackers = added, all = entries, result = lastResult }, { existing = #existing, ratio = lastResult.ratio }
		end
	end

	for _, entry in ipairs(scored) do
		local newEntry = { unit = entry.unit, fromTile = entry.unit.tile }
		table.insert(entries, newEntry)
		table.insert(added, newEntry)

		lastResult = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, entries, defender, targetTile, "attacker")

		if lastResult.breaks and not lastResult.tie then
			return { attackers = added, all = entries, result = lastResult }, { existing = #existing, added = #added, ratio = lastResult.ratio }
		end
	end

	return nil, {
		existing = #existing,
		candidates = #scored,
		bestRatio = lastResult and lastResult.ratio or 0,
		tie = lastResult and lastResult.tie or false,
	}
end

---Hot twin of Tactics.mayAttackFrom.
---@param world table
---@param originTile string
---@param targetOwner string
---@return boolean
function Tactics.mayAttackFromHot(world, originTile, targetOwner)
	local originOwner = world.tileOwner[originTile]

	if originOwner == nil or originOwner == world.me then
		return true
	end

	return GameModules.get("DiplomacyCommon").CanAttackFromCountryTile(world.meInstance, originOwner, targetOwner)
end

---Whether an attacker is still safe on its tile after the fight: the enemy units next to it (other
---than the beaten defender) cannot beat it at its predicted health.
---@param world table
---@param attackerEntry table { unit, fromTile }
---@param predictedHealth number
---@param beatenTile string
---@return boolean, table facts
function Tactics.safeAfterFight(world, attackerEntry, predictedHealth, beatenTile)
	local weakened = CombatModel.placedOn(attackerEntry.unit, attackerEntry.fromTile)
	weakened.health = predictedHealth

	local attackers = {}
	for _, entry in ipairs(CombatModel.potentialAttackers(world, attackerEntry.fromTile, world.enemies)) do
		if entry.fromTile ~= beatenTile then
			table.insert(attackers, entry)
		end
	end

	if #attackers == 0 then
		return true, { tile = attackerEntry.fromTile, threats = 0 }
	end

	local threat = CombatModel.engage(world.combat, attackers, weakened, attackerEntry.fromTile, "defender")

	return not threat.breaks, { tile = attackerEntry.fromTile, threats = #attackers, ratio = threat.ratio, predictedHealth = predictedHealth }
end

---True when an enemy unit stands next to a tile.
---@param world table
---@param tileName string
---@return boolean
local function touchesEnemy(world, tileName)
	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		local occupant = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]

		if occupant ~= nil and world.enemies[occupant.owner] then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

---Fewest-hop walk from a unit's tile over tiles it may pass (own and allowed owners, empty, not
---claimed or locked) to the first tile that satisfies a predicate. Used for rest and lane moves,
---which are not races, so the hop-shortest path is enough (T1); races use earliestArrival. With
---avoidEnemyContact, the walk never passes a tile next to an enemy unit: a moving unit that is
---attacked stops and defends where it stands (first live run: a flagged tank at 22 health was sent
---to rest along its front line).
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param unit table
---@param accept function(tileName) -> boolean
---@param avoidEnemyContact boolean?
---@return table? path from the unit's tile to the accepted tile
function Tactics.nearestTile(world, plan, unit, accept, avoidEnemyContact)
	local allowed = world.paths.movement[unit.owner]

	if allowed == nil then
		allowed = Ownership.movementCountries(unit.owner)
		world.paths.movement[unit.owner] = allowed
	end

	local previous = { [unit.tile] = false }
	local queue = { unit.tile }
	local head = 1

	while head <= #queue do
		local current = queue[head]
		head += 1

		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
			if previous[neighbourName] == nil then
				local owner = world.tileOwner[neighbourName]
				local passable = owner ~= nil
					and allowed[owner]
					and world.unitsByTile[neighbourName] == nil
					and plan.claims[neighbourName] == nil
					and plan.locks[neighbourName] == nil
					and not (avoidEnemyContact and touchesEnemy(world, neighbourName))

				if passable then
					previous[neighbourName] = current

					if accept(neighbourName) then
						local reversed = { neighbourName }
						local step = current

						while step ~= false and step ~= nil do
							table.insert(reversed, step)
							step = previous[step]
						end

						local path = {}
						for index = #reversed, 1, -1 do
							table.insert(path, reversed[index])
						end

						return path
					end

					table.insert(queue, neighbourName)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

---Cut a path to the client's limit (fewer than 100 tiles, PlayerControls.lua:691); the unit walks the
---first part now and the rest in a later cycle.
---@param path table
---@return table
function Tactics.truncatePath(path)
	if #path <= Pathing.MAX_PATH_TILES then
		return path
	end

	local cut = {}
	for index = 1, Pathing.MAX_PATH_TILES do
		cut[index] = path[index]
	end

	return cut
end

---Our available units standing next to a tile.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param tileName string
---@return table
function Tactics.availableNeighbours(world, plan, tileName)
	local units = {}

	for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[tileName].landNeighbours) do
		local unit = world.unitsByTile[neighbourName]

		if unit ~= nil and plan:isAvailable(unit) then
			table.insert(units, unit)
		end
	end

	return units
end

---True when a tile's owner is at war with us (its units and empty tiles are fair targets).
---@param world table
---@param tileName string
---@return boolean
function Tactics.isEnemyTile(world, tileName)
	local owner = world.tileOwner[tileName]
	return owner ~= nil and world.enemies[owner] == true
end

---Friends of a unit's country (cached in the world view).
---@param world table
---@param countryName string
---@return table
function Tactics.friendsOf(world, countryName)
	return WorldView.friendsOf(world, countryName)
end

---True when a country exists and a server AI controls it.
---@param countryName string
---@return boolean
function Tactics.isAI(countryName)
	return Ownership.isAI(countryName)
end

return Tactics

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Front/WorldView"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Front/WorldView

	One planning cycle's view of the world. A cycle never yields, and replication applies between
	frames, so reading the live game once at the start of a cycle gives a consistent picture: every
	unit snapshot, every relation set and every capital come from the same frame. The view also
	carries the per-cycle caches of the combat, path and supply models, so no cached value outlives
	the cycle that read it.
]]

local WorldView = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

---@module Game.Units
local Units = require("Game/Units")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatModel
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")

---@module Features.Movement.Pathing
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")

-- Constants.
local DEAD_STATE = "Dead"

---Build the view for our country.
---@param ourCountry Instance
---@param cycleLabel string
---@return table
function WorldView.build(ourCountry, cycleLabel)
	local me = ourCountry.Name

	local world = {
		cycleLabel = cycleLabel,
		me = me,
		meInstance = ourCountry,
		serverTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
		tileOwner = Ownership.tileOwner,
		ourTiles = Ownership.countryTiles[me] or {},
		enemies = Ownership.enemiesOf(me),
		friends = Ownership.friendsOf(me),
		unitsByTile = {},
		unitsById = {},
		unitsOf = {},
		ourUnits = {},
		enemyUnits = {},
		enemyCapitals = {},
		supplyCache = {},
		friendCache = {},
		enemyCache = {},
		duplicateTiles = {},
	}

	for record in pairs(Units.records) do
		if not record.superseded and record.model.Parent ~= nil then
			local unit = Units.snapshotNow(record)

			if unit.state ~= DEAD_STATE then
				local existing = world.unitsByTile[unit.tile]

				-- Two live Models on one tile exist only for the ~1 ms of a replacement; keep the newer id.
				if existing ~= nil then
					table.insert(world.duplicateTiles, { tile = unit.tile, ids = { existing.id, unit.id } })
				end

				if existing == nil or existing.id < unit.id then
					world.unitsByTile[unit.tile] = unit
				end

				world.unitsById[unit.id] = unit

				local owned = world.unitsOf[unit.owner or "?"]
				if owned == nil then
					owned = {}
					world.unitsOf[unit.owner or "?"] = owned
				end
				table.insert(owned, unit)

				if unit.owner == me then
					table.insert(world.ourUnits, unit)
				elseif unit.owner ~= nil and world.enemies[unit.owner] then
					table.insert(world.enemyUnits, unit)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local sortById = function(first, second)
		return first.id < second.id
	end
	table.sort(world.ourUnits, sortById)
	table.sort(world.enemyUnits, sortById)

	local capitalTile, capitalReason = Ownership.capitalTile(me)
	world.capitalTile = capitalTile
	world.capitalReason = capitalReason

	for enemyName in pairs(world.enemies) do
		local enemyCapital, enemyReason = Ownership.capitalTile(enemyName)
		world.enemyCapitals[enemyName] = enemyCapital

		if enemyCapital == nil then
			Logger.warn("Features.Front.WorldView", "enemyCapitalUnknown", { enemy = enemyName, reason = enemyReason })
		end
	end

	world.combat = CombatModel.newContext(world)
	world.paths = Pathing.newContext(world)

	if #world.duplicateTiles > 0 then
		Logger.debug("Features.Front.WorldView", "replacementOverlap", { tiles = world.duplicateTiles })
	end

	Logger.debug("Features.Front.WorldView", "built", {
		cycle = cycleLabel,
		me = me,
		enemies = world.enemies,
		ourUnits = #world.ourUnits,
		enemyUnits = #world.enemyUnits,
		capitalTile = capitalTile,
		enemyCapitals = world.enemyCapitals,
	})

	return world
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Front.WorldView", WorldView)

-- Hot-path helpers (cached per view).

---Countries friendly to a country, cached in the view.
---@param world table
---@param countryName string
---@return table set
function WorldView.friendsOf(world, countryName)
	local friends = world.friendCache[countryName]

	if friends == nil then
		friends = Ownership.friendsOf(countryName)
		world.friendCache[countryName] = friends
	end

	return friends
end

---Countries at war with a country, cached in the view.
---@param world table
---@param countryName string
---@return table set
function WorldView.enemiesOf(world, countryName)
	local enemies = world.enemyCache[countryName]

	if enemies == nil then
		enemies = Ownership.enemiesOf(countryName)
		world.enemyCache[countryName] = enemies
	end

	return enemies
end

return WorldView

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Movement/Pathing"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Movement/Pathing

	Where a unit can go and how long it takes. Hop times come from the game's speed formula
	(TroopCommon.GetTroopSpeedAndDesc, exact on 5,286/5,286 recorded MoveArrows, ground-combat.md
	§3.9): distance / (speed x no-supply factor x Army class coefficient x army group coefficient x
	terrain of the tile left x river crossing). A path adds the server's dwell between hops.

	The search follows the client's movement rules (PlayerControls / PreviewPath.ComputePath,
	REFERENCE R10, R12): only land tiles whose owner is in the mover's allowed set, every intermediate
	tile empty, the goal may hold a unit (an attack), fewer than 100 tiles. Unlike the client, which
	minimises distance, the search minimises time: the server follows any valid sent path verbatim
	(ground-commands.md §3), so a faster valid path is a legal advantage.
]]

local Pathing = {}

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

-- Constants.
-- T3 TroopCommon.lua:124: speed x 0.3 while noSupply or noMoney.
local NO_SUPPLY_SPEED_COEF = 0.3
-- T3 TroopCommon.lua:200: river crossing speed factor 1 + (-0.05 + r x -0.45).
local RIVER_SPEED_BASE = -0.05
local RIVER_SPEED_PER_MODIFIER = -0.45
-- T2 recorded (map.md §3.12, REFERENCE R10): server dwell between consecutive hops of one path.
local RECORDED_STEP_OVERHEAD_SECONDS = 0.18
-- T3 PlayerControls.lua:691: the client refuses a path of 100 tiles or more.
local MAX_PATH_TILES = 99
-- T5: binary heap layout, children of i at 2i and 2i + 1.
local HEAP_ARITY = 2
-- T5: path[1] is the start tile; hops begin at the second entry.
local FIRST_HOP_INDEX = 2
-- T3: retreat hop = distance / (2 x speed) (295/295 recorded retreats, map.md §3.16).
local RETREAT_SPEED_MULTIPLIER = 2
-- T3 TroopCommon.lua:146, 160: an absent speed coefficient reads as 1.
local ABSENT_COEFFICIENT = 1

Pathing.MAX_PATH_TILES = MAX_PATH_TILES

Formula.define({
	id = "move.hopSeconds",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "TroopCommon.GetTroopSpeedAndDesc lines 114-214; MoveArrow endTime - startTime matched 5,286/5,286",
	expression = "distance / (speed * (0.3 if unsupplied) * armySpeedCoef * groupSpeedCoef * terrainSpeedCoef(tile left) * (1 - 0.05 - 0.45 r if r > 0))",
	unit = "s",
	inputs = {
		distance = "studs (Utils.GetDistanceBetweenTiles)",
		speed = "speed attribute",
		unsupplied = "boolean: noSupply or noMoney at hop start",
		armySpeedCoef = "Army.<Class>SpeedCoef",
		groupSpeedCoef = "army group <Class>SpeedCoef (1 outside a group)",
		terrainSpeedCoef = "terrainModifiers[terrain of the tile left].<Class>SpeedCoef (1 when absent)",
		riverModifier = "river modifier tile left -> tile entered",
	},
	compute = function(inputs)
		local speed = inputs.speed

		if inputs.unsupplied then
			speed *= NO_SUPPLY_SPEED_COEF
		end

		speed = speed * inputs.armySpeedCoef * inputs.groupSpeedCoef * inputs.terrainSpeedCoef

		if inputs.riverModifier > 0 then
			speed *= 1 + RIVER_SPEED_BASE + inputs.riverModifier * RIVER_SPEED_PER_MODIFIER
		end

		return inputs.distance / speed
	end,
	examples = {
		-- ground-combat.md §3.9 (A L6492): Infantry2 speed 1.1, general 1.1, forest 0.85, river r 0.2, 10.8430 studs -> 12.2588 s (arrow 12.2588).
		{ inputs = { distance = 10.8430, speed = 1.1, unsupplied = false, armySpeedCoef = 1, groupSpeedCoef = 1.1, terrainSpeedCoef = 0.85, riverModifier = 0.2 }, expected = 12.2588, tolerance = 1e-3 },
		-- The same hop while unsupplied takes 1 / 0.3 as long (17 recorded hops of flagged units).
		{ inputs = { distance = 10.8430, speed = 1.1, unsupplied = true, armySpeedCoef = 1, groupSpeedCoef = 1.1, terrainSpeedCoef = 0.85, riverModifier = 0.2 }, expected = 12.2588 / 0.3, tolerance = 1e-2 },
	},
})

Formula.define({
	id = "move.retreatSeconds",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "recorded retreat hops, 295/295 (map.md §3.16)",
	expression = "distance / (2 * speed)",
	unit = "s",
	inputs = { distance = "studs", speed = "speed attribute" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		return inputs.distance / (RETREAT_SPEED_MULTIPLIER * inputs.speed)
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = { distance = 8.72, speed = 1 }, expected = 4.36, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

---A new per-cycle context (country speed coefficients are cached in it).
---@param world table
---@return table
function Pathing.newContext(world)
	return { world = world, speedCoefs = {}, movement = {} }
end

---The server's dwell between two hops of one path: recorded until measured live.
---@return number, string
function Pathing.stepOverheadSeconds()
	return RECORDED_STEP_OVERHEAD_SECONDS, "recorded server dwell per intermediate tile (map.md §3.12)"
end

---Allowed owner set for a country's movement, cached per cycle.
---@param context table
---@param countryName string
---@return table
local function movementSet(context, countryName)
	local allowed = context.movement[countryName]

	if allowed == nil then
		allowed = Ownership.movementCountries(countryName)
		context.movement[countryName] = allowed
	end

	return allowed
end

---Army and group speed coefficient of a unit's class, cached per cycle.
---@param context table
---@param unit table
---@return number
local function classSpeedCoef(context, unit)
	local key = tostring(unit.owner) .. "|" .. unit.class .. "|" .. tostring(unit.army)
	local coef = context.speedCoefs[key]

	if coef ~= nil then
		return coef
	end

	local country = Ownership.country(unit.owner)

	if country == nil then
		return error(string.format("Pathing: unknown country '%s' of unit %s", tostring(unit.owner), tostring(unit.id)))
	end

	local army = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Army")
	local armyCoef = Resolver.optionalAttribute(army, unit.class .. "SpeedCoef", "number", "TroopCommon.lua:146 reads it with default 1") or ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	local groupCoef = ABSENT_COEFFICIENT

	if unit.army ~= nil then
		local armies = army:FindFirstChild("Armies")
		local group = armies and armies:FindFirstChild(unit.army)

		if group ~= nil then
			groupCoef = Resolver.optionalAttribute(group, unit.class .. "SpeedCoef", "number", "TroopCommon.lua:160 reads it with default 1") or ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
		end
	end

	coef = { army = armyCoef, group = groupCoef }
	context.speedCoefs[key] = coef

	return coef
end

---Terrain speed coefficient of the tile a unit leaves.
---@param unitClass string
---@param tileName string
---@return number
local function terrainSpeed(unitClass, tileName)
	local terrain = MapGraph.tiles[tileName].terrain

	if terrain == nil then
		return ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	end

	local modifiers = GameModules.get("ArmyCommon").terrainModifiers[terrain]
	local value = modifiers and modifiers[unitClass .. "SpeedCoef"]

	if value == nil then
		return ABSENT_COEFFICIENT
	end

	return value
end

---Seconds for one hop (hot path; Pathing.hopSeconds is its traced twin).
---@param context table
---@param unit table snapshot
---@param fromName string
---@param toName string
---@return number
local function hopSecondsFor(context, unit, fromName, toName)
	local coefs = classSpeedCoef(context, unit)

	return Formula.evaluate("move.hopSeconds", {
		distance = MapGraph.edgeLength(fromName, toName),
		speed = unit.speed,
		unsupplied = unit.noSupply or unit.noMoney,
		armySpeedCoef = coefs.army,
		groupSpeedCoef = coefs.group,
		terrainSpeedCoef = terrainSpeed(unit.class, fromName),
		riverModifier = MapGraph.edgeRiver(fromName, toName),
	})
end

---Seconds for one hop of a unit from one tile to a neighbour.
---@param context table
---@param unit table snapshot
---@param fromName string
---@param toName string
---@return number
function Pathing.hopSeconds(context, unit, fromName, toName)
	return hopSecondsFor(context, unit, fromName, toName)
end

Tracer.instrument("Features.Movement.Pathing", Pathing)

-- Hot path, installed after instrumentation: searches expand thousands of edges per cycle; the
-- callers log the chosen paths and times, and every hop time is a logged formula evaluation.

---Binary heap push (min on priority).
---@param heap table
---@param node string
---@param priority number
local function heapPush(heap, node, priority)
	local entry = { node = node, priority = priority }
	table.insert(heap, entry)

	local index = #heap
	while index > 1 do
		local parentIndex = index // HEAP_ARITY
		if heap[parentIndex].priority <= priority then
			break
		end
		heap[index] = heap[parentIndex]
		index = parentIndex
	end

	heap[index] = entry
end

---Binary heap pop.
---@param heap table
---@return table?
local function heapPop(heap)
	local count = #heap
	if count == 0 then
		return nil
	end

	local top = heap[1]
	local last = table.remove(heap)

	if count > 1 then
		local index = 1
		local size = #heap

		while true do
			local child = index * HEAP_ARITY
			if child > size then
				break
			end

			if child + 1 <= size and heap[child + 1].priority < heap[child].priority then
				child += 1
			end

			if heap[child].priority >= last.priority then
				break
			end

			heap[index] = heap[child]
			index = child
		end

		heap[index] = last
	end

	return top
end

---Time-optimal search from a unit's tile. Intermediate tiles must be land, owned by a country the
---unit may move through, empty in the world, and not blocked by the options; a tile holding a unit
---is reached as a goal (attack or waiting) but never expanded.
---@param context table Pathing context
---@param unit table snapshot (its tile is the start)
---@param options table? { maxSeconds, blocked = set, isOccupied = function(tileName) -> boolean }
---@return table { times = tile -> seconds, previous = tile -> tile, hops = tile -> count, occupiedGoals = set }
function Pathing.search(context, unit, options)
	local world = context.world
	local maxSeconds = options and options.maxSeconds or math.huge
	local blocked = options and options.blocked or nil
	local isOccupied = options and options.isOccupied or function(tileName)
		return world.unitsByTile[tileName] ~= nil
	end
	local allowed = movementSet(context, unit.owner)
	local overhead = RECORDED_STEP_OVERHEAD_SECONDS

	local times = { [unit.tile] = 0 }
	local previous = {}
	local hops = { [unit.tile] = 0 }
	local occupiedGoals = {}
	local settled = {}
	local heap = {}

	heapPush(heap, unit.tile, 0)

	while true do
		local entry = heapPop(heap)
		if entry == nil then
			break
		end

		local current = entry.node

		if not settled[current] and entry.priority <= times[current] then
			settled[current] = true

			-- Occupied tiles other than the start are goals only.
			local expandable = current == unit.tile or not occupiedGoals[current]

			if expandable and hops[current] + 1 <= MAX_PATH_TILES - 1 then
				local stepCost = 0
				if current ~= unit.tile then
					stepCost = overhead
				end

				for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
					local owner = world.tileOwner[neighbourName]

					if not settled[neighbourName] and owner ~= nil and allowed[owner] and not (blocked and blocked[neighbourName]) then
						local arrival = times[current] + stepCost + hopSecondsFor(context, unit, current, neighbourName)

						if arrival <= maxSeconds and arrival < (times[neighbourName] or math.huge) then
							times[neighbourName] = arrival
							previous[neighbourName] = current
							hops[neighbourName] = hops[current] + 1

							if isOccupied(neighbourName) then
								occupiedGoals[neighbourName] = true
							else
								occupiedGoals[neighbourName] = nil
							end

							heapPush(heap, neighbourName, arrival)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return { times = times, previous = previous, hops = hops, occupiedGoals = occupiedGoals, start = unit.tile }
end

---Path from a search result to a goal: array of tile names from the start to the goal, or nil.
---@param searchResult table
---@param goalName string
---@return table?
function Pathing.pathTo(searchResult, goalName)
	if searchResult.times[goalName] == nil then
		return nil
	end

	local reversed = { goalName }
	local current = goalName

	while current ~= searchResult.start do
		current = searchResult.previous[current]

		if current == nil then
			return nil
		end

		table.insert(reversed, current)
	end

	local path = {}
	for index = #reversed, 1, -1 do
		table.insert(path, reversed[index])
	end

	return path
end

---Seconds for a unit to walk an explicit path (hops plus dwell).
---@param context table
---@param unit table
---@param path table array of tile names, start first
---@return number
function Pathing.pathSeconds(context, unit, path)
	local total = 0

	for index = FIRST_HOP_INDEX, #path do
		total += hopSecondsFor(context, unit, path[index - 1], path[index])

		if index > FIRST_HOP_INDEX then
			total += RECORDED_STEP_OVERHEAD_SECONDS
		end
	end

	return total
end

---Upper bound on a unit's movement speed on any tile: speed x class and group coefficients x the
---best terrain speed coefficient of its class (rivers and missing supply only slow it). Divided into
---a straight-line distance it gives a lower bound on any path time, used to skip searches that
---cannot beat an arrival already found.
---@param context table
---@param unit table
---@return number studs per second
function Pathing.fastestSpeed(context, unit)
	local coefs = classSpeedCoef(context, unit)
	local best = ABSENT_COEFFICIENT

	for _, modifiers in pairs(GameModules.get("ArmyCommon").terrainModifiers) do
		local value = modifiers[unit.class .. "SpeedCoef"]

		if value ~= nil and value > best then
			best = value
		end
	end

	return unit.speed * coefs.army * coefs.group * best
end

---Distance-ordered multi-source search over passable tiles (edge length in studs, the client's own
---path metric): for every reached tile, its distance to the nearest source and the next tile toward
---it. Used to stage reserves toward the front without one search per unit.
---@param sources table array of tile names
---@param passable function(tileName) -> boolean
---@return table { distance = tile -> studs, towardSource = tile -> next tile }
function Pathing.distanceField(sources, passable)
	local distance = {}
	local toward = {}
	local settled = {}
	local heap = {}

	for _, source in ipairs(sources) do
		distance[source] = 0
		heapPush(heap, source, 0)
	end

	while true do
		local entry = heapPop(heap)
		if entry == nil then
			break
		end

		local current = entry.node

		if not settled[current] and entry.priority <= distance[current] then
			settled[current] = true

			for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
				if not settled[neighbourName] and passable(neighbourName) then
					local candidate = distance[current] + MapGraph.edgeLength(current, neighbourName)

					if candidate < (distance[neighbourName] or math.huge) then
						distance[neighbourName] = candidate
						toward[neighbourName] = current
						heapPush(heap, neighbourName, candidate)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return { distance = distance, towardSource = toward }
end

---Seconds of a retreat hop.
---@param unit table
---@param fromName string
---@param toName string
---@return number
function Pathing.retreatSeconds(unit, fromName, toName)
	return Formula.evaluate("move.retreatSeconds", {
		distance = MapGraph.edgeLength(fromName, toName),
		speed = unit.speed,
	})
end

return Pathing

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Navy/NavyPlanner"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Navy/NavyPlanner

	Navy, lower priority than land (players.md §10.13: few naval actions by anyone; the server AI's
	Italy made 8 landings on undefended coasts). v1 does two things (DESIGN §8):
	- coast watch: every enemy fleet carrying divisions is logged with its tile and the coastal tiles
	  of ours next to its sea tile, so the front's gap tier sees landings as they happen (a landed
	  enemy unit is an enemy unit next to our land, which triggers an instant combat cycle);
	- docking: an idle damaged fleet of ours sails to the nearest own port city (ships repair only
	  while docked, +8 % max health per day, and cost no upkeep there, navy.md §3).
	Invasions stay off unless navy.invasions is on (not built in v1: the setting only reports).
]]

local NavyPlanner = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Fleets
local Fleets = require("Game/Fleets")

---@module Game.CountryState
local CountryState = require("Game/CountryState")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

-- Constants.
local TIER = "N"
local IDLE_STATE = "Idle"
-- T3 PlayerControls.lua:577 / PreviewPath: a naval path of 100 tiles or more is refused.
local MAX_PATH_TILES = 99

---Distance-ordered search over sea tiles from a fleet's tile to the first sea tile next to one of
---the goal land tiles (the client's naval metric: edge length). Returns the path ending on the goal.
---@param startTile string
---@param goalLandTiles table set of land tile names
---@return table?
local function navalPathToAny(startTile, goalLandTiles)
	local distance = { [startTile] = 0 }
	local previous = {}
	local settled = {}
	local frontier = { startTile }

	while #frontier > 0 do
		local bestIndex = 1
		for index, tileName in ipairs(frontier) do
			if distance[tileName] < distance[frontier[bestIndex]] then
				bestIndex = index
			end
		end

		local current = table.remove(frontier, bestIndex)

		if not settled[current] then
			settled[current] = true

			for _, landName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
				if goalLandTiles[landName] then
					local reversed = { landName, current }
					local step = previous[current]

					while step ~= nil do
						table.insert(reversed, step)
						step = previous[step]
					end

					local path = {}
					for index = #reversed, 1, -1 do
						table.insert(path, reversed[index])
					end

					if #path > MAX_PATH_TILES then
						return nil
					end

					return path
				end
			end

			for _, seaName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].seaNeighbours) do
				if not settled[seaName] then
					local candidate = distance[current] + MapGraph.edgeLength(current, seaName)

					if candidate < (distance[seaName] or math.huge) then
						distance[seaName] = candidate
						previous[seaName] = current
						table.insert(frontier, seaName)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

---Run the navy for one tick.
---@param world table
function NavyPlanner.run(world)
	local fleets = Fleets.scan()
	local ourPorts = {}

	for _, city in ipairs(CountryState.cities(world.meInstance)) do
		if city.isPort and world.tileOwner[city.tileName] == world.me then
			ourPorts[city.tileName] = city.name
		end
	end

	local threats = {}
	local docked = {}

	for _, fleet in ipairs(fleets) do
		if fleet.country ~= nil and world.enemies[fleet.country] and fleet.loadedDivisions > 0 then
			local coast = {}
			for _, landName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[fleet.tile].landNeighbours) do
				if world.tileOwner[landName] == world.me then
					table.insert(coast, landName)
				end
			end

			table.insert(threats, { country = fleet.country, tile = fleet.tile, divisions = fleet.loadedDivisions, ourCoastNearby = coast, state = fleet.state })
		end

		if fleet.country == world.me and fleet.state == IDLE_STATE and fleet.damaged then
			local path = navalPathToAny(fleet.tile, ourPorts)

			if path ~= nil then
				local instances = {}
				for _, tileName in ipairs(path) do
					table.insert(instances, MapGraph.tiles[tileName].instance)
				end

				ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
					remote = "MoveFleet",
					args = { instances },
					key = "dock:" .. fleet.tile .. ":" .. path[#path],
					reason = string.format("damaged idle fleet docks at %s to repair", ourPorts[path[#path]]),
					tier = TIER,
				})

				table.insert(docked, { from = fleet.tile, port = ourPorts[path[#path]] })
			end
		end
	end

	if #threats > 0 then
		Logger.warn("Features.Navy.NavyPlanner", "enemyTransports", { fleets = threats })
	end

	Logger.debug("Features.Navy.NavyPlanner", "run", { fleets = #fleets, enemyLoadedFleets = #threats, docking = docked })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Navy.NavyPlanner", NavyPlanner)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher

	The single door for every non-movement remote (economy, research, focus, army groups, navy,
	air). Same rules as the order executor: the act-or-stop gate decides between sending and
	dry-run; an action already in flight is not sent twice; every call and every reply is logged.
	RemoteFunctions run on their own thread (InvokeServer yields) owned by the dispatcher's maid;
	RemoteEvent replies that come back on the same event are logged by listeners connected at
	attach.

	Robux hazard (DESIGN §1.6, §7.1): a refused PurchaseFactory / PurchaseDefense makes the game's own
	UI open a purchase prompt. The planners pre-check price, caps and money before dispatching; a
	refusal reply is therefore logged as an ERROR (the pre-check was wrong).
]]

local ActionDispatcher = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.Remotes
local Remotes = require("Game/Remotes")

---@module Features.Core.Guard
local Guard = require("Features/Core/Guard")

---@module Game.Tick
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")

-- Constants.
-- T5: pcall / table.pack return the success flag at index 1 and results from index 2.
local RESULTS_START_INDEX = 2
-- Remotes whose failure reply opens a Robux purchase prompt in the game UI (economy.md §4).
local PROMPTING_REPLIES = { PurchaseFactory = true, PurchaseDefense = true }
local REPLY_REMOTES = { "PurchaseFactory", "PurchaseDefense", "AddTroopFromTileToArmy" }

-- State.
ActionDispatcher.maid = nil
ActionDispatcher.inFlight = {}
ActionDispatcher.lastDryRun = {}
ActionDispatcher.lastByRemote = {}
-- RemoteEvents give no return value; their effect reaches the attributes within one day tick, so
-- the same event is not sent again until one measured tick period has passed (first live run:
-- two tick cycles 0.5 s apart after a replication stall sent every production change twice).
ActionDispatcher.eventSentClock = {}
ActionDispatcher.stats = { dispatched = 0, dryRun = 0, dryRunRepeats = 0, duplicates = 0, results = 0, failures = 0, refusals = 0 }
ActionDispatcher.history = {}

---Describe an argument list for the log (instances by path).
---@param args table
---@return table
local function describeArgs(args)
	local described = {}

	for index, value in ipairs(args) do
		if typeof(value) == "Instance" then
			described[index] = value:GetFullName()
		else
			described[index] = value
		end
	end

	return described
end

---Send one action (or log it in dry-run).
---@param action table { remote, args = array, key = string, reason, tier?, onResult = function(ok, results)? }
---@return string "sent" | "dryRun" | "duplicate"
function ActionDispatcher.dispatch(action)
	local verdict = Guard.check()
	local described = describeArgs(action.args)

	if not verdict.canSend then
		ActionDispatcher.stats.dryRun += 1

		if ActionDispatcher.lastDryRun[action.key] ~= nil then
			ActionDispatcher.stats.dryRunRepeats += 1
		else
			ActionDispatcher.lastDryRun[action.key] = true
			Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "dryRunAction", {
				remote = action.remote,
				args = described,
				reason = action.reason,
				tier = action.tier,
				gate = verdict.reason,
			})
		end

		return "dryRun"
	end

	if ActionDispatcher.inFlight[action.key] then
		ActionDispatcher.stats.duplicates += 1
		return "duplicate"
	end

	local entry = Remotes.entry(action.remote)

	if entry.class == "RemoteEvent" then
		local sentClock = ActionDispatcher.eventSentClock[action.key]
		local period = Tick.periodSeconds()

		if sentClock ~= nil and period ~= nil and os.clock() - sentClock < period then
			ActionDispatcher.stats.duplicates += 1
			return "duplicate"
		end

		ActionDispatcher.eventSentClock[action.key] = os.clock()
	end

	ActionDispatcher.stats.dispatched += 1
	ActionDispatcher.lastByRemote[action.remote] = { key = action.key, args = described, reason = action.reason, sentServerTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() }

	Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "actionSent", {
		remote = action.remote,
		args = described,
		reason = action.reason,
		tier = action.tier,
	})

	if entry.class == "RemoteEvent" then
		Remotes.send(action.remote, table.unpack(action.args))
		return "sent"
	end

	ActionDispatcher.inFlight[action.key] = true

	local thread = task.spawn(function()
		local packed = table.pack(pcall(Remotes.send, action.remote, table.unpack(action.args)))
		ActionDispatcher.inFlight[action.key] = nil

		if not packed[1] then
			ActionDispatcher.stats.failures += 1
			Logger.error("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "invokeFailed", {
				remote = action.remote,
				args = described,
				message = tostring(packed[RESULTS_START_INDEX]),
			})
			return
		end

		local results = { table.unpack(packed, RESULTS_START_INDEX, packed.n) }
		ActionDispatcher.stats.results += 1

		table.insert(ActionDispatcher.history, { remote = action.remote, key = action.key, ok = results[1], results = results, serverTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() })

		Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "actionResult", {
			remote = action.remote,
			args = described,
			results = results,
			reason = action.reason,
		})

		if action.onResult ~= nil then
			action.onResult(results[1], results)
		end
	end)

	if ActionDispatcher.maid ~= nil then
		ActionDispatcher.maid:give(thread)
	end

	return "sent"
end

---Connect the reply listeners of RemoteEvents that answer on the same event.
---@param maid table
function ActionDispatcher.attach(maid)
	ActionDispatcher.maid = maid

	for _, remoteName in ipairs(REPLY_REMOTES) do
		maid:give(Remotes.replySignal(remoteName):Connect(function(...)
			local reply = table.pack(...)
			local ok = reply[1]
			local last = ActionDispatcher.lastByRemote[remoteName]

			ActionDispatcher.stats.results += 1

			local payload = {
				remote = remoteName,
				reply = describeArgs({ table.unpack(reply, 1, reply.n) }),
				lastAction = last,
			}

			-- The event answers every purchase this client makes, including the user's own clicks in
			-- the game's menus (fourth live run: 4 refusals logged as ours were the user's). A reply is
			-- ours only when we sent that remote within one measured tick period.
