			local period = Tick.periodSeconds()
			local ours = last ~= nil and period ~= nil and workspace:GetServerTimeNow() - last.sentServerTime <= period

			-- CityInfo treats any first value other than false as success (economy.md §4).
			if not ours then
				Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "replyToUserAction", payload)
			elseif PROMPTING_REPLIES[remoteName] and ok == false then
				ActionDispatcher.stats.refusals += 1
				Logger.error("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "purchaseRefused", payload)
			else
				Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "reply", payload)
			end
		end))
	end

	Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "attached", { replyRemotes = REPLY_REMOTES })
end

---Forget dry-run memory and in-flight markers (country change).
---@param reason string
function ActionDispatcher.reset(reason)
	table.clear(ActionDispatcher.inFlight)
	table.clear(ActionDispatcher.lastDryRun)
	table.clear(ActionDispatcher.eventSentClock)
	Logger.info("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", "reset", { reason = reason })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher", ActionDispatcher)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Orders/ArmyControl"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Orders/ArmyControl

	Army groups and the spawn anchor (DESIGN §5.6).
	- Release: the server's army-group AI keeps ordering every troop that carries an `army`
	  attribute (Spain inherited armies made 103 orders after its player's last call, players.md §1)
	  and may override our orders (OQ7). With armies.releaseInherited on, each such troop is toggled
	  out with AddTroopFromTileToArmy(tile, armyName) and every army's replenishTroops is switched off
	  so new spawns stay ours (ground-commands.md §3.8).
	- Spawn anchor: new units appear on Army.priorityCity when its tile is free, else the nearest free
	  own city, else the nearest free own tile (133/133 spawns). The anchor is the free, supplied city
	  of ours closest (studs along our own land) to the tiles that need support; it is changed only
	  when a different city wins.
]]

local ArmyControl = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.CountryState
local CountryState = require("Game/CountryState")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

---@module Features.Movement.Pathing
local Pathing = require("Features/Movement/Pathing")

---@module Features.Supply.SupplyModel
local SupplyModel = require("Features/Supply/SupplyModel")

---@module Features.Front.FrontAnalyzer
local FrontAnalyzer = require("Features/Front/FrontAnalyzer")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

-- Constants.
local TIER = "C"

---Release inherited army groups.
---@param world table
local function releaseArmies(world)
	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if unit.army ~= nil and unit.state ~= "Retreating" and unit.state ~= "Dead" then
			ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
				remote = "AddTroopFromTileToArmy",
				args = { MapGraph.tiles[unit.tile].instance, unit.army },
				key = "release:" .. tostring(unit.id) .. ":" .. unit.army,
				reason = "per-tile control needs troops outside server army groups",
				tier = TIER,
			})
		end
	end

	for _, army in ipairs(CountryState.armies(world.meInstance)) do
		if army.replenishTroops == true then
			ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
				remote = "ToggleArmyReplenish",
				args = { army.folder },
				key = "replenishOff:" .. army.name,
				reason = "new spawns must not join a server army group",
				tier = TIER,
			})
		end
	end
end

---Choose and set the spawn anchor.
---@param world table
---@param analysis table?
---@return table facts
local function setPriorityCity(world, analysis)
	local army = Resolver.requireChild(world.meInstance, "Army")
	local current = army:GetAttribute("priorityCity")
	local sources = {}

	if analysis ~= nil then
		for tileName in pairs(analysis.contact) do
			table.insert(sources, tileName)
		end

		for _, gap in ipairs(analysis.gaps) do
			table.insert(sources, gap.tile)
		end
	end

	table.sort(sources)

	if #sources == 0 then
		return { changed = false, reason = "no front tiles" }
	end

	local field = Pathing.distanceField(sources, function(tileName)
		return world.tileOwner[tileName] == world.me
	end)

	local best = nil
	for _, city in ipairs(CountryState.cities(world.meInstance)) do
		local tileName = city.tileName
		local distance = field.distance[tileName]

		local usable = distance ~= nil
			and world.tileOwner[tileName] == world.me
			and world.unitsByTile[tileName] == nil
			and #FrontAnalyzer.adjacentEnemyUnits(world, tileName) == 0
			and SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, tileName)

		if usable and (best == nil or distance < best.distance or (distance == best.distance and city.name < best.name)) then
			best = { name = city.name, tile = tileName, distance = distance }
		end
	end

	if best == nil then
		return { changed = false, reason = "no free, safe, supplied city" }
	end

	if best.name == current then
		return { changed = false, city = best.name }
	end

	ActionDispatcher.dispatch({
		remote = "SetPriorityCity",
		args = { best.name },
		key = "priority:" .. best.name,
		reason = string.format("closest free safe city to the front (%.1f studs)", best.distance),
		tier = TIER,
	})

	return { changed = true, from = current, to = best.name, distance = best.distance }
end

---Run army control for one tick.
---@param world table
---@param analysis table?
function ArmyControl.run(world, analysis)
	local facts = {}

	if Settings.get("armies.releaseInherited") then
		releaseArmies(world)
		facts.released = true
	end

	facts.priorityCity = setPriorityCity(world, analysis)

	Logger.debug("Features.Orders.ArmyControl", "run", facts)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Orders.ArmyControl", ArmyControl)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Orders/OrderExecutor"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Orders/OrderExecutor

	The single door for troop orders (DESIGN §6). It turns a front plan into MoveTroop / HaltTroop
	calls and proves each one:
	- minimal change: a unit already heading to the planned destination (or already attacking the
	  planned target) gets no new order, because a re-order restarts or drops the step in flight;
	- sequencing: units that vacate a tile another order needs are sent first (vacate before fill);
	  a unit blocked by our own moving unit waits and keeps its order (ground-commands.md §3.2);
	- validation at send time: the unit is still on the planned tile in an orderable state, every
	  intermediate tile is still empty;
	- dry-run: without ai.sendOrders the exact order is logged and nothing is sent;
	- acceptance: ~0.21-0.23 s after the call the unit writes targetTile == path[2] or
	  currentPath == JSON(path[2..]) / JSON(path[3..]) (REFERENCE R14); no acceptance within one
	  measured tick period is an ERROR with the unit's state, and the planner re-plans the unit;
	- user overrides: a unit of ours outside any army that starts a path we did not send is under
	  the user's control until it is Idle again, and the plan leaves it alone.
]]

local OrderExecutor = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Units
local Units = require("Game/Units")

---@module Game.Remotes
local Remotes = require("Game/Remotes")

---@module Game.Tick
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")

---@module Features.Front.Plan
local Plan = require("Features/Front/Plan")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatFormulas
local CombatFormulas = require("Features/Combat/CombatFormulas")

-- Constants.
-- A tier missing from Plan.TIER_RANK sorts after every known tier.
local UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK = math.huge
-- T0: currentPath of a unit with no tiles left.
local EMPTY_PATH_JSON = "[]"
local PATH_AFTER_TARGET_START = 3
local TARGET_INDEX = 2

-- Services.
local httpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

-- State.
OrderExecutor.pending = {}
OrderExecutor.committed = {}
OrderExecutor.userControlled = {}
OrderExecutor.stats = { planned = 0, unchanged = 0, sent = 0, dryRun = 0, rejectedAtSend = 0, accepted = 0, notAccepted = 0, halts = 0, userOverrides = 0 }
OrderExecutor.acceptanceLatencies = {}
-- Dry-run orders repeat every cycle while nothing moves; an unchanged one is counted, not re-logged.
OrderExecutor.lastDryRun = {}
-- The last order sent to each unit: an identical order is not sent again within one measured tick
-- period (second live run: an attack order that ended at once was re-sent 3 times in 1.2 s).
OrderExecutor.lastSent = {}
-- Units that just left an army group: the army AI's order in flight keeps running after the
-- release, and its later hops are not the user's (third live run: 9 released Romanian units were
-- taken for user orders and left alone for up to 80 s, including the capital's defender).
OrderExecutor.leftArmy = {}
OrderExecutor.ourCountryName = nil

---JSON of a slice of a path of tile names, as the server writes currentPath.
---@param path table
---@param fromIndex number
---@return string
local function pathJson(path, fromIndex)
	local slice = {}

	for index = fromIndex, #path do
		table.insert(slice, path[index])
	end

	return httpService:JSONEncode(slice)
end

---The final destination of a unit's current route, from its live attributes.
---@param unit table snapshot
---@return string?
local function currentDestination(unit)
	if unit.currentPath ~= nil and unit.currentPath ~= "[]" then
		local decodeOk, decoded = pcall(httpService.JSONDecode, httpService, unit.currentPath)

		if not decodeOk then
			return error(string.format("OrderExecutor: currentPath of unit %s is not JSON: %s", tostring(unit.id), tostring(decoded)))
		end

		if type(decoded) == "table" and #decoded > 0 then
			return tostring(decoded[#decoded])
		end
	end

	return unit.targetTile
end

---Orders of ours in progress, by unit id (read by the plan; the executor owns the table).
---@return table
function OrderExecutor.commitments()
	return OrderExecutor.committed
end

---True when the same order went to the same unit less than one measured tick period ago.
---@param order table
---@return boolean
function OrderExecutor.repeatedTooSoon(order)
	local last = OrderExecutor.lastSent[order.unitId]
	local period = Tick.periodSeconds()

	return last ~= nil
		and period ~= nil
		and last.signature == table.concat(order.path, ",")
		and os.clock() - last.clock < period
end

---Drop the commitments whose order is over, judged on the cycle's snapshot: the unit is gone, or it
---is Idle with no target and no tiles left and its order is no longer pending. Single attribute
---events are not enough: an upgrade replaces the Model and its attributes arrive one by one
---(fourth live run: an upgraded unit's order was taken for a user order).
---@param world table
function OrderExecutor.pruneCommitments(world)
	for unitId, commitment in pairs(OrderExecutor.committed) do
		local unit = world.unitsById[unitId]
		local finished = unit == nil
			or unit.state == "Retreating"
			or (unit.state == "Idle" and unit.targetTile == nil and (unit.currentPath == nil or unit.currentPath == EMPTY_PATH_JSON) and OrderExecutor.pending[unitId] == nil)

		if finished then
			OrderExecutor.committed[unitId] = nil
			Logger.trace("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "commitmentEnded", { unit = unitId, purpose = commitment.purpose, destination = commitment.destination })
		end
	end
end

---Units the plan must not touch (user-controlled).
---@return table set of unit ids
function OrderExecutor.blockedUnits()
	local blocked = {}

	for unitId in pairs(OrderExecutor.userControlled) do
		blocked[unitId] = true
	end

	return blocked
end

---Validate one order against the live world right before sending.
---@param order table
---@return boolean, string?
local function validateAtSend(order)
	local record = nil

	for candidate in pairs(Units.records) do
		if candidate.id == order.unitId and not candidate.superseded then
			record = candidate
		end
	end

	if record == nil then
		return false, "unit no longer exists"
	end

	if record.tile ~= order.fromTile then
		return false, string.format("unit moved to %s since planning", record.tile)
	end

	local state = record.model:GetAttribute("state")
	if state == "Defending" or state == "Retreating" or state == "Dead" then
		return false, "unit is " .. tostring(state)
	end

	for index = TARGET_INDEX, #order.path - 1 do
		if Units.liveUnitOn(order.path[index]) ~= nil then
			return false, string.format("intermediate tile %s is occupied", order.path[index])
		end
	end

	return true, nil
end

---Send (or log in dry-run) every order and halt of a plan.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param canSend boolean
---@return table summary
function OrderExecutor.execute(world, plan, canSend)
	OrderExecutor.ourCountryName = world.me

	local vacatedBy = {}
	for _, order in ipairs(plan.orders) do
		vacatedBy[order.fromTile] = order
	end

	local needed = {}
	for _, order in ipairs(plan.orders) do
		for index = TARGET_INDEX, #order.path do
			local vacating = vacatedBy[order.path[index]]
			if vacating ~= nil and vacating ~= order then
				needed[vacating] = true
			end
		end
	end

	local ordered = table.clone(plan.orders)
	table.sort(ordered, function(first, second)
		local firstNeeded = needed[first] == true
		local secondNeeded = needed[second] == true

		if firstNeeded ~= secondNeeded then
			return firstNeeded
		end

		local firstTier = Plan.TIER_RANK[first.tier] or UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK
		local secondTier = Plan.TIER_RANK[second.tier] or UNKNOWN_TIER_RANK

		if firstTier ~= secondTier then
			return firstTier < secondTier
		end

		return first.unitId < second.unitId
	end)

	local summary = { sent = 0, unchanged = 0, dryRun = 0, rejected = 0, halts = 0 }

	for _, order in ipairs(ordered) do
		OrderExecutor.stats.planned += 1

		local unit = world.unitsById[order.unitId]
		local destinationNow = unit and currentDestination(unit) or nil
		local attackingTarget = unit ~= nil and unit.state == "Attacking" and unit.targetTile == order.destination
		-- An order sent in an earlier cycle is not yet visible in the unit's attributes for about
		-- 0.2 s; the commitment says where it is going (first live run re-sent orders every 0.2 s).
		local commitment = OrderExecutor.committed[order.unitId]
		local alreadyOrdered = commitment ~= nil and commitment.destination == order.destination

		if destinationNow == order.destination or attackingTarget or alreadyOrdered then
			OrderExecutor.stats.unchanged += 1
			summary.unchanged += 1
		else
			local valid, why = validateAtSend(order)

			if not valid then
				OrderExecutor.stats.rejectedAtSend += 1
				summary.rejected += 1
				Logger.debug("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "rejectedAtSend", { unit = order.unitId, purpose = order.purpose, path = order.path, reason = why })
			elseif canSend and OrderExecutor.repeatedTooSoon(order) then
				summary.repeatSuppressed = (summary.repeatSuppressed or 0) + 1
				Logger.debug("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "repeatSuppressed", { unit = order.unitId, path = order.path, purpose = order.purpose })
			elseif not canSend then
				OrderExecutor.stats.dryRun += 1
				summary.dryRun += 1

				local signature = order.purpose .. "|" .. table.concat(order.path, ",")
				if OrderExecutor.lastDryRun[order.unitId] == signature then
					summary.dryRunRepeats = (summary.dryRunRepeats or 0) + 1
				else
					OrderExecutor.lastDryRun[order.unitId] = signature
					Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "dryRunOrder", {
						unit = order.unitId,
						troop = order.unitTroop,
						path = order.path,
						purpose = order.purpose,
						tier = order.tier,
						reason = order.reason,
						facts = order.facts,
					})
				end
			else
				local instances = {}
				for _, tileName in ipairs(order.path) do
					table.insert(instances, MapGraph.tiles[tileName].instance)
				end

				Remotes.send("MoveTroop", instances)

				-- Our order replaces whatever the unit was doing, including a user or army order.
				OrderExecutor.userControlled[order.unitId] = nil
				OrderExecutor.leftArmy[order.unitId] = nil

				local pendingEntry = {
					order = order,
					sentClock = os.clock(),
					expectedTarget = order.path[TARGET_INDEX],
					expectedPathFromTarget = pathJson(order.path, TARGET_INDEX),
					expectedPathAfterTarget = nil,
				}

				if #order.path >= PATH_AFTER_TARGET_START then
					pendingEntry.expectedPathAfterTarget = pathJson(order.path, PATH_AFTER_TARGET_START)
				end

				OrderExecutor.pending[order.unitId] = pendingEntry
				OrderExecutor.committed[order.unitId] = {
					path = order.path,
					purpose = order.purpose,
					tier = order.tier,
					destination = order.destination,
					facts = order.facts,
					sentClock = os.clock(),
				}
				OrderExecutor.stats.sent += 1
				summary.sent += 1
				OrderExecutor.lastSent[order.unitId] = { signature = table.concat(order.path, ","), clock = os.clock() }

				Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "sent", {
					unit = order.unitId,
					troop = order.unitTroop,
					path = order.path,
					purpose = order.purpose,
					tier = order.tier,
					reason = order.reason,
					facts = order.facts,
				})
			end
		end
	end

	for _, halt in ipairs(plan.halts) do
		summary.halts += 1
		OrderExecutor.stats.halts += 1

		if canSend then
			Remotes.send("HaltTroop", MapGraph.tiles[halt.fromTile].instance)
			OrderExecutor.committed[halt.unitId] = nil
			Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "haltSent", { unit = halt.unitId, tile = halt.fromTile, reason = halt.reason, facts = halt.facts })
		else
			Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "dryRunHalt", { unit = halt.unitId, tile = halt.fromTile, reason = halt.reason, facts = halt.facts })
		end
	end

	Logger.debug("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "executed", { cycle = world.cycleLabel, summary = summary, canSend = canSend })

	return summary
end

---Check a unit attribute change against pending acceptances and user overrides. Hot path.
---@param record table Units record
---@param attributeName string
---@param value any
local function onUnitChanged(record, attributeName, value)
	if attributeName ~= "targetTile" and attributeName ~= "currentPath" and attributeName ~= "state" and attributeName ~= "army" then
		return
	end

	local pendingEntry = OrderExecutor.pending[record.id]

	if pendingEntry ~= nil then
		local accepted = (attributeName == "targetTile" and value == pendingEntry.expectedTarget)
			or (attributeName == "currentPath" and (value == pendingEntry.expectedPathFromTarget or (pendingEntry.expectedPathAfterTarget ~= nil and value == pendingEntry.expectedPathAfterTarget)))

		if accepted then
			local latency = os.clock() - pendingEntry.sentClock
			OrderExecutor.pending[record.id] = nil
			OrderExecutor.stats.accepted += 1
			table.insert(OrderExecutor.acceptanceLatencies, latency)

			Logger.debug("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "accepted", {
				unit = record.id,
				purpose = pendingEntry.order.purpose,
				latencySeconds = latency,
				by = attributeName,
			})
		end

		-- While the order is unaccepted, only its end (below) matters; its own writes are not the user's.
		if attributeName == "targetTile" then
			return
		end
	end

	if record.owner ~= OrderExecutor.ourCountryName then
		return
	end

	if attributeName == "army" then
		if value == nil then
			OrderExecutor.leftArmy[record.id] = true
		end

		return
	end

	-- Between two hops of one path the unit is briefly Idle with no targetTile while currentPath
	-- still lists the tiles left (state-model.md §3.5); the order is over only when that list is
	-- empty too. The three writes arrive in any order, so the test runs on each of them.
	local state = record.model:GetAttribute("state")
	local targetTile = record.model:GetAttribute("targetTile")
	local remaining = record.model:GetAttribute("currentPath")

	if state == "Idle" and targetTile == nil and (remaining == nil or remaining == EMPTY_PATH_JSON) then
		if OrderExecutor.userControlled[record.id] then
			OrderExecutor.userControlled[record.id] = nil
			Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "userOverrideEnded", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile })
		end

		OrderExecutor.leftArmy[record.id] = nil

		return
	end

	if attributeName ~= "targetTile" or value == nil then
		return
	end

	-- A defender beaten at 10 retreats: the server writes targetTile, state = "Retreating" and the
	-- flag in one frame, but the change events arrive one by one (second live run: 9 retreats were
	-- taken for user orders). Every recorded retreat started at exactly 10 health.
	local health = record.model:GetAttribute("health")
	if state == "Retreating" or (type(health) == "number" and health <= CombatFormulas.DEFENDER_HEALTH_FLOOR) then
		return
	end

	local committed = OrderExecutor.committed[record.id]
	if committed ~= nil and table.find(committed.path, value) ~= nil then
		return
	end

	if record.model:GetAttribute("army") ~= nil then
		return
	end

	if OrderExecutor.leftArmy[record.id] then
		Logger.debug("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "armyOrderLeftover", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, targetTile = value })
		return
	end

	if not OrderExecutor.userControlled[record.id] then
		OrderExecutor.userControlled[record.id] = true
		OrderExecutor.stats.userOverrides += 1

		Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "userOverride", {
			unit = record.id,
			tile = record.tile,
			targetTile = value,
			reason = "our unit started a path the executor did not send; left to the user until it is Idle",
		})
	end
end

---Every pending order older than one measured tick period is reported and dropped, except an order
---to a unit that is defending: it waits for the defence to end and keeps its place.
local function checkTimeouts()
	local period = Tick.periodSeconds()

	if period == nil then
		return
	end

	local now = os.clock()

	for unitId, pendingEntry in pairs(OrderExecutor.pending) do
		if now - pendingEntry.sentClock > period then
			local record = nil
			for candidate in pairs(Units.records) do
				if candidate.id == unitId and not candidate.superseded then
					record = candidate
				end
			end

			local stateNow = record and record.model:GetAttribute("state") or nil

			if stateNow == "Defending" then
				-- An order whose first step has not started waits while the unit defends and runs when
				-- the defence ends (ground-commands.md §3.2); the clock restarts, the order stays.
				pendingEntry.sentClock = now

				if not pendingEntry.deferredLogged then
					pendingEntry.deferredLogged = true
					Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "orderWaitsForDefence", {
						unit = unitId,
						path = pendingEntry.order.path,
						purpose = pendingEntry.order.purpose,
					})
				end
			else
				OrderExecutor.pending[unitId] = nil
				OrderExecutor.committed[unitId] = nil
				OrderExecutor.stats.notAccepted += 1

				Logger.error("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "orderNotAccepted", {
					unit = unitId,
					path = pendingEntry.order.path,
					purpose = pendingEntry.order.purpose,
					waitedSeconds = now - pendingEntry.sentClock,
					boundSeconds = period,
					unitTile = record and record.tile or nil,
					unitState = stateNow or "gone",
					unitTarget = record and record.model:GetAttribute("targetTile") or nil,
				})
			end
		end
	end
end

---Connect acceptance tracking.
---@param maid table
function OrderExecutor.attach(maid)
	maid:give(Units.unitChanged:connect(onUnitChanged))
	maid:give(Tick.dayTick:connect(checkTimeouts))
	maid:give(Units.unitRemoved:connect(function(record)
		if not record.superseded then
			OrderExecutor.pending[record.id] = nil
			OrderExecutor.committed[record.id] = nil
			OrderExecutor.userControlled[record.id] = nil
			OrderExecutor.leftArmy[record.id] = nil
			OrderExecutor.lastSent[record.id] = nil
		end
	end))

	Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "attached", {})
end

---Forget every pending order and override (country change or detach).
---@param reason string
function OrderExecutor.reset(reason)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.pending)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.committed)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.userControlled)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.lastDryRun)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.lastSent)
	table.clear(OrderExecutor.leftArmy)
	Logger.info("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", "reset", { reason = reason })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Orders.OrderExecutor", OrderExecutor)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Review/PlanOverlay"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Review/PlanOverlay

	Draws the last cycle's planned orders over the map (ui.showPlans): one thin bar per hop of every
	order, coloured by what the order is for, plus a marker on every locked tile (retreat lanes, the
	spawn tile). The parts live in a local folder in workspace, are anchored, non-colliding and not
	queryable, and are rebuilt every cycle. Everything here is display only; no size or colour enters
	a decision.
]]

local PlanOverlay = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

-- Constants (display only).
local FOLDER_NAME = "CTW_WarAI_Plan"
local BAR_THICKNESS = 0.6
local HEIGHT_ABOVE_TILE = 1.5
local LOCK_MARKER_SIZE = 1.2
local BAR_TRANSPARENCY = 0.25
local COLOR_ATTACK = Color3.fromRGB(235, 64, 52)
local COLOR_DEFENCE = Color3.fromRGB(52, 152, 235)
local COLOR_MOVE = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
local COLOR_LOCK = Color3.fromRGB(245, 212, 66)
local ATTACK_TIERS = { A0 = true, A1 = true, A2 = true, A3 = true, A4 = true }
local DEFENCE_TIERS = { T0 = true, T1 = true, T2 = true, T3 = true }
-- T5: a bar's centre is halfway between its two end points.
local HALF = 0.5
-- T5: path[1] is the start tile; bars begin at the second entry.
local FIRST_HOP_INDEX = 2

-- State.
PlanOverlay.folder = nil

---The overlay folder (created on first use, owned by the caller's maid).
---@param maid table
---@return Folder
local function folderFor(maid)
	if PlanOverlay.folder == nil or PlanOverlay.folder.Parent == nil then
		local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
		folder.Name = FOLDER_NAME
		folder.Parent = workspace
		PlanOverlay.folder = folder
		maid:give(folder)
	end

	return PlanOverlay.folder
end

---A display part.
---@param parent Instance
---@param size Vector3
---@param cframe CFrame
---@param color Color3
local function addPart(parent, size, cframe, color)
	local part = Instance.new("Part")
	part.Anchored = true
	part.CanCollide = false
	part.CanQuery = false
	part.CanTouch = false
	part.CastShadow = false
	part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
	part.Transparency = BAR_TRANSPARENCY
	part.Color = color
	part.Size = size
	part.CFrame = cframe
	part.Parent = parent
end

---Redraw the overlay for a plan.
---@param plan table
---@param maid table
function PlanOverlay.draw(plan, maid)
	local folder = folderFor(maid)
	folder:ClearAllChildren()

	if not Settings.get("ui.showPlans") then
		return
	end

	local lift = Vector3.new(0, HEIGHT_ABOVE_TILE, 0)
	local bars = 0

	for _, order in ipairs(plan.orders) do
		local color = COLOR_MOVE
		if ATTACK_TIERS[order.tier] then
			color = COLOR_ATTACK
		elseif DEFENCE_TIERS[order.tier] then
			color = COLOR_DEFENCE
		end

		for index = FIRST_HOP_INDEX, #order.path do
			local fromPosition = MapGraph.tiles[order.path[index - 1]].position + lift
			local toPosition = MapGraph.tiles[order.path[index]].position + lift
			local length = (toPosition - fromPosition).Magnitude
			local centre = fromPosition:Lerp(toPosition, HALF)

			addPart(folder, Vector3.new(BAR_THICKNESS, BAR_THICKNESS, length), CFrame.lookAt(centre, toPosition), color)
			bars += 1
		end
	end

	for tileName in pairs(plan.locks) do
		local position = MapGraph.tiles[tileName].position + lift
		addPart(folder, Vector3.new(LOCK_MARKER_SIZE, LOCK_MARKER_SIZE, LOCK_MARKER_SIZE), CFrame.new(position), COLOR_LOCK)
	end

	Logger.trace("Features.Review.PlanOverlay", "drawn", { bars = bars })
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Review.PlanOverlay", PlanOverlay)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Review/Scorecard"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Review/Scorecard

	Measures the AI instead of claiming it (DESIGN §11):
	- attack predictions: every attack the executor sent is recorded with its predicted ratio and
	  break time; it resolves as won when the defender retreats or dies, as lost when all our
	  attackers stopped while the defender still stands; the break time is compared with the
	  prediction;
	- hit interval (T2): the time between two consecutive health drops of one of our attackers while
	  it keeps attacking the same tile; the median replaces the recorded 1.0494 s in the combat model;
	- order acceptance and reaction latency come from the executor and the scheduler.
]]

local Scorecard = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Stats
local Stats = require("Utility/Stats")

---@module Game.Units
local Units = require("Game/Units")

---@module Features.Combat.CombatModel
local CombatModel = require("Features/Combat/CombatModel")

---@module Features.Orders.OrderExecutor
local OrderExecutor = require("Features/Orders/OrderExecutor")

-- Constants.
local ATTACK_PURPOSES = { capitalStrike = true, kill = true, surroundKill = true, relief = true, attack = true, cutOffAttack = true }
local BEATEN_STATES = { Retreating = true, Dead = true }

-- State.
Scorecard.predictions = {}
Scorecard.resolved = { won = 0, lost = 0, predictedWonAndWon = 0, breakErrors = {} }
Scorecard.lastHit = {}
Scorecard.attackTarget = {}
Scorecard.interruptions = {}

---Record the attacks a cycle actually sent.
---@param world table
---@param plan table
---@param canSend boolean
function Scorecard.recordLaunches(world, plan, canSend)
	if not canSend then
		return
	end

	for _, order in ipairs(plan.orders) do
		if ATTACK_PURPOSES[order.purpose] and order.isAttack then
			local defender = world.unitsByTile[order.destination]

			if defender ~= nil and Scorecard.predictions[defender.id] == nil then
				Scorecard.predictions[defender.id] = {
					targetTile = order.destination,
					defenderTroop = defender.troopName,
					predictedRatio = order.facts and order.facts.ratio or nil,
					predictedBreakSeconds = order.facts and order.facts.breakSeconds or nil,
					startServerTime = world.serverTime,
					purpose = order.purpose,
				}
			end
		end
	end
end

---Resolve predictions and measure hit intervals from unit changes. Hot path.
---@param record table
---@param attributeName string
---@param value any
local function onUnitChanged(record, attributeName, value)
	if attributeName == "state" then
		local prediction = Scorecard.predictions[record.id]

		if prediction ~= nil and BEATEN_STATES[value] then
			Scorecard.predictions[record.id] = nil
			Scorecard.resolved.won += 1

			local actual = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() - prediction.startServerTime

			if prediction.predictedBreakSeconds ~= nil then
				table.insert(Scorecard.resolved.breakErrors, actual - prediction.predictedBreakSeconds)
				Scorecard.resolved.predictedWonAndWon += 1
			end

			Logger.info("Features.Review.Scorecard", "attackWon", {
				target = prediction.targetTile,
				troop = prediction.defenderTroop,
				outcome = value,
				actualSeconds = actual,
				predictedSeconds = prediction.predictedBreakSeconds,
				predictedRatio = prediction.predictedRatio,
				purpose = prediction.purpose,
			})
		end

		if record.owner == Units.ourCountryName then
			if value == "Attacking" then
				Scorecard.attackTarget[record] = record.model:GetAttribute("targetTile")
			else
				local target = Scorecard.attackTarget[record]
				Scorecard.attackTarget[record] = nil
				Scorecard.lastHit[record] = nil

				-- Why an attack of ours stopped before its defender broke: Defending = an enemy
				-- attacked our attacker (an attacked attacker stops, ground-combat.md §3.7),
				-- Idle / Waiting = it reached 20 or the defender left, Moving = re-ordered.
				if target ~= nil then
					local stillStanding = false
					for defenderId, pending in pairs(Scorecard.predictions) do
						if pending.targetTile == target then
							stillStanding = true
						end
					end

					if stillStanding then
						Scorecard.interruptions[value] = (Scorecard.interruptions[value] or 0) + 1

						Logger.info("Features.Review.Scorecard", "attackInterrupted", {
							unit = record.id,
							troop = record.troopName,
							target = target,
							newState = value,
							health = record.model:GetAttribute("health"),
						})
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return
	end

	-- targetTile and state arrive as separate writes in either order; keep the attack target current.
	if attributeName == "targetTile" and record.owner == Units.ourCountryName and value ~= nil and record.model:GetAttribute("state") == "Attacking" then
		Scorecard.attackTarget[record] = value
		return
	end

	if attributeName == "health" and record.owner == Units.ourCountryName and record.model:GetAttribute("state") == "Attacking" then
		local target = record.model:GetAttribute("targetTile")
		local now = os.clock()
		local last = Scorecard.lastHit[record]

		if last ~= nil and last.target == target and type(value) == "number" and value < last.health then
			table.insert(CombatModel.measuredHitIntervals, now - last.clock)
		end

		Scorecard.lastHit[record] = { target = target, clock = now, health = value }
	end
end

---Resolve predictions whose attackers all stopped (called each tick).
---@param world table
function Scorecard.resolveStopped(world)
	for defenderId, prediction in pairs(Scorecard.predictions) do
		local defender = world.unitsById[defenderId]
		local stillAttacked = false

		if defender ~= nil then
			for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
				local commitment = OrderExecutor.committed[unit.id]
				local attacking = unit.state == "Attacking" and unit.targetTile == defender.tile
				-- An order sent but not yet accepted (about 0.2 s) still counts.
				local ordered = commitment ~= nil and commitment.destination == defender.tile

				if attacking or ordered then
					stillAttacked = true
				end
			end
		end

		if defender == nil then
			-- The defender left the map without a beaten state we saw (a kill can destroy without "Dead").
			Scorecard.predictions[defenderId] = nil
			Scorecard.resolved.won += 1
		elseif not stillAttacked then
			Scorecard.predictions[defenderId] = nil
			Scorecard.resolved.lost += 1

			Logger.warn("Features.Review.Scorecard", "attackLost", {
				target = prediction.targetTile,
				troop = prediction.defenderTroop,
				defenderHealth = defender.health,
				predictedRatio = prediction.predictedRatio,
				purpose = prediction.purpose,
			})
		end
	end
end

---Summary for the menu and logs.
---@return table
function Scorecard.summary()
	local medianError = Stats.median(Scorecard.resolved.breakErrors)

	local open = 0
	for _ in pairs(Scorecard.predictions) do
		open += 1
	end

	local interval, intervalSource = CombatModel.hitIntervalSeconds()

	return {
		interruptions = Scorecard.interruptions,
		attacksWon = Scorecard.resolved.won,
		attacksLost = Scorecard.resolved.lost,
		open = open,
		medianBreakErrorSeconds = medianError,
		hitIntervalSeconds = interval,
		hitIntervalSource = intervalSource,
	}
end

---Connect the measurements.
---@param maid table
function Scorecard.attach(maid)
	maid:give(Units.unitChanged:connect(onUnitChanged))
	maid:give(Units.unitRemoved:connect(function(record)
		Scorecard.lastHit[record] = nil
		Scorecard.attackTarget[record] = nil
	end))

	Logger.info("Features.Review.Scorecard", "attached", {})
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Review.Scorecard", Scorecard)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Features/Supply/SupplyModel"] = [=[
--[[
	Features/Supply/SupplyModel

	Predicts the server's supply pass (user rule U1, verified with 0 exceptions over 159,592
	own-tile unit-ticks, air.md §3.10 and war-and-loop.md §3.13): a tile owned by country C is
	supplied when a chain of land tiles owned by C links it to C's capital tile or to any city tile
	C owns whose city folder has isPort. Allied, overlord, enemy and neutral land does not carry
	supply; airfields and other cities are not sources. A unit standing on another country's tile
	is judged by that tile owner's network.

	`noSupply` then appears on the second pass without a stamp (supply.cutAfterTicks = 2) and
	costs x noSupplyCombatCoef in combat, x0.3 speed and all healing. The live flag and the tile's
	lastTimeSupplied remain the truth; this model predicts cuts before they happen and ranks
	captures by how many enemy units they would cut off (supply.neckValue).
]]

local SupplyModel = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

-- Constants.
-- T3 (recorded rule): noSupply appears on the second consecutive pass without a stamp.
local CUT_AFTER_TICKS = 2

Formula.define({
	id = "supply.cutAfterTicks",
	tier = "T3",
	source = "recorded supply census: noSupply true after two consecutive missed stamps (rec1 543/543, rec2 1,037/1,037; war-and-loop.md §3.13)",
	expression = "2",
	unit = "day ticks",
	inputs = {},
	compute = function()
		return CUT_AFTER_TICKS
	end,
	examples = {
		{ inputs = {}, expected = 2, tolerance = 0 },
	},
})

---Supply sources of a country: its capital tile (while it owns it) and every port city tile it owns.
---@param world table
---@param countryName string
---@return table array of tile names
local function sourcesOf(world, countryName)
	local sources = {}
	local capitalTile = Ownership.capitalTile(countryName)

	if capitalTile ~= nil and world.tileOwner[capitalTile] == countryName then
		table.insert(sources, capitalTile)
	end

	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)

	if country ~= nil then
		for _, cityFolder in ipairs(Resolver.requireChild(country, "Cities"):GetChildren()) do
			if cityFolder:GetAttribute("isPort") == true then
				local tileName = cityFolder:GetAttribute("tileName")

				if type(tileName) == "string" and world.tileOwner[tileName] == countryName and tileName ~= capitalTile then
					table.insert(sources, tileName)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return sources
end

---Breadth-first reach over a country's own land from its sources, optionally treating one tile as
---lost. Hot path.
---@param world table
---@param countryName string
---@param sources table
---@param removedTile string?
---@return table set of supplied tile names
local function reach(world, countryName, sources, removedTile)
	local reached = {}
	local queue = {}
	local head = 1

	for _, source in ipairs(sources) do
		if source ~= removedTile and not reached[source] then
			reached[source] = true
			table.insert(queue, source)
		end
	end

	while head <= #queue do
		local current = queue[head]
		head += 1

		for _, neighbourName in ipairs(MapGraph.tiles[current].landNeighbours) do
			if not reached[neighbourName] and neighbourName ~= removedTile and world.tileOwner[neighbourName] == countryName then
				reached[neighbourName] = true
				table.insert(queue, neighbourName)
			end
		end
	end

	return reached
end

---Supplied tiles of a country, cached in the world view for the cycle.
---@param world table
---@param countryName string
---@return table set
function SupplyModel.suppliedTiles(world, countryName)
	local cache = world.supplyCache
	local cached = cache[countryName]

	if cached ~= nil then
		return cached.reached
	end

	local sources = sourcesOf(world, countryName)
	local reached = reach(world, countryName, sources, nil)

	cache[countryName] = { reached = reached, sources = sources }

	return reached
end

---True when the model predicts that a unit on this tile is supplied.
---@param world table
---@param tileName string
---@return boolean
function SupplyModel.isSupplied(world, tileName)
	local owner = world.tileOwner[tileName]

	if owner == nil then
		return false
	end

	return SupplyModel.suppliedTiles(world, owner)[tileName] == true
end

---Units of a country that would lose supply if the given tile (owned by that country) changed
---owner: those on tiles supplied now and not supplied without the tile. The tile's own unit counts
---too when it is not a source itself.
---@param world table
---@param countryName string
---@param tileName string
---@return table array of unit snapshots that would be cut off
function SupplyModel.unitsCutByLosing(world, countryName, tileName)
	SupplyModel.suppliedTiles(world, countryName)

	local cached = world.supplyCache[countryName]
	local without = reach(world, countryName, cached.sources, tileName)
	local cut = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.unitsOf[countryName] or {}) do
		local unitTile = unit.tile
		if unitTile ~= tileName and cached.reached[unitTile] and not without[unitTile] then
			table.insert(cut, unit)
		end
	end

	return cut
end

---Log a summary of the supply prediction for our country against the live flags.
---@param world table
function SupplyModel.logOurPrediction(world)
	local reached = SupplyModel.suppliedTiles(world, world.me)
	local predictedCut = {}
	local liveFlagged = {}

	for _, unit in ipairs(world.ourUnits) do
		if world.tileOwner[unit.tile] == world.me and not reached[unit.tile] then
			table.insert(predictedCut, unit.tile)
		end

		if unit.noSupply then
			table.insert(liveFlagged, unit.tile)
		end
	end

	Logger.debug("Features.Supply.SupplyModel", "ourSupply", {
		sources = world.supplyCache[world.me].sources,
		predictedCutTiles = predictedCut,
		liveNoSupplyTiles = liveFlagged,
	})
end

return Tracer.instrument("Features.Supply.SupplyModel", SupplyModel)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Arrows"] = [=[
--[[
	Game/Arrows

	The server's movement arrows in workspace.Arrows (MoveArrow, AttackArrow, RetreatArrow,
	NavyMoveArrow). An arrow has no owner attribute; it is linked to a move by its pivot, which sits at
	the XZ midpoint of the two tile centres (within 0.02 studs over every recorded arrow, map.md §3.13)
	and whose look vector points at the destination. A MoveArrow's or RetreatArrow's endTime is the
	server time of arrival.
]]

local Arrows = {}

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

-- Constants.
-- T2 recorded: largest distance between an arrow pivot and its tile-pair midpoint (XZ), map.md §3.13.
local PIVOT_TOLERANCE_STUDS = 0.02
local ARROWS_FOLDER = "Arrows"
-- T5: arithmetic midpoint of two points.
local MIDPOINT_DIVISOR = 2

---Server time at which a move of the given kind from one tile to a neighbour ends, or nil when no
---matching arrow exists.
---@param arrowName string "MoveArrow" | "RetreatArrow" | "NavyMoveArrow"
---@param fromName string
---@param toName string
---@return number?
function Arrows.endTime(arrowName, fromName, toName)
	local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild(ARROWS_FOLDER)
	local fromRecord = MapGraph.tiles[fromName]
	local toRecord = MapGraph.tiles[toName]

	if folder == nil or fromRecord == nil or toRecord == nil then
		return nil
	end

	local midpoint = (fromRecord.position + toRecord.position) / MIDPOINT_DIVISOR

	for _, arrow in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if arrow.Name == arrowName and arrow:IsA("PVInstance") then
			local pivot = arrow:GetPivot().Position
			local offsetX = pivot.X - midpoint.X
			local offsetZ = pivot.Z - midpoint.Z

			if math.sqrt(offsetX * offsetX + offsetZ * offsetZ) <= PIVOT_TOLERANCE_STUDS then
				local endTime = arrow:GetAttribute("endTime")

				if type(endTime) == "number" then
					return endTime
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.Arrows", Arrows)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/CountryState"] = [=[
--[[
	Game/CountryState

	Readers for a country's replicated state: treasury, production items, research, focus, army
	classes and groups, cities, and the modifier folders (Army, Techs). Every read goes to the live
	attribute (T0) at call time, so a plan never works from a stale copy; required attributes raise
	with their path when missing (REFERENCE R2 lists them).
]]

local CountryState = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Serializer
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

-- Constants.
local ARMY_CLASS_NAMES_SOURCE = "Army.Classes children (a class folder appears with the first tech of the class)"

---Required number attribute.
---@param instance Instance
---@param attributeName string
---@return number
local function number(instance, attributeName)
	return Resolver.requireAttribute(instance, attributeName, "number")
end

---Treasury and headline counters of a country.
---@param country Instance
---@return table
function CountryState.treasury(country)
	return {
		money = number(country, "money"),
		manpower = number(country, "manpower"),
		politicalPower = number(country, "politicalPower"),
		divisions = number(country, "divisions"),
		factories = number(country, "factories"),
		tileCount = number(country, "tileCount"),
	}
end

---Every production item with its live numbers (REFERENCE R2, R15).
---@param country Instance
---@return table array sorted by name
function CountryState.productionItems(country)
	local folder = Resolver.requireChild(country, "ProductionItems")
	local items = {}

	for _, item in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		table.insert(items, {
			name = item.Name,
			instance = item,
			stock = number(item, "stock"),
			production = number(item, "production"),
			consumption = Resolver.optionalAttribute(item, "consumption", "number", "written from the first healing draw on"),
			factories = number(item, "factories"),
			factoriesValue = number(item, "factoriesValue"),
			outputCoef = number(item, "outputCoef"),
			maxStock = Resolver.optionalAttribute(item, "maxStock", "number", "Consumer Goods has no stock cap (economy.md §3)"),
		})
	end

	table.sort(items, function(first, second)
		return first.name < second.name
	end)

	return items
end

---Every tech of the country: price, duration, progress, purchased.
---@param country Instance
---@return table name -> record
function CountryState.techs(country)
	local folder = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Techs")
	local techs = {}

	for _, tech in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if tech:IsA("ObjectValue") then
			techs[tech.Name] = {
				name = tech.Name,
				instance = tech,
				price = number(tech, "price"),
				duration = number(tech, "duration"),
				progress = number(tech, "progress"),
				purchased = Resolver.optionalAttribute(tech, "purchased", "boolean", "absent until the tech is bought") == true,
			}
		end
	end

	return techs
end

---Research slots: slot name -> tech name or false when free.
---@param country Instance
---@return table
function CountryState.researchSlots(country)
	local folder = Resolver.requireChild(country, "ResearchSlots")
	local slots = {}

	for _, slot in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if slot:IsA("ObjectValue") then
			slots[slot.Name] = slot.Value and slot.Value.Name or false
		end
	end

	return slots
end

---Focus tree of the current ideology with the running focuses.
---@param country Instance
---@return table { current = array of names, focuses = name -> record }
function CountryState.focuses(country)
	local government = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Government")
	local folder = Resolver.requireChild(government, "Focuses")
	local currentText = Resolver.requireAttribute(folder, "currentFocuses", "string")
	local focuses = {}

	for _, focus in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if focus:IsA("ObjectValue") then
			local requiredText = Resolver.requireAttribute(focus, "required", "string")

			focuses[focus.Name] = {
				name = focus.Name,
				instance = focus,
				duration = number(focus, "duration"),
				ppCost = number(focus, "ppCost"),
				progress = number(focus, "progress"),
				required = Serializer.decode(requiredText, focus:GetFullName() .. ".required"),
				purchased = Resolver.optionalAttribute(focus, "purchased", "boolean", "absent until the focus is started") == true,
				effectLines = Resolver.optionalAttribute(focus, "effectLines", "string", "only some focus trees replicate effect text"),
			}
		end
	end

	return {
		current = Serializer.decode(currentText, folder:GetFullName() .. ".currentFocuses"),
		focuses = focuses,
		focusSpeed = Resolver.requireAttribute(government, "focusSpeed", "number"),
	}
end

---Army classes (training pipeline) by class name.
---@param country Instance
---@return table class -> record
function CountryState.armyClasses(country)
	local army = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Army")
	local classesFolder = Resolver.requireChild(army, "Classes")
	local classes = {}

	for _, classFolder in ipairs(classesFolder:GetChildren()) do
		classes[classFolder.Name] = {
			name = classFolder.Name,
			folder = classFolder,
			count = number(classFolder, "count"),
			target = number(classFolder, "target"),
			startedTrainingTime = number(classFolder, "startedTrainingTime"),
			trainDuration = number(classFolder, "trainDuration"),
			baseCost = number(classFolder, "baseCost"),
			costIncrease = number(classFolder, "costIncrease"),
			hasManpower = Resolver.requireAttribute(classFolder, "hasManpower", "boolean"),
		}
	end

	Logger.trace("Game.CountryState", "armyClassesRead", { country = country.Name, source = ARMY_CLASS_NAMES_SOURCE })

	return classes
end

---Army groups with their settings.
---@param country Instance
---@return table array
function CountryState.armies(country)
	local army = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Army")
	local armiesFolder = Resolver.requireChild(army, "Armies")
	local armies = {}

	for _, armyFolder in ipairs(armiesFolder:GetChildren()) do
		table.insert(armies, {
			name = armyFolder.Name,
			folder = armyFolder,
			command = Resolver.optionalAttribute(armyFolder, "command", "string", "set by ChangeArmyOrder; new armies start with Attack"),
			replenishTroops = Resolver.optionalAttribute(armyFolder, "replenishTroops", "boolean", "new armies start with true"),
			divisions = Resolver.optionalAttribute(armyFolder, "divisions", "number", "new armies start with 0"),
			targetCountries = Resolver.optionalAttribute(armyFolder, "targetCountries", "string", "human-created armies have none"),
		})
	end

	table.sort(armies, function(first, second)
		return first.name < second.name
	end)

	return armies
end

---Cities of the country with their economic attributes.
---@param country Instance
---@return table array sorted by name
function CountryState.cities(country)
	local citiesFolder = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Cities")
	local cities = {}

	for _, cityFolder in ipairs(citiesFolder:GetChildren()) do
		table.insert(cities, {
			name = cityFolder.Name,
			folder = cityFolder,
			tileName = Resolver.requireAttribute(cityFolder, "tileName", "string"),
			isCapital = Resolver.optionalAttribute(cityFolder, "isCapital", "boolean", "set on capitals only") == true,
			isPort = Resolver.optionalAttribute(cityFolder, "isPort", "boolean", "set on ports only") == true,
			isAirfield = Resolver.optionalAttribute(cityFolder, "isAirfield", "boolean", "set on airfield cities only") == true,
			population = Resolver.optionalAttribute(cityFolder, "population", "number", "static city data"),
			factories = number(cityFolder, "factories"),
			maxFactories = number(cityFolder, "maxFactories"),
			defenses = number(cityFolder, "defenses"),
			maxDefenses = number(cityFolder, "maxDefenses"),
			factoryBuildQueue = Resolver.optionalAttribute(cityFolder, "factoryBuildQueue", "number", "set while factories are queued") or 0,
			defenseBuildQueue = Resolver.optionalAttribute(cityFolder, "defenseBuildQueue", "number", "set while defenses are queued") or 0,
		})
	end

	table.sort(cities, function(first, second)
		return first.name < second.name
	end)

	return cities
end

---An attribute of a country's modifier folder (Army, Techs, Economy, Government, Navy, Airforce).
---@param country Instance
---@param folderName string
---@param attributeName string
---@return number
function CountryState.folderNumber(country, folderName, attributeName)
	return number(Resolver.requireChild(country, folderName), attributeName)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.CountryState", CountryState)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Fleets"] = [=[
--[[
	Game/Fleets

	Fleets at sea: a Model named "Fleet" on a sea tile (several may share a tile) with the attributes
	country, color, ModelName, AAPower, state, targetTile, currentPath, and a Ships child holding the
	ship ObjectValues (shipType, class, attack, maxAttack, health, maxHealth, speed, AAPower,
	divisionCapacity, loadedDivisions). Navy.Fleets goes stale, so fleets are read from the map
	(navy.md §2). The scan runs once per day tick; fleets move in legs of several seconds.
]]

local Fleets = {}

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Serializer
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

-- Constants.
local FLEET_MODEL_NAME = "Fleet"
local EMPTY_CARGO = "[]"

---Every fleet on the map.
---@return table array of { model, tile, country, state, targetTile, currentPath, ships, loadedDivisions, damaged }
function Fleets.scan()
	local fleets = {}

	for _, tileName in ipairs(MapGraph.tileOrder) do
		local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]

		if record.isSea then
			for _, child in ipairs(record.instance:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name == FLEET_MODEL_NAME and child:IsA("Model") then
					local shipsFolder = Resolver.optionalChild(child, "Ships", "a fleet Model is filled with its Ships folder right after it appears")
					local ships = {}
					local loaded = 0
					local damaged = false

					if shipsFolder ~= nil then
						for _, ship in ipairs(shipsFolder:GetChildren()) do
							local health = Resolver.requireAttribute(ship, "health", "number")
							local maxHealth = Resolver.requireAttribute(ship, "maxHealth", "number")
							local cargoText = Resolver.optionalAttribute(ship, "loadedDivisions", "string", "only transports and battleships carry divisions")
							local cargo = 0

							if cargoText ~= nil and cargoText ~= EMPTY_CARGO and cargoText ~= "" then
								local decoded = Serializer.decode(cargoText, ship:GetFullName() .. ".loadedDivisions")
								if type(decoded) == "table" then
									cargo = #decoded
								end
							end

							loaded += cargo
							damaged = damaged or health < maxHealth

							table.insert(ships, {
								instance = ship,
								shipType = Resolver.requireAttribute(ship, "shipType", "string"),
								health = health,
								maxHealth = maxHealth,
								speed = Resolver.requireAttribute(ship, "speed", "number"),
								cargo = cargo,
							})
						end
					end

					table.insert(fleets, {
						model = child,
						tile = tileName,
						country = Resolver.optionalAttribute(child, "country", "string", "set by the server when the fleet is created"),
						state = Resolver.optionalAttribute(child, "state", "string", "set by the server when the fleet is created"),
						targetTile = Resolver.optionalAttribute(child, "targetTile", "string", "set only while the fleet moves"),
						ships = ships,
						loadedDivisions = loaded,
						damaged = damaged,
					})
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return fleets
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.Fleets", Fleets)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/GameModules"] = [=[
--[[
	Game/GameModules

	The game's own shared ModuleScripts (ReplicatedStorage.MapData and ReplicatedStorage.Common.*).
	They are the same modules the server runs, so their tables and functions are derivation tier T3:
	terrain modifiers, river modifiers, troop base stats, tile distances, diplomacy relations, the
	research catalogue and the tile graph. Every member the AI relies on is checked when the module
	loads, so a game update that renames one stops the script with the missing name instead of
	letting a nil flow into a decision.
]]

local GameModules = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

-- Constants.
-- Module name -> slash path under ReplicatedStorage and the members the AI reads (T3, dump/scripts).
local MODULE_SPECS = {
	MapData = {
		path = "MapData",
		members = { "colorToCountry", "countryToCities", "tileNameToCity", "GetCityFolderFromTile" },
	},
	Utils = {
		path = "Common/Utils",
		members = { "secondsPerDay", "GetDistanceBetweenTiles" },
	},
	ArmyCommon = {
		path = "Common/ArmyCommon",
		members = { "terrainModifiers", "GetRiverModifierBetweenTiles", "troopNameToInfo", "upgrades", "divisionSize", "GetTroopBaseStatsRTF" },
	},
	TroopCommon = {
		path = "Common/TroopCommon",
		members = { "CalculateStatsAgainstTroop", "GetTroopClass", "GetTroopSpeed", "GetTroopConsumption" },
	},
	DiplomacyCommon = {
		path = "Common/DiplomacyCommon",
		members = { "AreCountriesFriendly", "CanAttackFromCountryTile", "GetCaptureCountryFromOriginTile", "CompareIdeologies" },
	},
	TileGraph = {
		path = "Common/TileGraph",
		members = { "GetAsync" },
	},
	ResearchCommon = {
		path = "Common/ResearchCommon",
		members = { "techs" },
	},
	GameLoopCommon = {
		path = "Common/GameLoopCommon",
		members = { "relationToCoef", "GetWinTileLimit", "CalculateCountryWinScore" },
	},
}

-- Services.
local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

-- State.
GameModules.loaded = {}

---Require one game module through the resolver and check every member the AI reads.
---@param moduleName string key of MODULE_SPECS
---@return table
function GameModules.loadModule(moduleName)
	local spec = MODULE_SPECS[moduleName]

	if spec == nil then
		return error(string.format("GameModules.loadModule: '%s' is not a known game module", tostring(moduleName)))
	end

	local moduleScript = Resolver.requireDescendant(replicatedStorage, spec.path)

	if not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		return error(string.format("GameModules.loadModule: '%s' is a %s, not a ModuleScript", moduleScript:GetFullName(), moduleScript.ClassName))
	end

	local moduleValue = require(moduleScript)

	if type(moduleValue) ~= "table" then
		return error(string.format("GameModules.loadModule: '%s' returned %s, expected a table", moduleScript:GetFullName(), typeof(moduleValue)))
	end

	local missingMembers = {}
	for _, memberName in ipairs(spec.members) do
		if moduleValue[memberName] == nil then
			table.insert(missingMembers, memberName)
		end
	end

	if #missingMembers > 0 then
		return error(string.format("GameModules.loadModule: '%s' lacks %s (game update?)", moduleScript:GetFullName(), table.concat(missingMembers, ", ")))
	end

	GameModules.loaded[moduleName] = moduleValue

	Logger.info("Game.GameModules", "loaded", {
		module = moduleName,
		instance = moduleScript:GetFullName(),
		checkedMembers = spec.members,
	})

	return moduleValue
end

---Load every game module the AI uses.
function GameModules.loadAll()
	for moduleName in pairs(MODULE_SPECS) do
		GameModules.loadModule(moduleName)
	end
end

Tracer.instrument("Game.GameModules", GameModules)

-- Hot-path functions, installed after instrumentation: they run thousands of times per planning
-- cycle (profiled in the first live run: 1.1 M trace records in 78 s). Failures still raise with a
-- named message, which the traced caller logs with its traceback.

---A loaded game module; asking before loadAll is a programming error.
---@param moduleName string
---@return table
function GameModules.get(moduleName)
	local moduleValue = GameModules.loaded[moduleName]

	if moduleValue == nil then
		return error(string.format("GameModules.get: '%s' is not loaded (call GameModules.loadAll first)", tostring(moduleName)))
	end

	return moduleValue
end

return GameModules


]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/MapGraph"] = [=[
--[[
	Game/MapGraph

	The static map: every tile's position, terrain, rivers, air region, city and neighbours. The
	adjacency comes from the game's own ReplicatedStorage.Common.TileGraph (the module the client's
	path preview uses): TileNeighbours parsed, de-duplicated, symmetrised and sorted by
	(name, x, z, y), so our neighbour order and therefore our tie-breaks equal the game's.

	Tile records are plain data read by the planners in hot loops (MapGraph.tiles[name].position),
	so field access costs no trace record; the accessor functions below are traced. Edge lengths and
	river modifiers come from Utils.GetDistanceBetweenTiles and ArmyCommon.GetRiverModifierBetweenTiles
	(T3) and are cached per edge.

	Land tile colour and attribute changes are forwarded as signals; ownership, units and the tick
	detector subscribe to them.
]]

local MapGraph = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Signal
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

-- Constants.
-- T3: sea tiles are the ones whose name contains "Sea" (PreviewPath.ComputeNavalPath, Utils.lua:356).
local SEA_NAME_FRAGMENT = "Sea"

-- State.
MapGraph.mapModel = nil
MapGraph.tiles = {}
MapGraph.tileOrder = {}
MapGraph.landCount = 0
MapGraph.seaCount = 0
MapGraph.edgeLengths = {}
MapGraph.edgeRivers = {}
MapGraph.tileColorChanged = Signal.new("Game.MapGraph.tileColorChanged")
MapGraph.tileAttributeChanged = Signal.new("Game.MapGraph.tileAttributeChanged")

---True for a sea tile name.
---@param tileName string
---@return boolean
local function isSeaName(tileName)
	return string.find(tileName, SEA_NAME_FRAGMENT, 1, true) ~= nil
end

---Build every tile record from the map Model. Yields while the game's TileGraph builds.
---@param mapModel Model
---@param maid table owns the per-tile connections
function MapGraph.build(mapModel, maid)
	local TileGraph = GameModules.get("TileGraph")
	local MapData = GameModules.get("MapData")

	-- nil batch size = TileGraph's own default (256, TileGraph.lua:40).
	local orderedTiles, neighboursByTile = TileGraph.GetAsync(mapModel, nil)

	if orderedTiles == nil or neighboursByTile == nil then
		return error(string.format("MapGraph.build: TileGraph.GetAsync returned nil for '%s' (a tile lacks TileNeighbours or the map was replaced)", mapModel:GetFullName()))
	end

	table.clear(MapGraph.tiles)
	table.clear(MapGraph.tileOrder)
	table.clear(MapGraph.edgeLengths)
	table.clear(MapGraph.edgeRivers)
	MapGraph.landCount = 0
	MapGraph.seaCount = 0
	MapGraph.mapModel = mapModel

	local cityTiles = 0
	local coastalTiles = 0

	for _, tileInstance in ipairs(orderedTiles) do
		local tileName = tileInstance.Name
		local isSea = isSeaName(tileName)
		local cityEntry = MapData.tileNameToCity[tileName]

		local record = {
			name = tileName,
			instance = tileInstance,
			isSea = isSea,
			position = tileInstance.Position,
			terrain = tileInstance:GetAttribute("terrain"),
			airRegion = tileInstance:GetAttribute("airRegion"),
			cityName = cityEntry and cityEntry.name or nil,
			neighbours = {},
			landNeighbours = {},
			seaNeighbours = {},
			isCoastal = false,
		}

		MapGraph.tiles[tileName] = record
		table.insert(MapGraph.tileOrder, tileName)

		if isSea then
			MapGraph.seaCount += 1
		else
			MapGraph.landCount += 1
		end

		if record.cityName ~= nil then
			cityTiles += 1
		end
	end

	for _, tileInstance in ipairs(orderedTiles) do
		local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileInstance.Name]

		for _, neighbourInstance in ipairs(neighboursByTile[tileInstance]) do
			local neighbourName = neighbourInstance.Name
			local neighbourRecord = MapGraph.tiles[neighbourName]

			if neighbourRecord == nil then
				return error(string.format("MapGraph.build: neighbour '%s' of '%s' is not a map tile", neighbourName, record.name))
			end

			table.insert(record.neighbours, neighbourName)

			if neighbourRecord.isSea then
				table.insert(record.seaNeighbours, neighbourName)
			else
				table.insert(record.landNeighbours, neighbourName)
			end
		end

		if not record.isSea and #record.seaNeighbours > 0 then
			record.isCoastal = true
			coastalTiles += 1
		end
	end

	-- Forward land tile changes. Sea tile colour is client-local (map.md §6) and is never read.
	for _, tileName in ipairs(MapGraph.tileOrder) do
		local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]

		if not record.isSea then
			local tileInstance = record.instance

			maid:give(tileInstance:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Color"):Connect(function()
				MapGraph.tileColorChanged:fire(tileName)
			end))

			maid:give(tileInstance.AttributeChanged:Connect(function(attributeName)
				MapGraph.tileAttributeChanged:fire(tileName, attributeName, tileInstance:GetAttribute(attributeName))
			end))
		end
	end

	Logger.info("Game.MapGraph", "built", {
		map = mapModel:GetFullName(),
		tiles = #MapGraph.tileOrder,
		land = MapGraph.landCount,
		sea = MapGraph.seaCount,
		cityTiles = cityTiles,
		coastalTiles = coastalTiles,
	})
end

---3D distance between two tiles (Utils.GetDistanceBetweenTiles, T3), cached per edge.
---Hot path: planners call MapGraph.edgeLength directly; this traced wrapper serves tools and tests.
---@param fromName string
---@param toName string
---@return number
function MapGraph.distance(fromName, toName)
	return MapGraph.edgeLength(fromName, toName)
end

---River modifier crossing from one tile to another (ArmyCommon.GetRiverModifierBetweenTiles, T3):
---0 without a river, else 0.2 .. 1 by the river letter. Cached per directed edge.
---@param fromName string
---@param toName string
---@return number
function MapGraph.riverModifier(fromName, toName)
	return MapGraph.edgeRiver(fromName, toName)
end

---Drop every tile record (map replaced).
---@param reason string
function MapGraph.clear(reason)
	Logger.info("Game.MapGraph", "cleared", { reason = reason, tiles = #MapGraph.tileOrder })

	table.clear(MapGraph.tiles)
	table.clear(MapGraph.tileOrder)
	table.clear(MapGraph.edgeLengths)
	table.clear(MapGraph.edgeRivers)
	MapGraph.mapModel = nil
	MapGraph.landCount = 0
	MapGraph.seaCount = 0
end

Tracer.instrument("Game.MapGraph", MapGraph)

-- Hot-path functions, installed after instrumentation: they run thousands of times per planning
-- cycle (profiled in the first live run: 1.1 M trace records in 78 s). Failures still raise with a
-- named message, which the traced caller logs with its traceback.

---The record of a tile; an unknown name raises.
---@param tileName string
---@return table
function MapGraph.tile(tileName)
	local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]

	if record == nil then
		return error(string.format("MapGraph.tile: unknown tile '%s'", tostring(tileName)))
	end

	return record
end

---True when tileName is a known land tile.
---@param tileName string
---@return boolean
function MapGraph.isLand(tileName)
	local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]
	return record ~= nil and not record.isSea
end

-- Hot-path accessors, installed after instrumentation so the planners' inner loops stay untraced.
-- They are pure cached reads of T3 functions; MapGraph.distance / riverModifier are their traced twins.

---Cached edge length in studs.
---@param fromName string
---@param toName string
---@return number
function MapGraph.edgeLength(fromName, toName)
	local fromLengths = MapGraph.edgeLengths[fromName]

	if fromLengths == nil then
		fromLengths = {}
		MapGraph.edgeLengths[fromName] = fromLengths
	end

	local length = fromLengths[toName]

	if length == nil then
		local fromRecord = MapGraph.tiles[fromName]
		local toRecord = MapGraph.tiles[toName]

		if fromRecord == nil or toRecord == nil then
			return error(string.format("MapGraph.edgeLength: unknown tile in '%s' -> '%s'", tostring(fromName), tostring(toName)))
		end

		length = GameModules.get("Utils").GetDistanceBetweenTiles(fromRecord.instance, toRecord.instance)
		fromLengths[toName] = length
	end

	return length
end

---Cached river modifier of a directed edge.
---@param fromName string
---@param toName string
---@return number
function MapGraph.edgeRiver(fromName, toName)
	local fromRivers = MapGraph.edgeRivers[fromName]

	if fromRivers == nil then
		fromRivers = {}
		MapGraph.edgeRivers[fromName] = fromRivers
	end

	local modifier = fromRivers[toName]

	if modifier == nil then
		local fromRecord = MapGraph.tiles[fromName]
		local toRecord = MapGraph.tiles[toName]

		if fromRecord == nil or toRecord == nil then
			return error(string.format("MapGraph.edgeRiver: unknown tile in '%s' -> '%s'", tostring(fromName), tostring(toName)))
		end

		modifier = GameModules.get("ArmyCommon").GetRiverModifierBetweenTiles(fromRecord.instance, toRecord.instance)
		fromRivers[toName] = modifier
	end

	return modifier
end

return MapGraph

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Ownership"] = [=[
--[[
	Game/Ownership

	Who owns what. A land tile belongs to the country whose `color` attribute equals the tile's Color
	(the game's own rule, MapData.colorToCountry[tile.Color:ToHex()], map.md §6); troops carry the
	same colour in their `color` attribute. The colour table is built from the live country
	attributes (T0) and cross-checked against MapData's table (T3).

	Relations follow DiplomacyCommon (T3): friendly = self, Allies, Puppets, overlord (puppetOf),
	co-puppets. Entries naming a dead country are skipped (a dead country's Diplomacy folders are
	frozen at death, war-and-loop.md §6). Movement colours follow PlayerControls.UpdateAllowedColors
	(own, Enemies, Allies, Puppets, overlord, overlord's Puppets).

	Capital tile: the city named by the country's `capital` attribute; its tile is the city folder's
	`tileName` while the folder is still the country's, else MapData.countryToCities (static data).
]]

local Ownership = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Signal
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

---@module Game.Session
local Session = require("Game/Session")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

-- State.
Ownership.countries = {}
Ownership.colorToCountry = {}
Ownership.tileOwner = {}
Ownership.countryTiles = {}
Ownership.countryTileCounts = {}
Ownership.unknownColors = {}
Ownership.countMismatchStreaks = {}
Ownership.ownerChanged = Signal.new("Game.Ownership.ownerChanged")

---Add a tile to a country's set.
---@param countryName string?
---@param tileName string
local function addTile(countryName, tileName)
	if countryName == nil then
		return
	end

	local tiles = Ownership.countryTiles[countryName]
	if tiles == nil then
		tiles = {}
		Ownership.countryTiles[countryName] = tiles
		Ownership.countryTileCounts[countryName] = 0
	end

	if not tiles[tileName] then
		tiles[tileName] = true
		Ownership.countryTileCounts[countryName] += 1
	end
end

---Remove a tile from a country's set.
---@param countryName string?
---@param tileName string
local function removeTile(countryName, tileName)
	if countryName == nil then
		return
	end

	local tiles = Ownership.countryTiles[countryName]
	if tiles ~= nil and tiles[tileName] then
		tiles[tileName] = nil
		Ownership.countryTileCounts[countryName] -= 1
	end
end

---Country name for a colour, or nil. A colour that matches no living country's is land nobody
---owns (the EurasiaWW2 map has such tiles, e.g. Tile619 #5b5d69): logged once per colour as a
---WARN, and the tile counts as unowned (never entered, never supplied).
---@param color Color3
---@param where string what carried the colour
---@return string?
local function countryForColor(color, where)
	local hex = string.lower(color:ToHex())
	local countryName = Ownership.colorToCountry[hex]

	if countryName == nil and not Ownership.unknownColors[hex] then
		Ownership.unknownColors[hex] = true
		Logger.warn("Game.Ownership", "unownedColor", { hex = hex, where = where, meaning = "no country has this colour: the tile is treated as unowned" })
	end

	return countryName
end

---Read the owner of one land tile from its colour and update the sets. Hot path (colour signal).
---@param tileName string
local function refreshTile(tileName)
	local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]

	if record == nil or record.isSea then
		return
	end

	local previous = Ownership.tileOwner[tileName]
	local current = countryForColor(record.instance.Color, tileName)

	if previous == current then
		return
	end

	removeTile(previous, tileName)
	addTile(current, tileName)
	Ownership.tileOwner[tileName] = current

	Ownership.ownerChanged:fire(tileName, previous, current)
end

---Living-country check used inside loops (untraced twin of Ownership.isAlive).
---@param countryName string
---@return boolean
local function aliveByName(countryName)
	local country = Ownership.countries[countryName]
	return country ~= nil and country.Parent ~= nil and country:GetAttribute("isDead") ~= true
end

---Rebuild the colour table from the live countries and compare it with MapData's.
function Ownership.rebuildCountries()
	table.clear(Ownership.countries)
	table.clear(Ownership.colorToCountry)

	local MapData = GameModules.get("MapData")
	local disagreements = {}

	for _, country in ipairs(Session.countriesFolder():GetChildren()) do
		if country:IsA("ObjectValue") then
			local color = Resolver.requireAttribute(country, "color", "Color3")
			local hex = string.lower(color:ToHex())

			if Ownership.colorToCountry[hex] ~= nil then
				return error(string.format("Ownership.rebuildCountries: '%s' and '%s' share colour %s", country.Name, Ownership.colorToCountry[hex], hex))
			end

			Ownership.countries[country.Name] = country
			Ownership.colorToCountry[hex] = country.Name

			local staticName = MapData.colorToCountry[hex] or MapData.colorToCountry[string.upper(hex)]
			if staticName ~= nil and staticName ~= country.Name then
				table.insert(disagreements, { hex = hex, live = country.Name, mapData = staticName })
			end
		end
	end

	if #disagreements > 0 then
		Logger.warn("Game.Ownership", "colorTableDisagrees", { disagreements = disagreements })
	end

	Logger.info("Game.Ownership", "countriesRebuilt", { countries = #Session.countriesFolder():GetChildren() })
end

---Read every land tile's owner.
function Ownership.rebuildTiles()
	table.clear(Ownership.tileOwner)
	table.clear(Ownership.countryTiles)
	table.clear(Ownership.countryTileCounts)

	for _, tileName in ipairs(MapGraph.tileOrder) do
		local record = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]

		if not record.isSea then
			local owner = countryForColor(record.instance.Color, tileName)
			Ownership.tileOwner[tileName] = owner
			addTile(owner, tileName)
		end
	end

	Logger.info("Game.Ownership", "tilesRebuilt", { countries = Ownership.countryTileCounts })
end

---Subscribe to colour changes and build the initial state.
---@param maid table
function Ownership.attach(maid)
	Ownership.rebuildCountries()
	Ownership.rebuildTiles()

	maid:give(MapGraph.tileColorChanged:connect(refreshTile))

	maid:give(Session.countriesFolder().ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		Logger.info("Game.Ownership", "countryAdded", { country = child.Name })
		Ownership.rebuildCountries()
	end))
end

---True when a server AI controls the country (Countries.<X>.Value is nil; war-and-loop.md §3.11).
---@param countryName string
---@return boolean
function Ownership.isAI(countryName)
	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)

	if country == nil then
		return error(string.format("Ownership.isAI: unknown country '%s'", tostring(countryName)))
	end

	return country.Value == nil
end

---Names of the living countries listed as children of a Diplomacy subfolder.
---@param countryName string
---@param folderName string "Enemies" | "Allies" | "Puppets" | "Justifications" | ...
---@return table set of names
function Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, folderName)
	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)

	if country == nil then
		return error(string.format("Ownership.diplomacyNames: unknown country '%s'", tostring(countryName)))
	end

	local diplomacy = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Diplomacy")
	local folder = Resolver.requireChild(diplomacy, folderName)
	local names = {}

	for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if aliveByName(child.Name) then
			names[child.Name] = true
		end
	end

	return names
end

---Living enemies of a country.
---@param countryName string
---@return table set
function Ownership.enemiesOf(countryName)
	return Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, "Enemies")
end

---Countries friendly to a country (self, Allies, Puppets, overlord, co-puppets), living only.
