---@param countryName string
---@return table set
function Ownership.friendsOf(countryName)
	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)

	if country == nil then
		return error(string.format("Ownership.friendsOf: unknown country '%s'", tostring(countryName)))
	end

	local friends = { [countryName] = true }

	for name in pairs(Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, "Allies")) do
		friends[name] = true
	end

	for name in pairs(Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, "Puppets")) do
		friends[name] = true
	end

	local overlordName = country:GetAttribute("puppetOf")

	if overlordName ~= nil then
		if aliveByName(overlordName) then
			friends[overlordName] = true
		end

		for otherName, other in pairs(Ownership.countries) do
			if other:GetAttribute("puppetOf") == overlordName and aliveByName(otherName) then
				friends[otherName] = true
			end
		end
	end

	return friends
end

---Countries whose tiles a country's troops may path through (PlayerControls.UpdateAllowedColors).
---@param countryName string
---@return table set
function Ownership.movementCountries(countryName)
	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)

	if country == nil then
		return error(string.format("Ownership.movementCountries: unknown country '%s'", tostring(countryName)))
	end

	local allowed = { [countryName] = true }

	for name in pairs(Ownership.enemiesOf(countryName)) do
		allowed[name] = true
	end

	for name in pairs(Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, "Allies")) do
		allowed[name] = true
	end

	for name in pairs(Ownership.diplomacyNames(countryName, "Puppets")) do
		allowed[name] = true
	end

	local overlordName = country:GetAttribute("puppetOf")

	if overlordName ~= nil and Ownership.country(overlordName) ~= nil then
		allowed[overlordName] = true

		for name in pairs(Ownership.diplomacyNames(overlordName, "Puppets")) do
			allowed[name] = true
		end
	end

	return allowed
end

---The land tile of a country's capital, or nil with the reason.
---@param countryName string
---@return string?, string?
function Ownership.capitalTile(countryName)
	local country = Ownership.country(countryName)

	if country == nil then
		return nil, "unknown country"
	end

	local capitalName = country:GetAttribute("capital")

	if type(capitalName) ~= "string" then
		return nil, "no capital attribute"
	end

	local cities = Resolver.requireChild(country, "Cities")
	local cityFolder = cities:FindFirstChild(capitalName)

	if cityFolder ~= nil then
		return Resolver.requireAttribute(cityFolder, "tileName", "string"), nil
	end

	local staticCities = GameModules.get("MapData").countryToCities[countryName]
	local staticCity = staticCities and staticCities[capitalName]

	if staticCity == nil or staticCity.tile == nil then
		return nil, string.format("capital '%s' is neither in Cities nor in MapData.countryToCities", capitalName)
	end

	return tostring(staticCity.tile), nil
end

---Compare our tile sets with every living country's tileCount. A mismatch that persists past one
---check is a model error (tile colours and tileCount disagree for up to 238 ms during annexations,
---state-model.md §5, so one check is tolerated).
---@return number mismatches that persisted
function Ownership.verifyCounts()
	local persisted = 0

	for countryName, country in pairs(Ownership.countries) do
		if country.Parent ~= nil and country:GetAttribute("isDead") ~= true then
			local reported = country:GetAttribute("tileCount")
			local counted = Ownership.countryTileCounts[countryName] or 0

			if type(reported) == "number" and reported ~= counted then
				local streak = (Ownership.countMismatchStreaks[countryName] or 0) + 1
				Ownership.countMismatchStreaks[countryName] = streak

				if streak > 1 then
					persisted += 1
					Logger.error("Game.Ownership", "tileCountMismatch", {
						country = countryName,
						reported = reported,
						counted = counted,
						consecutiveChecks = streak,
					})
				end
			else
				Ownership.countMismatchStreaks[countryName] = nil
			end
		end
	end

	return persisted
end

Tracer.instrument("Game.Ownership", Ownership)

-- Hot-path functions, installed after instrumentation: they run thousands of times per planning
-- cycle (profiled in the first live run: 1.1 M trace records in 78 s). Failures still raise with a
-- named message, which the traced caller logs with its traceback.

---The country instance by name, or nil when the game has no such country.
---@param countryName string
---@return Instance?
function Ownership.country(countryName)
	local country = Ownership.countries[countryName]

	if country ~= nil and country.Parent == nil then
		return nil
	end

	return country
end

---True when the country exists and is not dead.
---@param countryName string
---@return boolean
function Ownership.isAlive(countryName)
	return aliveByName(countryName)
end

---The country owning a troop Model, from its colour attribute.
---@param model Instance
---@return string?
function Ownership.troopOwner(model)
	local color = model:GetAttribute("color")

	if typeof(color) ~= "Color3" then
		return nil
	end

	return countryForColor(color, model:GetFullName())
end

return Ownership


]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Remotes"] = [=[
--[[
	Game/Remotes

	The only registry of remotes the AI may call (DESIGN §6 whitelist). Each entry names the path
	under ReplicatedStorage, the class the dump recorded (dump/data/remotes.json), the arguments the
	game's own client sends (scout reports, T3) and the reply channel. Diplomacy, country selection,
	admin, shop and tutorial remotes are deliberately absent, so no code path can reach them.

	Remotes.send is called only by Orders/OrderExecutor and Orders/ActionDispatcher, after the
	act-or-stop gate allowed the call; this module does not decide anything.
]]

local Remotes = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

-- Constants.
-- name -> { path under ReplicatedStorage, class, args, reply } (T3: dump/data/remotes.json and the
-- client callers cited in ground-commands.md §4, economy.md §4, navy.md §4, air.md §4).
local REGISTRY = {
	MoveTroop = { path = "Player/MoveTroop", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(path: {tile BasePart}) source first", reply = "unit attributes (targetTile/currentPath) 0.21-0.23 s later" },
	HaltTroop = { path = "Army/HaltTroop", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(tile BasePart) of the own troop", reply = "unit attributes" },
	AddTroopFromTileToArmy = { path = "Army/AddTroopFromTileToArmy", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(tile BasePart, armyName string) toggles membership", reply = "OnClientEvent(inst, 'On'|'Off'|error)" },
	ToggleArmyReplenish = { path = "Army/ToggleArmyReplenish", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(army Folder)", reply = "army replenishTroops flips" },
	SetPriorityCity = { path = "Army/SetPriorityCity", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(cityName string)", reply = "Army.priorityCity" },
	TryIncreaseTarget = { path = "Army/TryIncreaseTarget", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(classFolder)", reply = "(ok, msg)" },
	TryBuyManpower = { path = "Army/TryBuyManpower", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "()", reply = "(ok, msg)" },
	SetProductionPercentage = { path = "Production/SetProductionPercentage", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(item ObjectValue, value 0..1)", reply = "item factoriesValue/factories" },
	PurchaseFactory = { path = "Cities/PurchaseFactory", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(city Folder)", reply = "OnClientEvent(ok, msg); failure opens a Robux prompt in the game UI" },
	PurchaseDefense = { path = "Cities/PurchaseDefense", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(city Folder)", reply = "OnClientEvent(ok, msg); failure opens a Robux prompt in the game UI" },
	TryPurchaseTech = { path = "Research/TryPurchaseTech", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(tech ObjectValue)", reply = "truthy when bought" },
	StartResearch = { path = "Research/StartResearch", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(tech ObjectValue) into a free slot", reply = "slot Value; both slots full fires OnAllSlotsOccupied (gamepass prompt)" },
	TryStartFocus = { path = "Government/TryStartFocus", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(focus ObjectValue)", reply = "(ok, err)" },
	TryPurchaseShip = { path = "Economy/TryPurchaseShip", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(cityName string, shipType string)", reply = "falsy on failure" },
	TryExitPortWithFleet = { path = "Cities/Port/TryExitPortWithFleet", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(city Folder, {ship ObjectValue})", reply = "(true, seaTile) | (false, message)" },
	MoveFleet = { path = "Fleet/MoveFleet", class = "RemoteEvent", args = "(path: {tile}) fleet tile first, sea tiles, optional land goal", reply = "fleet attributes" },
	TryDeployPlanes = { path = "Planes/TryDeployPlanes", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(city Folder, AirMap region Part, planeData {6 keys})", reply = "(ok, _)" },
	TryMovePlanes = { path = "Planes/TryMovePlanes", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(source city Folder, destination city Folder, planeData)", reply = "(ok, err)" },
	TryRetreat = { path = "Planes/TryRetreat", class = "RemoteFunction", args = "(AirMap region Part)", reply = "truthy on success" },
}

-- State.
Remotes.sendCounts = {}

---The registry entry of a remote; unknown names raise (the whitelist is closed).
---@param remoteName string
---@return table
function Remotes.entry(remoteName)
	local entry = REGISTRY[remoteName]

	if entry == nil then
		return error(string.format("Remotes: '%s' is not on the whitelist", tostring(remoteName)))
	end

	return entry
end

---Resolve a whitelisted remote instance and check its class.
---@param remoteName string
---@return Instance
function Remotes.resolve(remoteName)
	local entry = Remotes.entry(remoteName)
	local remote = Resolver.requireDescendant(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"), entry.path)

	if remote.ClassName ~= entry.class then
		return error(string.format("Remotes: '%s' is a %s, the registry expects %s (game update?)", remote:GetFullName(), remote.ClassName, entry.class))
	end

	return remote
end

---Resolve every whitelisted remote once, so a renamed remote fails at attach, not mid-war.
function Remotes.verifyAll()
	local verified = {}

	for remoteName in pairs(REGISTRY) do
		Remotes.resolve(remoteName)
		table.insert(verified, remoteName)
	end

	table.sort(verified)

	Logger.info("Game.Remotes", "verified", { remotes = verified })
end

---Send a whitelisted remote. Events return nothing; functions return their results.
---@param remoteName string
---@return any
function Remotes.send(remoteName, ...)
	local entry = Remotes.entry(remoteName)
	local remote = Remotes.resolve(remoteName)

	Remotes.sendCounts[remoteName] = (Remotes.sendCounts[remoteName] or 0) + 1

	if entry.class == "RemoteEvent" then
		remote:FireServer(...)
		return nil
	end

	return remote:InvokeServer(...)
end

---The remote's OnClientEvent, for replies that come back on the same event.
---@param remoteName string
---@return RBXScriptSignal
function Remotes.replySignal(remoteName)
	local entry = Remotes.entry(remoteName)

	if entry.class ~= "RemoteEvent" then
		return error(string.format("Remotes.replySignal: '%s' is a %s and replies by return value", remoteName, entry.class))
	end

	return Remotes.resolve(remoteName).OnClientEvent
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.Remotes", Remotes)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Session"] = [=[
--[[
	Game/Session

	Identity of the running game and of the local player's country. Answers three questions the
	act-or-stop gate asks before anything is sent: is this Conquer The World WW2, is the client still
	replicating, and which country is ours right now. Announces a country change and a map
	replacement (new round) so every cache can rebuild.

	Our country is valid only while LocalPlayer.Country.Value is a child of ReplicatedStorage.Countries,
	is not dead, still names us as its player, and still owns tiles (REFERENCE R1: the loss order puts
	tileCount 0 first).
]]

local Session = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Signal
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

-- Constants.
-- T0: Conquer The World WW2's place id, read in-game while scouting (dump/data/session.json).
local CTW_PLACE_ID = 14422394494
local MAP_MODEL_NAME = "Map"
local MAP_ATTRIBUTE = "map"

-- Services.
local players = game:GetService("Players")
local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local networkClient = game:GetService("NetworkClient")

-- State.
Session.localPlayer = players.LocalPlayer
Session.countryChanged = Signal.new("Game.Session.countryChanged")
Session.mapReplaced = Signal.new("Game.Session.mapReplaced")
Session.lastCountryName = nil

---True when the client runs Conquer The World WW2 (read in-game; the bridge's place report is not trusted).
---@return boolean
function Session.isCorrectPlace()
	return game.PlaceId == CTW_PLACE_ID
end

---The game's country registry.
---@return Instance
function Session.countriesFolder()
	return Resolver.requireChild(replicatedStorage, "Countries")
end

---The current map Model (workspace.Map).
---@return Model
function Session.mapModel()
	local mapModel = Resolver.requireChild(workspace, MAP_MODEL_NAME)

	if not mapModel:IsA("Model") then
		return error(string.format("Session.mapModel: workspace.%s is a %s, expected Model", MAP_MODEL_NAME, mapModel.ClassName))
	end

	return mapModel
end

---The map name the round runs on, e.g. "EurasiaModern".
---@return string
function Session.mapName()
	return Resolver.requireAttribute(workspace, MAP_ATTRIBUTE, "string")
end

---True while the network replicator exists. A disconnected client (idle kick, error 278) loses it
---while GetServerTimeNow keeps running (war-and-loop.md §6).
---@return boolean
function Session.replicatorPresent()
	return networkClient:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClientReplicator") ~= nil
end

---Our country instance and, when there is none, the reason.
---@return Instance?, string?
function Session.ourCountry()
	local countryPointer = Resolver.optionalChild(Session.localPlayer, "Country", "the player has not joined a country yet")

	if countryPointer == nil then
		return nil, "LocalPlayer has no Country value"
	end

	local country = countryPointer.Value

	if country == nil then
		return nil, "no country selected (Country.Value is nil)"
	end

	if country.Parent ~= Session.countriesFolder() then
		return nil, string.format("Country.Value '%s' is not in ReplicatedStorage.Countries", country:GetFullName())
	end

	if country:GetAttribute("isDead") == true then
		return nil, string.format("%s is dead (annexed)", country.Name)
	end

	if country.Value ~= Session.localPlayer then
		return nil, string.format("%s is no longer controlled by the local player", country.Name)
	end

	local tileCount = Resolver.requireAttribute(country, "tileCount", "number")
	if tileCount <= 0 then
		return nil, string.format("%s owns no tiles (tileCount %s)", country.Name, tostring(tileCount))
	end

	return country, nil
end

---Connect the identity signals. Everything is owned by the caller's maid.
---@param maid table
function Session.attach(maid)
	local countryPointer = Resolver.optionalChild(Session.localPlayer, "Country", "the player has not joined a country yet")

	local function announceCountry(origin)
		local country, reason = Session.ourCountry()
		local countryName = country and country.Name or nil

		Logger.info("Game.Session", "country", {
			origin = origin,
			country = countryName,
			previous = Session.lastCountryName,
			reason = reason,
		})

		Session.lastCountryName = countryName
		Session.countryChanged:fire(country, reason)
	end

	if countryPointer ~= nil then
		maid:give(countryPointer.Changed:Connect(function()
			announceCountry("LocalPlayer.Country.Changed")
		end))
	else
		-- The Country value is created by the game when the player first joins a country.
		maid:give(Session.localPlayer.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child.Name ~= "Country" then
				return
			end

			maid:give(child.Changed:Connect(function()
				announceCountry("LocalPlayer.Country.Changed")
			end))

			announceCountry("LocalPlayer.Country added")
		end))
	end

	maid:give(workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == MAP_MODEL_NAME and child:IsA("Model") then
			Logger.info("Game.Session", "mapModelAdded", { map = workspace:GetAttribute(MAP_ATTRIBUTE) })
			Session.mapReplaced:fire("workspace.Map added")
		end
	end))

	maid:give(workspace:GetAttributeChangedSignal(MAP_ATTRIBUTE):Connect(function()
		Logger.info("Game.Session", "mapAttributeChanged", { map = workspace:GetAttribute(MAP_ATTRIBUTE) })
		Session.mapReplaced:fire("workspace map attribute changed")
	end))

	Logger.info("Game.Session", "attached", {
		placeId = game.PlaceId,
		correctPlace = Session.isCorrectPlace(),
		jobId = game.JobId,
		map = workspace:GetAttribute(MAP_ATTRIBUTE),
		replicator = Session.replicatorPresent(),
	})

	announceCountry("attach")
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.Session", Session)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Tick"] = [=[
--[[
	Game/Tick

	Detects the server's day tick and measures its period (derivation tier T2). The tick-pass supply
	stamp (tile attribute lastTimeSupplied) carries the server time of the pass as its value and is
	written on every supplied unit's tile of every country in the same pass (state-model.md §3.3,
	war-and-loop.md §3.8). Arrival copies written when a unit moves carry an older value, so a new
	tick is a stamp value that exceeds the last tick's value by more than half a day: two passes are
	one Utils.secondsPerDay apart plus server overhead, while stamps of one pass share its time.

	The measured period is the median of every interval seen this session; it replaces the recorded
	3.07 / 3.12 s wherever a decision needs the tick length.
]]

local Tick = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Signal
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

-- Constants.
local STAMP_ATTRIBUTE = "lastTimeSupplied"
-- T1: a pass writes one time value; passes are secondsPerDay apart, so half a day separates them.
local PASS_SEPARATION_FRACTION_OF_DAY = 0.5
-- T1: a stamp can arrive late by at most one period before the next pass is due (late ticks in
-- recording B arrived 1.5-3.2 s after the pass), so replication is stale after two periods.
local STALE_AFTER_PERIODS = 2
-- T5: the median of n sorted values sits at position (n + 1) / 2; an even n averages the two middles.
local MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR = 2

-- State.
Tick.dayTick = Signal.new("Game.Tick.dayTick")
Tick.lastPassServerTime = nil
Tick.lastPassClock = nil
Tick.passCount = 0
Tick.intervals = {}
Tick.sortedIntervals = {}
-- The median changes only when a pass adds an interval; every other caller reads this value
-- (fourth live run: the median and its whole input list were logged many times per cycle).
Tick.cachedPeriod = nil

Formula.define({
	id = "tick.periodSeconds",
	tier = "T2",
	source = "median interval between consecutive tick-pass stamp values measured this session (Game/Tick)",
	expression = "median(intervals)",
	unit = "s",
	inputs = { sortedIntervals = "array of s, ascending" },
	compute = function(inputs)
		local sorted = inputs.sortedIntervals
		local count = #sorted

		if count == 0 then
			return error("tick.periodSeconds: no interval measured yet")
		end

		local middle = (count + 1) / MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR
		local lower = sorted[math.floor(middle)]
		local upper = sorted[math.ceil(middle)]

		return (lower + upper) / MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR
	end,
	examples = {
		-- Recording A: consecutive supply stamps 3.056 .. 3.084 s apart, median 3.067 (war-and-loop.md §3.8).
		{ inputs = { sortedIntervals = { 3.056, 3.067, 3.084 } }, expected = 3.067, tolerance = 1e-9 },
		{ inputs = { sortedIntervals = { 3.098, 3.117, 3.117, 3.163 } }, expected = 3.117, tolerance = 1e-9 },
	},
})

---Insert a value into an ascending array.
---@param sorted table
---@param value number
local function insertSorted(sorted, value)
	local insertAt = #sorted + 1

	for index, existing in ipairs(sorted) do
		if value < existing then
			insertAt = index
			break
		end
	end

	table.insert(sorted, insertAt, value)
end

---Handle one lastTimeSupplied write. Hot path: called for every stamp of every unit, so it is a
---local function; the new-tick branch logs.
---@param tileName string
---@param stampValue any
local function onStamp(tileName, stampValue)
	if type(stampValue) ~= "number" then
		return
	end

	local lastPass = Tick.lastPassServerTime
	local separation = GameModules.get("Utils").secondsPerDay * PASS_SEPARATION_FRACTION_OF_DAY

	if lastPass ~= nil and stampValue <= lastPass + separation then
		return
	end

	local nowClock = os.clock()

	if lastPass ~= nil then
		local interval = stampValue - lastPass
		table.insert(Tick.intervals, interval)
		insertSorted(Tick.sortedIntervals, interval)
		Tick.cachedPeriod = Formula.evaluate("tick.periodSeconds", { sortedIntervals = Tick.sortedIntervals })
	end

	Tick.lastPassServerTime = stampValue
	Tick.lastPassClock = nowClock
	Tick.passCount += 1

	Logger.debug("Game.Tick", "dayTick", {
		pass = Tick.passCount,
		serverTime = stampValue,
		firstStampTile = tileName,
		arrivalLagSeconds = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() - stampValue,
		intervalSeconds = lastPass and (stampValue - lastPass) or nil,
		measuredPeriodSeconds = Tick.cachedPeriod,
	})

	Tick.dayTick:fire({
		pass = Tick.passCount,
		serverTime = stampValue,
	})
end

---Measured tick period, or nil before two passes were seen.
---@return number?
function Tick.periodSeconds()
	return Tick.cachedPeriod
end

---True when a pass arrived within the stale bound; before the period is measured, false.
---@return boolean, string
function Tick.isFresh()
	local period = Tick.periodSeconds()

	if period == nil or Tick.lastPassClock == nil then
		return false, string.format("tick period not measured yet (%d passes seen)", Tick.passCount)
	end

	local sinceLast = os.clock() - Tick.lastPassClock
	local bound = period * STALE_AFTER_PERIODS

	if sinceLast > bound then
		return false, string.format("no supply pass for %.2f s (bound %.2f s = %d periods of %.3f s)", sinceLast, bound, STALE_AFTER_PERIODS, period)
	end

	return true, string.format("last pass %.2f s ago", sinceLast)
end

---Route tile attribute changes into the detector.
---@param mapGraph table Game/MapGraph with its tileAttributeChanged signal
---@param maid table
function Tick.attach(mapGraph, maid)
	maid:give(mapGraph.tileAttributeChanged:connect(function(tileName, attributeName, value)
		if attributeName == STAMP_ATTRIBUTE then
			onStamp(tileName, value)
		end
	end))

	Logger.info("Game.Tick", "attached", { attribute = STAMP_ATTRIBUTE })
end

---Forget every measurement (new map or round).
---@param reason string
function Tick.reset(reason)
	Logger.info("Game.Tick", "reset", { reason = reason, passes = Tick.passCount, intervals = #Tick.intervals })

	Tick.lastPassServerTime = nil
	Tick.lastPassClock = nil
	Tick.passCount = 0
	Tick.cachedPeriod = nil
	table.clear(Tick.intervals)
	table.clear(Tick.sortedIntervals)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Game.Tick", Tick)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Game/Units"] = [=[
--[[
	Game/Units

	Every land troop on the map, kept current from the game's own signals:
	- a troop appears as a Model under a land tile (workspace.Map.DescendantAdded crosses the map
	  boundary for spawns, landings and upgrades);
	- it moves by being re-parented from tile to tile (its Parent property changes; a move stays
	  inside the map, so DescendantAdded does not fire for it);
	- it disappears when its Parent leaves the map (death, annexation, embarkation).

	A unit keeps one id across moves. An upgrade adds the new Model to the same tile about 1 ms before
	it destroys the old one (state-model.md §3.5); the new Model inherits the old id, so records keyed
	by id (our NoRetreat record) survive the swap.

	Our NoRetreat record (DESIGN §3.2, war-and-loop critic G2): when one of our units enters
	Retreating, it is flagged in our record; the record clears when the live dieOnNextRetreat flag
	turns false or when its health reaches the highest health at which the server was ever seen
	clearing the flag, because an upgraded Model shows no flag although the server may still hold it.
]]

local Units = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Utility.Signal
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

-- Constants.
-- T2 (recordings A/B, war-and-loop.md §3.14): lowest health at which a NoRetreat flag was seen
-- cleared on a heal tick; the server's threshold T satisfies 38.17 < T <= 41.652.
local FLAG_CLEAR_UPPER_BOUND = 41.652
local DEAD_STATE = "Dead"
local RETREATING_STATE = "Retreating"
-- T3: troop class = troopName without digits (TroopCommon.GetTroopClass).
local DIGITS_PATTERN = "%d"
local LEVEL_PATTERN = "%d+"

-- State.
Units.mapModel = nil
Units.records = {}
Units.recordByModel = {}
Units.tileRecords = {}
Units.nextId = 0
Units.flagRecords = {}
Units.ourCountryName = nil
Units.unitAdded = Signal.new("Game.Units.unitAdded")
Units.unitMoved = Signal.new("Game.Units.unitMoved")
Units.unitRemoved = Signal.new("Game.Units.unitRemoved")
Units.unitChanged = Signal.new("Game.Units.unitChanged")

---Class and level from a troop name ("Tanks3" -> "Tanks", 3).
---@param troopName string
---@return string, number
local function classAndLevel(troopName)
	local troopClass = string.gsub(troopName, DIGITS_PATTERN, "")
	local levelText = string.match(troopName, LEVEL_PATTERN)

	if levelText == nil then
		return error(string.format("Units: troop name '%s' carries no level digits", troopName))
	end

	return troopClass, tonumber(levelText)
end

---Tile list of records for a tile name.
---@param tileName string
---@return table
local function tileList(tileName)
	local list = Units.tileRecords[tileName]

	if list == nil then
		list = {}
		Units.tileRecords[tileName] = list
	end

	return list
end

---Remove a record from its tile list.
---@param record table
local function detachFromTile(record)
	local list = Units.tileRecords[record.tile]

	if list == nil then
		return
	end

	local index = table.find(list, record)
	if index ~= nil then
		table.remove(list, index)
	end

	if #list == 0 then
		Units.tileRecords[record.tile] = nil
	end
end

---True when a Model sits directly on a land tile of the tracked map.
---@param model Instance
---@return boolean
local function isTroopModel(model)
	if not model:IsA("Model") then
		return false
	end

	local parent = model.Parent

	return parent ~= nil and parent.Parent == Units.mapModel and MapGraph.isLand(parent.Name)
end

---Update our NoRetreat record from one attribute change of one of our units.
---@param record table
---@param attributeName string
---@param value any
local function updateFlagRecord(record, attributeName, value)
	if record.owner ~= Units.ourCountryName then
		return
	end

	local flag = Units.flagRecords[record.id]

	if attributeName == "state" and value == RETREATING_STATE and flag == nil then
		Units.flagRecords[record.id] = { sinceServerTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(), tile = record.tile }
		Logger.info("Game.Units", "flagRecorded", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, troop = record.troopName })
		return
	end

	if attributeName == "dieOnNextRetreat" and value == true and flag == nil then
		Units.flagRecords[record.id] = { sinceServerTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(), tile = record.tile }
		Logger.info("Game.Units", "flagRecorded", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, troop = record.troopName, origin = "live attribute" })
		return
	end

	if flag == nil then
		return
	end

	if attributeName == "dieOnNextRetreat" and value == false then
		Units.flagRecords[record.id] = nil
		Logger.info("Game.Units", "flagCleared", { unit = record.id, tile = record.tile, origin = "live attribute false" })
		return
	end

	if attributeName == "health" and type(value) == "number" and value >= FLAG_CLEAR_UPPER_BOUND then
		Units.flagRecords[record.id] = nil
		Logger.info("Game.Units", "flagCleared", {
			unit = record.id,
			tile = record.tile,
			health = value,
			bound = FLAG_CLEAR_UPPER_BOUND,
			origin = "health reached the recorded clear bound",
		})
	end
end

---Start tracking a troop Model found on a land tile.
---@param model Model
---@param maid table
---@param origin string
local function track(model, maid, origin)
	if Units.recordByModel[model] ~= nil then
		return
	end

	local tileName = model.Parent.Name
	local troopName = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "troopName", "string")
	local troopClass, level = classAndLevel(troopName)
	local owner = Ownership.troopOwner(model)

	-- An upgrade or unload adds the new Model while the old one of the same country is still there.
	local predecessor = nil
	for _, existing in ipairs(tileList(tileName)) do
		if existing.owner == owner and not existing.superseded then
			predecessor = existing
		end
	end

	local unitId
	if predecessor ~= nil then
		unitId = predecessor.id
		predecessor.superseded = true
	else
		Units.nextId += 1
		unitId = Units.nextId
	end

	local record = {
		id = unitId,
		model = model,
		tile = tileName,
		owner = owner,
		troopName = troopName,
		class = troopClass,
		level = level,
		superseded = false,
		addedServerTime = workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),
	}

	Units.records[record] = true
	Units.recordByModel[model] = record
	table.insert(tileList(tileName), record)

	local connections = {}

	table.insert(connections, model.AttributeChanged:Connect(function(attributeName)
		local value = model:GetAttribute(attributeName)
		updateFlagRecord(record, attributeName, value)
		Units.unitChanged:fire(record, attributeName, value)
	end))

	table.insert(connections, model:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Parent"):Connect(function()
		local parent = model.Parent

		if parent ~= nil and parent.Parent == Units.mapModel and MapGraph.isLand(parent.Name) then
			local fromTile = record.tile
			detachFromTile(record)
			record.tile = parent.Name
			table.insert(tileList(parent.Name), record)
			Units.unitMoved:fire(record, fromTile, parent.Name)
			return
		end

		for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do
			connection:Disconnect()
		end

		detachFromTile(record)
		Units.records[record] = nil
		Units.recordByModel[model] = nil

		local reason = parent == nil and "destroyed" or ("left the map for " .. parent:GetFullName())

		if not record.superseded then
			Units.flagRecords[record.id] = nil
		end

		Units.unitRemoved:fire(record, record.tile, reason)
	end))

	for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do
		maid:give(connection)
	end

	if predecessor ~= nil then
		Logger.debug("Game.Units", "unitReplaced", { unit = unitId, tile = tileName, from = predecessor.troopName, to = troopName })
	end

	Units.unitAdded:fire(record, origin)
end

---Forget every tracked unit (the map-scoped maid that owned their connections was cleaned).
---@param reason string
function Units.reset(reason)
	local tracked = 0
	for _ in pairs(Units.records) do
		tracked += 1
	end

	table.clear(Units.records)
	table.clear(Units.recordByModel)
	table.clear(Units.tileRecords)
	table.clear(Units.flagRecords)
	Units.mapModel = nil

	Logger.info("Game.Units", "reset", { reason = reason, forgotten = tracked })
end

---Scan the map and subscribe to new troop Models. The connections belong to the map-scoped maid;
---the module's signals outlive map rebuilds and are owned by the orchestrator.
---@param mapModel Model
---@param ourCountryName string?
---@param maid table
function Units.attach(mapModel, ourCountryName, maid)
	Units.mapModel = mapModel
	Units.ourCountryName = ourCountryName

	local tracked = 0
	for _, tileName in ipairs(MapGraph.tileOrder) do
		local tileRecord = MapGraph.tiles[tileName]

		if not tileRecord.isSea then
			for _, child in ipairs(tileRecord.instance:GetChildren()) do
				if isTroopModel(child) then
					track(child, maid, "initial scan")
					tracked += 1
				end
			end
		end
	end

	maid:give(mapModel.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
		if isTroopModel(descendant) then
			track(descendant, maid, "DescendantAdded")
		end
	end))

	Logger.info("Game.Units", "attached", { tracked = tracked, ourCountry = ourCountryName })
end

---Set which country is ours (flag records are kept for our units only).
---@param countryName string?
function Units.setOurCountry(countryName)
	if Units.ourCountryName ~= countryName then
		table.clear(Units.flagRecords)
	end

	Units.ourCountryName = countryName
end

---The live, non-superseded, non-dead unit on a tile, or nil.
---@param tileName string
---@return table?
function Units.unitOn(tileName)
	return Units.liveUnitOn(tileName)
end

---Every tracked unit of one country.
---@param countryName string
---@return table array of records
function Units.unitsOf(countryName)
	local result = {}

	for record in pairs(Units.records) do
		if record.owner == countryName and not record.superseded and record.model:GetAttribute("state") ~= DEAD_STATE then
			table.insert(result, record)
		end
	end

	table.sort(result, function(first, second)
		return first.id < second.id
	end)

	return result
end

---True when our NoRetreat record or the live attribute flags the unit.
---@param record table
---@return boolean
function Units.isFlagged(record)
	return Units.flaggedNow(record)
end

---Plain snapshot of a unit's attributes for one planning cycle. Required attributes raise when
---missing; attributes the game sets only in some states are optional with their reason.
---@param record table
---@return table
function Units.snapshot(record)
	return Units.snapshotNow(record)
end

---Counts for status reports.
---@return table
function Units.stats()
	local total = 0
	local byOwner = {}

	for record in pairs(Units.records) do
		if not record.superseded then
			total += 1
			byOwner[record.owner or "?"] = (byOwner[record.owner or "?"] or 0) + 1
		end
	end

	local flagged = 0
	for _ in pairs(Units.flagRecords) do
		flagged += 1
	end

	return { total = total, byOwner = byOwner, ourFlagRecords = flagged }
end

Tracer.instrument("Game.Units", Units)

-- Hot-path twins used by the planners' inner loops (the traced functions above delegate here).

---@param tileName string
---@return table?
function Units.liveUnitOn(tileName)
	local list = Units.tileRecords[tileName]

	if list == nil then
		return nil
	end

	for index = #list, 1, -1 do
		local record = list[index]

		if not record.superseded and record.model.Parent ~= nil and record.model:GetAttribute("state") ~= DEAD_STATE then
			return record
		end
	end

	return nil
end

---@param record table
---@return boolean
function Units.flaggedNow(record)
	return Units.flagRecords[record.id] ~= nil or record.model:GetAttribute("dieOnNextRetreat") == true
end

---@param record table
---@return table
function Units.snapshotNow(record)
	local model = record.model

	return {
		id = record.id,
		tile = record.tile,
		owner = record.owner,
		troopName = record.troopName,
		class = record.class,
		level = record.level,
		model = model,
		state = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "state", "string"),
		health = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "health", "number"),
		attack = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "attack", "number"),
		defense = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "defense", "number"),
		speed = Resolver.requireAttribute(model, "speed", "number"),
		experience = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "experience", "number", "absent on a fresh Model before its first attack") or 0,
		targetTile = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "targetTile", "string", "set only while moving, waiting, attacking or retreating"),
		currentPath = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "currentPath", "string", "set only while an order has tiles left"),
		attackerCount = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "attackerCount", "number", "set only once the unit has been attacked"),
		noSupply = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "noSupply", "boolean", "written from the first supply pass on") == true,
		noMoney = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "noMoney", "boolean", "written from the first upkeep pass on") == true,
		liveFlag = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "dieOnNextRetreat", "boolean", "written from the first retreat on") == true,
		flagged = Units.flaggedNow(record),
		replenishConsumption = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "replenishConsumption", "number", "TroopCommon reads it with a default of 1"),
		replenishSuppliesName = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "replenishSuppliesName", "string", "set by the server with the base stats"),
		army = Resolver.optionalAttribute(model, "army", "string", "set only for members of an army group"),
	}
end

return Units

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["GUI/Linoria"] = [=[
--[[
	GUI/Linoria

	Loads LinoriaLib (Library, ThemeManager, SaveManager) from the repository the user always uses.
	A failed download or compile raises with the exact URL, so a moved repository is a readable
	error instead of a missing menu.
]]

local Linoria = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

-- Constants.
-- T5: the user's standing UI library choice (github.com/violin-suzutsuki/LinoriaLib, main branch);
-- runtime download approved by the user on 2026-09-25.
local REPOSITORY_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/violin-suzutsuki/LinoriaLib/main/"
local LIBRARY_FILES = {
	library = "Library.lua",
	themeManager = "addons/ThemeManager.lua",
	saveManager = "addons/SaveManager.lua",
}

---Download one library file, compile it, and run it.
---@param relativePath string
---@return any
function Linoria.fetch(relativePath)
	local url = REPOSITORY_URL .. relativePath
	local fetchOk, body = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, url)

	if not fetchOk then
		return error(string.format("Linoria.fetch: download of '%s' failed: %s", url, tostring(body)))
	end

	local chunk, compileError = loadstring(body, "=LinoriaLib/" .. relativePath)

	if chunk == nil then
		return error(string.format("Linoria.fetch: '%s' failed to compile: %s", url, tostring(compileError)))
	end

	local libraryValue = chunk()

	if libraryValue == nil then
		return error(string.format("Linoria.fetch: '%s' returned nothing", url))
	end

	Logger.info("GUI.Linoria", "loaded", { url = url, bytes = #body })

	return libraryValue
end

---Load the three LinoriaLib parts.
---@return table { library, themeManager, saveManager }
function Linoria.load()
	local libraries = {}

	for partName, relativePath in pairs(LIBRARY_FILES) do
		libraries[partName] = Linoria.fetch(relativePath)
	end

	return libraries
end

return Tracer.instrument("GUI.Linoria", Linoria)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Main"] = [=[
--[[
	Main

	Entry point. Detaches any previous copy, publishes the new orchestrator on shared, and attaches
	it under a profiled xpcall. A failure before the session log exists is written to
	CTW_WarAI/logs/boot_error.json so it is never lost.
]]

-- Check for table that is shared between executions.
if not shared then
	return error("CTW_WarAI: the executor provides no shared table")
end

---@module WarAI
local WarAI = require("WarAI")

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Profiler
local Profiler = require("Utility/Profiler")

---@module Utility.Serializer
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

-- Constants.
local BOOT_ERROR_PATH = "CTW_WarAI/logs/boot_error.json"
-- T5: debug.traceback level of the function that raised, seen from inside the xpcall handler.
local RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL = 2

---This is called when initialization errors.
---@param failure any
---@return any
local function onInitializeError(failure)
	local stackText = debug.traceback(tostring(failure), RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL)

	Logger.error("Main", "initializeFailed", { message = tostring(failure) }, stackText)
	Logger.flush()

	-- If no sink was attached yet, the record above only lives in memory; write it out directly.
	if #Logger.sinks == 0 then
		writefile(BOOT_ERROR_PATH, Serializer.encode({
			message = tostring(failure),
			traceback = stackText,
			pendingRecords = Logger.pendingRecords,
		}))
	end

	shared.CTW_BootError = { message = tostring(failure), traceback = stackText }

	WarAI.detach()

	return failure
end

---Initialize the script.
local function initializeScript()
	-- Detach the old instance before replacing shared state.
	if shared.CTW_WarAI then
		shared.CTW_WarAI.detach()
	end

	shared.CTW_BootError = nil

	-- Publish and initialize the new instance.
	shared.CTW_WarAI = WarAI
	WarAI.attach()
end

-- Safely profile initialization and report failures.
Profiler.run("Main_InitializeScript", function(...)
	return xpcall(initializeScript, onInitializeError, ...)
end)

return WarAI

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Menu"] = [=[
--[[
	Menu

	The LinoriaLib window. Every control is generated from the settings registry, so the menu can
	only offer settings that declare their unit, range, default provenance and effect. Status lines
	are pushed in by the orchestrator after each state change; the menu never polls.
]]

local Menu = {}

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

-- Constants.
local WINDOW_TITLE = "CTW War AI"
-- Workspace folder for LinoriaLib themes and saved configs, inside this project's folder.
local MENU_FOLDER = "CTW_WarAI/menu"
local STATUS_TAB_NAME = "Status"
local UI_SETTINGS_TAB_NAME = "UI Settings"
-- T5: LinoriaLib's documented example keybind for toggling the menu.
local DEFAULT_MENU_KEY = "End"

-- T5: debug.traceback level of the function that raised, seen from inside an xpcall handler.
local RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL = 2

-- Services.
local runService = game:GetService("RunService")

-- State.
Menu.library = nil
Menu.statusLabels = {}
Menu.pendingStatus = {}
Menu.unloading = false

---Tooltip text that states what a control changes, its unit, and where its default came from.
---@param definition table
---@return string
local function tooltipFor(definition)
	return string.format("%s\nUnit: %s\nDefault: %s (%s)", definition.effect, definition.unit, tostring(definition.default), definition.defaultSource)
end

---Add one generated control to a groupbox.
---@param groupbox table
---@param definition table
function Menu.addControl(groupbox, definition)
	local function onValue(value)
		Settings.set(definition.key, value, "menu")
	end

	if definition.kind == "toggle" then
		groupbox:AddToggle(definition.key, {
			Text = definition.label,
			Default = definition.default,
			Tooltip = tooltipFor(definition),
			Callback = onValue,
		})
		return
	end

	if definition.kind == "slider" then
		groupbox:AddSlider(definition.key, {
			Text = string.format("%s (%s)", definition.label, definition.unit),
			Default = definition.default,
			Min = definition.min,
			Max = definition.max,
			Rounding = definition.rounding,
			Compact = false,
			Tooltip = tooltipFor(definition),
			Callback = onValue,
		})
		return
	end

	if definition.kind == "dropdown" then
		groupbox:AddDropdown(definition.key, {
			Text = definition.label,
			Values = definition.choices,
			Default = definition.default,
			Multi = false,
			Tooltip = tooltipFor(definition),
			Callback = onValue,
		})
		return
	end

	return error(string.format("Menu.addControl: no builder for kind '%s' (%s)", tostring(definition.kind), definition.key))
end

---Build the window from the settings registry plus the status and UI-settings tabs.
---@param libraries table { library, themeManager, saveManager }
---@param statusKeys table ordered status line keys, e.g. { "state", "country" }
---@param onUnload function called when the user unloads the menu
function Menu.build(libraries, statusKeys, onUnload)
	local library = libraries.library
	Menu.library = library

	local window = library:CreateWindow({
		Title = WINDOW_TITLE,
		Center = true,
		AutoShow = true,
	})

	-- Status first, so the AI's state is the first thing the user sees.
	local statusTab = window:AddTab(STATUS_TAB_NAME)
	local statusGroupbox = statusTab:AddLeftGroupbox("AI state")

	for _, statusKey in ipairs(statusKeys) do
		Menu.statusLabels[statusKey] = statusGroupbox:AddLabel(statusKey .. ": -", true)
	end

	local generatedControls = 0
	for _, tabLayout in ipairs(Settings.layout()) do
		local tab = window:AddTab(tabLayout.name)

		-- Alternate columns so sections fill the window evenly.
		local useLeftColumn = true

		for _, section in ipairs(tabLayout.sections) do
			local groupbox
			if useLeftColumn then
				groupbox = tab:AddLeftGroupbox(section.name)
			else
				groupbox = tab:AddRightGroupbox(section.name)
			end

			useLeftColumn = not useLeftColumn

			for _, definition in ipairs(section.definitions) do
				Menu.addControl(groupbox, definition)
				generatedControls += 1
			end
		end
	end

	local uiTab = window:AddTab(UI_SETTINGS_TAB_NAME)
	local menuGroupbox = uiTab:AddLeftGroupbox("Menu")

	menuGroupbox:AddButton({
		Text = "Unload CTW War AI",
		Func = onUnload,
		DoubleClick = true,
		Tooltip = "Stops the AI, disconnects everything it owns, and closes this menu.",
	})

	menuGroupbox:AddLabel("Menu bind"):AddKeyPicker("MenuKeybind", {
		Default = DEFAULT_MENU_KEY,
		NoUI = true,
		Text = "Menu keybind",
	})

	library.ToggleKeybind = getgenv().Options.MenuKeybind

	local themeManager = libraries.themeManager
	local saveManager = libraries.saveManager

	themeManager:SetLibrary(library)
	saveManager:SetLibrary(library)
	saveManager:IgnoreThemeSettings()
	saveManager:SetIgnoreIndexes({ "MenuKeybind" })
	themeManager:SetFolder(MENU_FOLDER)
	saveManager:SetFolder(MENU_FOLDER)
	saveManager:BuildConfigSection(uiTab)
	themeManager:ApplyToTab(uiTab)

	-- Unloading from the library (its own button or keybind path) must also stop the AI.
	library:OnUnload(function()
		if Menu.unloading then
			return
		end

		Menu.unloading = true
		onUnload()
	end)

	Logger.info("Menu", "built", {
		generatedControls = generatedControls,
		statusLines = #statusKeys,
	})
end

---Queue a new text for one status line. Planning cycles run on threads started by game signals,
---which may not write to the menu's GUI (first live run: "lacking capability Plugin"); the pump
---thread started from the script's own thread applies the texts once per frame.
---@param statusKey string
---@param text string
function Menu.setStatus(statusKey, text)
	if Menu.statusLabels[statusKey] == nil then
		return error(string.format("Menu.setStatus: unknown status line '%s'", tostring(statusKey)))
	end

	Menu.pendingStatus[statusKey] = text
end

---Apply every queued status text (runs on the pump thread).
function Menu.applyPending()
	for statusKey, text in pairs(Menu.pendingStatus) do
		local label = Menu.statusLabels[statusKey]

		if label ~= nil then
			label:SetText(statusKey .. ": " .. text)
		end
	end

	table.clear(Menu.pendingStatus)
end

---Start the pump: a thread created by the caller (the script's own thread, which may write to the
---GUI) that applies queued status texts once per frame until the menu is destroyed. A failure is
---logged once with its traceback and stops the pump, so it cannot flood the log every frame.
---@return thread
function Menu.startPump()
	return task.spawn(function()
		while Menu.library ~= nil do
			runService.Heartbeat:Wait()

			local pumpOk = xpcall(Menu.applyPending, function(failure)
				Logger.error("Menu", "statusPumpFailed", { message = tostring(failure) }, debug.traceback(tostring(failure), RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL))
				return failure
			end)

			if not pumpOk then
				break
			end
		end
	end)
end

---Close the window if it is still open.
function Menu.destroy()
	if Menu.library == nil then
		return
	end

	if not Menu.unloading then
		Menu.unloading = true
		Menu.library:Unload()
	end

	Menu.library = nil
	table.clear(Menu.statusLabels)
	table.clear(Menu.pendingStatus)
end

return Tracer.instrument("Menu", Menu)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/FileSink"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/FileSink

	A Logger sink that writes one JSON object per line to a file in the executor workspace.
	Records are encoded when pushed and appended on flush; the owner flushes once per frame and
	Logger flushes immediately on ERROR.
]]

local FileSink = {}
FileSink.__index = FileSink

---@module Utility.Serializer
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

---Create a sink that owns one file.
---@param filePath string path relative to the executor workspace
---@return table
function FileSink.new(filePath)
	-- Potassium's appendfile raises "file does not exist" on a missing file (verified 2026-09-25),
	-- so the file is created before the first append.
	writefile(filePath, "")

	if not isfile(filePath) then
		return error(string.format("FileSink.new: file '%s' could not be created", filePath))
	end

	local self = setmetatable({}, FileSink)

	self.filePath = filePath
	self.buffer = {}
	self.recordsWritten = 0
	self.bytesWritten = 0
	self.flushCount = 0

	return self
end

---Encode and buffer one record.
---@param record table
function FileSink:push(record)
	table.insert(self.buffer, Serializer.encode(record))
end

---Append every buffered record to the file.
function FileSink:flush()
	if #self.buffer == 0 then
		return
	end

	local chunk = table.concat(self.buffer, "\n") .. "\n"

	appendfile(self.filePath, chunk)

	self.recordsWritten += #self.buffer
	self.bytesWritten += #chunk
	self.flushCount += 1

	table.clear(self.buffer)
end

---Report what this sink has written.
---@return table
function FileSink:stats()
	return {
		filePath = self.filePath,
		recordsWritten = self.recordsWritten,
		bytesWritten = self.bytesWritten,
		flushCount = self.flushCount,
		buffered = #self.buffer,
	}
end

return FileSink

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Formula"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Formula

	The formula registry. Every expression that produces a number used in a decision is declared
	here with its derivation tier, input source, symbolic expression, input units, output unit and
	worked examples. Examples run as assertions when the formula is defined, so a drifted formula
	stops the script at load. Every evaluation logs its inputs, output and provenance; every
	selection between candidates logs the winner, the losers and the deciding fact.
]]

local Formula = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

-- Constants.
-- Derivation tiers from the style guide's ladder; T6 (no provenance) is never accepted.
local ALLOWED_TIERS = { T0 = true, T1 = true, T2 = true, T3 = true, T4 = true, T5 = true }

-- State.
Formula.definitions = {}
-- When false, the first evaluation of each formula inside one trace (one planning cycle) is logged
-- in full and later ones are counted; Formula.flushSuppressed reports the counts, so no record is
-- dropped silently. The Debug setting "formula.logEveryEvaluation" turns full logging on.
Formula.logEveryEvaluation = false
Formula.loggedByTrace = {}
Formula.suppressedByTrace = {}

---Declare a formula with provenance, units, and worked examples that run immediately.
---@param definition table { id, tier, source, expression, unit, inputs = { name = unit }, compute, examples }
---@return table
function Formula.define(definition)
	assert(definition.id, "Formula.define: id is required")
	assert(ALLOWED_TIERS[definition.tier], string.format("Formula.define: '%s' has no allowed derivation tier", tostring(definition.id)))
	assert(definition.source, "Formula.define: source of the inputs is required")
	assert(definition.expression, "Formula.define: symbolic expression is required")
	assert(definition.unit, "Formula.define: output unit is required")
	assert(definition.inputs, "Formula.define: input units are required")
	assert(definition.compute, "Formula.define: compute function is required")
	assert(definition.examples and #definition.examples > 0, "Formula.define: at least one worked example is required")

	if Formula.definitions[definition.id] then
		return error(string.format("Formula.define: '%s' is already defined", definition.id))
	end

	Formula.definitions[definition.id] = definition

	-- Run every worked example at load so a drifted formula fails immediately and visibly.
	for exampleIndex, example in ipairs(definition.examples) do
		local produced = definition.compute(example.inputs)
		local passed

		if type(example.expected) == "number" then
			local tolerance = example.tolerance
			if tolerance == nil then
				tolerance = 0
			end

			-- Exact equality first, so an infinite expectation (a defender already beaten) can pass.
			passed = type(produced) == "number" and (produced == example.expected or math.abs(produced - example.expected) <= tolerance)
		else
			passed = produced == example.expected
		end

		Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "example", {
			id = definition.id,
			expression = definition.expression,
			exampleIndex = exampleIndex,
			inputs = example.inputs,
			expected = example.expected,
			produced = produced,
			unit = definition.unit,
			passed = passed,
		})

		if not passed then
			return error(string.format(
				"Formula '%s' example %d drifted: expected %s, produced %s (%s)",
				definition.id,
				exampleIndex,
				tostring(example.expected),
				tostring(produced),
				definition.unit
			))
		end
	end

	return definition
end

---Report how many evaluations of each formula were counted instead of logged in one trace, and
---forget that trace. Called at the end of every planning cycle.
---@param traceId string
function Formula.flushSuppressed(traceId)
	local suppressed = Formula.suppressedByTrace[traceId]

	if suppressed ~= nil then
		Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "suppressedEvaluations", {
			traceId = traceId,
			counts = suppressed,
			reason = "first evaluation per formula and trace is logged in full; enable formula.logEveryEvaluation to log all",
		})
	end

	Formula.suppressedByTrace[traceId] = nil
	Formula.loggedByTrace[traceId] = nil
end

---Describe the candidates that lost a selection.
---@param candidates table
---@param winner table?
---@param context table
---@return table
function Formula.describeRejected(candidates, winner, context)
	local rejected = {}

	for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
		if candidate ~= winner then
			table.insert(rejected, {
				id = candidate.id,
				reason = candidate.rejectReason and candidate.rejectReason(context) or "applies() returned false or ranked lower",
			})
		end
	end

	return rejected
end

---Select the first candidate that applies to a context and record the decision.
---No applicable candidate is a game change, not a normal path, so it raises.
---@param decisionName string
---@param candidates table array of { id, reason, applies(context), rejectReason?(context) }
---@param context table
---@return table
function Formula.select(decisionName, candidates, context)
	for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
		if candidate.applies(context) then
			Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "branch", {
				decision = decisionName,
				chosen = candidate.id,
				reason = candidate.reason,
				rejected = Formula.describeRejected(candidates, candidate, context),
				context = context,
			})

			return candidate
		end
	end

	return error(string.format("Formula.select: no candidate applies for '%s'", decisionName))
end

---Record a ranked choice: the winner, every loser with its score, and the deciding fact.
---Used where options are scored by declared formulas rather than filtered by applies().
---@param decisionName string
---@param ranked table array of { id, score, facts } sorted best first
---@param decidingFact string
function Formula.recordChoice(decisionName, ranked, decidingFact)
	if #ranked == 0 then
		return error(string.format("Formula.recordChoice: '%s' has no candidates", decisionName))
	end

	-- Every entry after the winner (index 1) lost.
	local losers = {}
	for index, entry in ipairs(ranked) do
		if index > 1 then
			table.insert(losers, { id = entry.id, score = entry.score, facts = entry.facts })
		end
	end

	Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "branch", {
		decision = decisionName,
		chosen = ranked[1].id,
		chosenScore = ranked[1].score,
		chosenFacts = ranked[1].facts,
		rejected = losers,
		reason = decidingFact,
	})
end

Tracer.instrument("Utility.Formula", Formula)

-- Hot path, installed after instrumentation: Formula.evaluate writes its own formula record (the
-- first evaluation per formula and cycle in full, the rest counted), so a trace record per call
-- would only repeat it.

---Evaluate a declared formula and log its inputs, output, and provenance.
---Undeclared or missing inputs are rejected so an unknown knob cannot enter through the back door.
---@param id string
---@param inputs table
---@return any
function Formula.evaluate(id, inputs)
	local definition = Formula.definitions[id]

	if not definition then
		return error(string.format("Formula.evaluate: unknown formula '%s'", id))
	end

	for inputName in pairs(inputs) do
		if not definition.inputs[inputName] then
			return error(string.format("Formula.evaluate: '%s' received undeclared input '%s'", id, inputName))
		end
	end

	for inputName in pairs(definition.inputs) do
		if inputs[inputName] == nil then
			return error(string.format("Formula.evaluate: '%s' is missing input '%s'", id, inputName))
		end
	end

	local value = definition.compute(inputs)

	if Logger.accepts("DEBUG") then
		local traceId = "none"
		if Logger.contextProvider then
			traceId = Logger.contextProvider()
		end

		local logged = Formula.loggedByTrace[traceId]
		if logged == nil then
			logged = {}
			Formula.loggedByTrace[traceId] = logged
		end

		if Formula.logEveryEvaluation or not logged[id] then
			logged[id] = true

			Logger.debug("Utility.Formula", "formula", {
				id = id,
				expression = definition.expression,
				tier = definition.tier,
				source = definition.source,
				inputs = inputs,
				inputUnits = definition.inputs,
				output = value,
				unit = definition.unit,
			})
		else
			local suppressed = Formula.suppressedByTrace[traceId]
			if suppressed == nil then
				suppressed = {}
				Formula.suppressedByTrace[traceId] = suppressed
			end

			suppressed[id] = (suppressed[id] or 0) + 1
		end
	end

	return value
end

return Formula


]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Logger"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Logger

	Structured logging. Every record has the shape from the style guide (timestamp, level, scope,
	event, traceId, depth, source, payload, traceback). Producers never format; sinks do.
	Records produced before the first sink is attached are held and replayed to it, so nothing
	logged during boot is lost.

	Logger and Tracer are the instrumentation layer itself, so they are not passed through
	Tracer.instrument (that would recurse); every other public module is.
]]

local Logger = {}

-- Constants.
-- Level weights, lowest to highest (style guide 7.2).
local LEVEL_WEIGHTS = { TRACE = 1, DEBUG = 2, INFO = 3, WARN = 4, ERROR = 5 }
-- T5: debug.info stack level of the code that logged: 1 = Logger.write, 2 = Logger.<level>, 3 = its caller.
local LOG_CALLER_STACK_LEVEL = 3
-- T5: debug.traceback level of the function that called Logger.error.
local ERROR_CALLER_STACK_LEVEL = 2

-- The selectable levels, lowest to highest, for the settings UI.
Logger.LEVELS = { "TRACE", "DEBUG", "INFO", "WARN", "ERROR" }

-- State.
Logger.activeLevel = "DEBUG"
Logger.sessionStartClock = os.clock()
Logger.counts = { TRACE = 0, DEBUG = 0, INFO = 0, WARN = 0, ERROR = 0 }
Logger.lastByLevel = {}
Logger.sinks = {}
Logger.pendingRecords = {}
-- Set by Tracer at load so records carry the current trace id and depth without a circular require.
Logger.contextProvider = nil

---Report whether a level would be kept, so callers can skip building payloads.
---@param level string
---@return boolean
function Logger.accepts(level)
	return LEVEL_WEIGHTS[level] >= LEVEL_WEIGHTS[Logger.activeLevel]
end

---Change the active level. Unknown levels are rejected loudly.
---@param level string one of Logger.LEVELS
function Logger.setLevel(level)
	if LEVEL_WEIGHTS[level] == nil then
		return error(string.format("Logger.setLevel: unknown level '%s'", tostring(level)))
	end

	local previousLevel = Logger.activeLevel
	Logger.activeLevel = level

	Logger.info("Utility.Logger", "levelChanged", { previous = previousLevel, current = level })
end

---Attach a sink. Records produced before the first sink existed are replayed to it.
---@param sink table object with push(record) and flush()
function Logger.addSink(sink)
	table.insert(Logger.sinks, sink)

	if #Logger.pendingRecords == 0 then
		return
	end

	for _, pendingRecord in ipairs(Logger.pendingRecords) do
		sink:push(pendingRecord)
	end

	local replayedCount = #Logger.pendingRecords
	table.clear(Logger.pendingRecords)

	Logger.info("Utility.Logger", "pendingReplayed", { records = replayedCount })
end

---Detach a sink after flushing it.
---@param sink table
function Logger.removeSink(sink)
	for index, attachedSink in ipairs(Logger.sinks) do
		if attachedSink == sink then
			sink:flush()
			table.remove(Logger.sinks, index)
			return
		end
	end

	return error("Logger.removeSink: sink is not attached")
end

---Build one structured record and hand it to every sink.
---Caller source comes from debug.info, never from a hand-written string.
---@param level string
---@param scope string module path, matching the folder layout
---@param event string call | return | step | formula | branch | resolve | error | lifecycle names
---@param payload table?
---@param traceback string?
function Logger.write(level, scope, event, payload, traceback)
	if not Logger.accepts(level) then
		return
	end

	-- Level 3 is the function that called Logger.<level>.
	local callerSource, callerLine = debug.info(LOG_CALLER_STACK_LEVEL, "sl")

	local traceId = "none"
	local depth = 0
	if Logger.contextProvider then
		traceId, depth = Logger.contextProvider()
	end

	local record = {
		timestamp = os.clock() - Logger.sessionStartClock,
		level = level,
		scope = scope,
		event = event,
		traceId = traceId,
		depth = depth,
		source = string.format("%s:%s", tostring(callerSource), tostring(callerLine)),
		payload = payload,
		traceback = traceback,
	}

	Logger.counts[level] += 1
	Logger.lastByLevel[level] = record

	if #Logger.sinks == 0 then
		table.insert(Logger.pendingRecords, record)
		return
	end

	for _, sink in ipairs(Logger.sinks) do
		sink:push(record)
	end

	-- An error must reach disk even if the session dies right after.
	if level == "ERROR" then
		Logger.flush()
	end
end

---Flush every sink.
function Logger.flush()
	for _, sink in ipairs(Logger.sinks) do
		sink:flush()
	end
end

---@param scope string
---@param event string
---@param payload table?
function Logger.trace(scope, event, payload)
	Logger.write("TRACE", scope, event, payload)
end

---@param scope string
---@param event string
---@param payload table?
function Logger.debug(scope, event, payload)
	Logger.write("DEBUG", scope, event, payload)
end

---@param scope string
---@param event string
---@param payload table?
function Logger.info(scope, event, payload)
	Logger.write("INFO", scope, event, payload)
end

---@param scope string
---@param event string
---@param payload table?
---@param traceback string?
function Logger.warn(scope, event, payload, traceback)
	Logger.write("WARN", scope, event, payload, traceback)
end

---@param scope string
---@param event string
---@param payload table?
---@param traceback string? captured at the raise site; when omitted, the caller's stack is captured here
function Logger.error(scope, event, payload, traceback)
	-- ERROR always carries a traceback (style guide 7.5); level 2 is the caller of Logger.error.
	local stackText = traceback
	if stackText == nil then
		stackText = debug.traceback(nil, ERROR_CALLER_STACK_LEVEL)
	end

	Logger.write("ERROR", scope, event, payload, stackText)
end

return Logger

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Maid"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Maid

	Ownership of everything a feature creates: connections, threads, instances, cleanup functions,
	and objects with a Destroy method. Cleaning a maid releases all of it and reports the counts,
	so teardown can be proven by its numbers.
]]

local Maid = {}
Maid.__index = Maid

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---Create a maid owned by a named feature.
---@param ownerName string
---@return table
function Maid.new(ownerName)
	local self = setmetatable({}, Maid)

	self.ownerName = ownerName
	self.tasks = {}

	return self
end

---Release every owned task, newest first, and report what was released.
---@return table counts per task type
function Maid:clean()
	local releasedCounts = {}

	for index = #self.tasks, 1, -1 do
		local ownedTask = self.tasks[index]
		local taskType = typeof(ownedTask)

		if taskType == "RBXScriptConnection" then
			ownedTask:Disconnect()
		elseif taskType == "thread" then
			-- A thread that already finished cannot be cancelled; that is a normal end state.
			if coroutine.status(ownedTask) ~= "dead" then
				task.cancel(ownedTask)
			end
		elseif taskType == "Instance" then
			ownedTask:Destroy()
		elseif taskType == "function" then
			ownedTask()
		else
			ownedTask:Destroy()
		end

		releasedCounts[taskType] = (releasedCounts[taskType] or 0) + 1
	end

	table.clear(self.tasks)

	Logger.info("Utility.Maid", "cleaned", { owner = self.ownerName, released = releasedCounts })

	return releasedCounts
end

Tracer.instrument("Utility.Maid", Maid)

-- Hot-path functions, installed after instrumentation: they run thousands of times per planning
-- cycle (profiled in the first live run: 1.1 M trace records in 78 s). Failures still raise with a
-- named message, which the traced caller logs with its traceback.

---Take ownership of a task and return it.
---@param ownedTask any RBXScriptConnection | thread | Instance | function | table with Destroy
---@return any
function Maid:give(ownedTask)
	local taskType = typeof(ownedTask)

	local supported = taskType == "RBXScriptConnection"
		or taskType == "thread"
		or taskType == "Instance"
		or taskType == "function"
		or (taskType == "table" and type(ownedTask.Destroy) == "function")

	if not supported then
		return error(string.format("Maid:give(%s): unsupported task type '%s'", self.ownerName, taskType))
	end

	table.insert(self.tasks, ownedTask)

	return ownedTask
end

---Number of owned tasks.
---@return number
function Maid:count()
	return #self.tasks
end

return Maid


]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Profiler"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Profiler

	Measures how long labelled operations take, keeps per-label totals for the UI, and logs each
	measurement. Numbers about cost in comments or the UI come from here, never from estimates.
]]

local Profiler = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

-- Constants.
-- T5: pcall returns the success flag at index 1 and the callee's results from index 2.
local PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX = 2

-- State.
Profiler.stats = {}

---Run a function, measure it, and return its results. Failures propagate unchanged.
---@param label string
---@param callee function
---@return any
function Profiler.run(label, callee, ...)
	local startClock = os.clock()
	local results = table.pack(pcall(callee, ...))
	local durationSeconds = os.clock() - startClock

	local labelStats = Profiler.stats[label]
	if labelStats == nil then
		labelStats = { count = 0, totalSeconds = 0, maxSeconds = 0, lastSeconds = 0, failures = 0 }
		Profiler.stats[label] = labelStats
	end

	labelStats.count += 1
	labelStats.totalSeconds += durationSeconds
	labelStats.lastSeconds = durationSeconds
	labelStats.maxSeconds = math.max(labelStats.maxSeconds, durationSeconds)

	if not results[1] then
		labelStats.failures += 1
	end

	if Logger.accepts("DEBUG") then
		Logger.debug("Utility.Profiler", "profile", {
			label = label,
			durationSeconds = durationSeconds,
			succeeded = results[1],
		})
	end

	-- The failure was raised inside callee; re-raise it unchanged so the caller's handler sees it.
	if not results[1] then
		return error(results[PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX], 0)
	end

	return table.unpack(results, PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX, results.n)
end

---Copy of the per-label statistics.
---@return table
function Profiler.snapshot()
	local copy = {}

	for label, labelStats in pairs(Profiler.stats) do
		copy[label] = table.clone(labelStats)
	end

	return copy
end

-- Profiler is not passed through Tracer.instrument: it wraps traced code and tracing it would
-- measure the tracer instead of the work.
return Profiler

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Resolver"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Resolver

	The single way to reach anything in the game tree. Required instances either resolve or raise
	a named error with the exact missing path, so a game update produces a readable failure instead
	of a silent no-op. Resolutions are cached per session and re-resolved when the cached instance
	has left the tree. Optional lookups (a tile that may or may not hold a troop) must state why
	absence is a normal fact.
]]

local Resolver = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

-- State.
Resolver.cache = {}
Resolver.cacheHits = 0
Resolver.cacheMisses = 0

---Resolve a service by name or raise with the name that is missing.
---@param serviceName string
---@return Instance
function Resolver.requireService(serviceName)
	local serviceOk, service = pcall(game.GetService, game, serviceName)

	if not serviceOk or service == nil then
		return error(string.format("Resolver: service '%s' unavailable: %s", serviceName, tostring(service)))
	end

	return service
end

---Resolve a required descendant by slash path ("Countries/Germany/Economy"), cached per session.
---@param root Instance
---@param path string
---@return Instance
function Resolver.requireDescendant(root, path)
	local cacheKey = root:GetFullName() .. "|" .. path
	local cached = Resolver.cache[cacheKey]

	-- A cached instance that left the tree is stale; resolve again.
	if cached ~= nil and cached:IsDescendantOf(game) then
		Resolver.cacheHits += 1
		return cached
	end

	Resolver.cacheMisses += 1

	local current = root
	for _, segment in ipairs(string.split(path, "/")) do
		local nextInstance = current:FindFirstChild(segment)

		if nextInstance == nil then
			return error(string.format("Resolver: missing '%s' under '%s' (stopped at '%s')", path, root:GetFullName(), current:GetFullName()))
		end

		current = nextInstance
	end

	Resolver.cache[cacheKey] = current

	Logger.debug("Utility.Resolver", "resolve", {
		root = root:GetFullName(),
		path = path,
		className = current.ClassName,
		cachedPreviously = cached ~= nil,
	})

	return current
end

---Wait for a child to appear, driven by ChildAdded, raising after a caller-derived timeout.
---@param parent Instance
---@param childName string
---@param timeoutSeconds number derived by the caller (T1/T2); its provenance goes in timeoutSource
---@param timeoutSource string where the timeout came from
---@return Instance
function Resolver.awaitChild(parent, childName, timeoutSeconds, timeoutSource)
	local existing = parent:FindFirstChild(childName)
	if existing ~= nil then
		return existing
	end

	local arrivedSignal = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local arrived = nil
	local connection = parent.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == childName then
			arrived = child
			arrivedSignal:Fire()
		end
	end)

	local timeoutThread = task.delay(timeoutSeconds, function()
		arrivedSignal:Fire()
	end)

	arrivedSignal.Event:Wait()

	connection:Disconnect()

	-- When the timeout fired, the timeout thread is the one that resumed us (status "normal") and
	-- cannot be cancelled; only a timeout still waiting on its delay is cancelled.
	if coroutine.status(timeoutThread) == "suspended" then
		task.cancel(timeoutThread)
	end

	arrivedSignal:Destroy()

	if arrived == nil then
		return error(string.format(
			"Resolver: '%s' did not appear under '%s' within %s s (timeout from %s)",
			childName,
			parent:GetFullName(),
			tostring(timeoutSeconds),
			timeoutSource
		))
	end

	return arrived
end

---Drop every cached resolution, for example when the local player's country changes.
---@param reason string
function Resolver.invalidate(reason)
	local droppedCount = 0
	for _ in pairs(Resolver.cache) do
		droppedCount += 1
	end

	table.clear(Resolver.cache)

	Logger.info("Utility.Resolver", "invalidated", { reason = reason, dropped = droppedCount })
end

Tracer.instrument("Utility.Resolver", Resolver)

-- Hot-path functions, installed after instrumentation: they run thousands of times per planning
-- cycle (profiled in the first live run: 1.1 M trace records in 78 s). Failures still raise with a
-- named message, which the traced caller logs with its traceback.

---Resolve a required direct child or raise with the missing path.
---@param parent Instance
---@param childName string
---@return Instance
function Resolver.requireChild(parent, childName)
	local child = parent:FindFirstChild(childName)

	if child == nil then
		return error(string.format("Resolver: missing '%s' under '%s'", childName, parent:GetFullName()))
	end

	return child
end

---Look up a child whose absence is a normal game fact; the reason is required and logged at TRACE.
---@param parent Instance
---@param childName string
---@param absenceReason string why a missing child is expected, e.g. "tile without a troop"
---@return Instance?
function Resolver.optionalChild(parent, childName, absenceReason)
	assert(type(absenceReason) == "string" and absenceReason ~= "", "Resolver.optionalChild: absenceReason is required")

	return parent:FindFirstChild(childName)
end

---Read a required attribute or raise with the instance path, the attribute name and the type found.
---@param instance Instance
---@param attributeName string
---@param expectedType string typeof() name, e.g. "number", "string", "boolean", "Color3"
---@return any
function Resolver.requireAttribute(instance, attributeName, expectedType)
	local value = instance:GetAttribute(attributeName)

	if typeof(value) ~= expectedType then
		return error(string.format(
			"Resolver: attribute '%s' on '%s' is %s, expected %s",
			attributeName,
			instance:GetFullName(),
			typeof(value),
			expectedType
		))
	end

	return value
end

---Read an attribute whose absence is a normal game fact (the reason is required). A present value
---of the wrong type still raises: absence is a fact, a wrong type is a game change.
---@param instance Instance
---@param attributeName string
---@param expectedType string
---@param absenceReason string why a missing attribute is expected, e.g. "set only while a troop is moving"
---@return any
function Resolver.optionalAttribute(instance, attributeName, expectedType, absenceReason)
	assert(type(absenceReason) == "string" and absenceReason ~= "", "Resolver.optionalAttribute: absenceReason is required")

	local value = instance:GetAttribute(attributeName)

	if value ~= nil and typeof(value) ~= expectedType then
		return error(string.format(
			"Resolver: attribute '%s' on '%s' is %s, expected %s or absent (%s)",
			attributeName,
			instance:GetFullName(),
			typeof(value),
			expectedType,
			absenceReason
		))
	end

	return value
end

return Resolver


]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Serializer"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Serializer

	Converts any Luau value into a JSON-encodable value without losing what it was, so log
	records and data files can hold instances, vectors, colours, enums and functions.
]]

local Serializer = {}

-- Services.
local httpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

---Convert any Luau value into a JSON-encodable value.
---Hot path (every log record): not instrumented; Logger accounts for records written.
---@param value any
---@param seen table tables currently on the conversion stack, used only to cut cycles
---@return any
function Serializer.toJsonSafe(value, seen)
	local valueType = typeof(value)

	if valueType == "nil" or valueType == "boolean" then
		return value
	end

	if valueType == "number" then
		-- JSON has no NaN or infinity; keep them as tagged values instead of corrupting the file.
		if value ~= value then
			return { ["$number"] = "NaN" }
		end

		if value == math.huge then
			return { ["$number"] = "Infinity" }
		end

		if value == -math.huge then
			return { ["$number"] = "-Infinity" }
		end

		return value
	end

	if valueType == "string" then
		if utf8.len(value) then
			return value
		end

		-- Invalid UTF-8 would make JSONEncode fail; keep the raw bytes as hex.
		local hexBytes = string.gsub(value, ".", function(character)
			return string.format("%02x", string.byte(character))
		end)
