		return { ["$bytesHex"] = hexBytes }
	end

	if valueType == "Instance" then
		return { ["$instance"] = value:GetFullName(), ["$class"] = value.ClassName }
	end

	if valueType == "Vector3" then
		return { ["$Vector3"] = { value.X, value.Y, value.Z } }
	end

	if valueType == "Vector2" then
		return { ["$Vector2"] = { value.X, value.Y } }
	end

	if valueType == "CFrame" then
		return { ["$CFrame"] = { value:GetComponents() } }
	end

	if valueType == "Color3" then
		return { ["$Color3"] = { value.R, value.G, value.B } }
	end

	if valueType == "BrickColor" then
		return { ["$BrickColor"] = value.Name }
	end

	if valueType == "EnumItem" then
		return { ["$Enum"] = tostring(value) }
	end

	if valueType == "UDim2" then
		return { ["$UDim2"] = { value.X.Scale, value.X.Offset, value.Y.Scale, value.Y.Offset } }
	end

	if valueType == "function" then
		local sourceName, lineNumber, functionName = debug.info(value, "sln")
		return { ["$function"] = { source = sourceName, line = lineNumber, name = functionName } }
	end

	if valueType == "table" then
		if seen[value] then
			return { ["$cycle"] = true }
		end

		seen[value] = true

		-- A table is an array only when its keys are exactly the integers 1..#value.
		local keyCount = 0
		for _ in pairs(value) do
			keyCount += 1
		end

		local isArray = keyCount == #value
		if isArray then
			for index = 1, #value do
				if value[index] == nil then
					isArray = false
					break
				end
			end
		end

		local converted = {}
		if isArray then
			for index = 1, #value do
				converted[index] = Serializer.toJsonSafe(value[index], seen)
			end
		else
			for key, entry in pairs(value) do
				local keyText = type(key) == "string" and key or ("[" .. typeof(key) .. "]" .. tostring(key))
				converted[keyText] = Serializer.toJsonSafe(entry, seen)
			end
		end

		-- Only cycles are cut; a table shared by two branches is written in both.
		seen[value] = nil

		return converted
	end

	return { ["$" .. valueType] = tostring(value) }
end

---Encode a value as one JSON document.
---@param value any
---@return string
function Serializer.encode(value)
	return httpService:JSONEncode(Serializer.toJsonSafe(value, {}))
end

---Decode a JSON document produced by the game (attribute payloads such as battleInfo).
---Raises with the source name when the text is not valid JSON.
---@param text string
---@param sourceName string where the text came from, for the error message
---@return any
function Serializer.decode(text, sourceName)
	local decodeOk, decoded = pcall(httpService.JSONDecode, httpService, text)

	if not decodeOk then
		return error(string.format("Serializer.decode: '%s' is not valid JSON: %s", sourceName, tostring(decoded)))
	end

	return decoded
end

return Serializer

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Signal"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Signal

	A synchronous event used between the AI's own layers (the world model announces a change, the
	scheduler reacts). Listeners run in connection order. A listener that fails is logged as an
	ERROR with its traceback and counted; the remaining listeners still run, because one broken
	reaction must not blind every other consumer of the same fact. The failure count is part of the
	signal's stats and reaches the menu through the logger's error counters.
]]

local Signal = {}
Signal.__index = Signal

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

-- Constants.
-- T5: debug.traceback level of the listener that raised, seen from inside the xpcall handler.
local RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL = 2

---Create a named signal.
---@param signalName string e.g. "Game.Units.unitAdded"
---@return table
function Signal.new(signalName)
	local self = setmetatable({}, Signal)

	self.signalName = signalName
	self.listeners = {}
	self.fireCount = 0
	self.failureCount = 0

	return self
end

---Connect a listener; returns a function that disconnects it (give it to a Maid).
---@param listener function
---@return function
function Signal:connect(listener)
	assert(type(listener) == "function", string.format("Signal(%s):connect needs a function", self.signalName))

	table.insert(self.listeners, listener)

	return function()
		local index = table.find(self.listeners, listener)
		if index ~= nil then
			table.remove(self.listeners, index)
		end
	end
end

---Call every listener with the arguments.
function Signal:fire(...)
	self.fireCount += 1

	-- Listeners may disconnect while the signal fires; iterate over a copy.
	local listenersNow = table.clone(self.listeners)

	for _, listener in ipairs(listenersNow) do
		local signalName = self.signalName

		local listenerOk = xpcall(listener, function(failure)
			Logger.error("Utility.Signal", "listenerFailed", {
				signal = signalName,
				message = tostring(failure),
			}, debug.traceback(tostring(failure), RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL))

			return failure
		end, ...)

		if not listenerOk then
			self.failureCount += 1
		end
	end
end

---Counters for status reports.
---@return table
function Signal:stats()
	return {
		signal = self.signalName,
		listeners = #self.listeners,
		fired = self.fireCount,
		failures = self.failureCount,
	}
end

---Drop every listener.
function Signal:Destroy()
	table.clear(self.listeners)
end

-- Signal is not instrumented: it is called on every world event and its listeners are traced
-- functions already, so tracing fire() would double every record.
return Signal

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Stats"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Stats

	Small descriptive statistics for measured samples (T2 values such as tick periods, hit
	intervals and reaction latencies). Pure functions; they never modify their input.
]]

local Stats = {}

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

-- Constants.
-- T5: the median of n sorted values sits at position (n + 1) / 2; an even n averages the two middles.
local MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR = 2

---Median of a list of numbers, or nil for an empty list.
---@param samples table array of numbers
---@return number?
function Stats.median(samples)
	local count = #samples

	if count == 0 then
		return nil
	end

	local sorted = table.clone(samples)
	table.sort(sorted)

	local middle = (count + 1) / MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR

	return (sorted[math.floor(middle)] + sorted[math.ceil(middle)]) / MEDIAN_POSITION_DIVISOR
end

---Largest value of a list, or nil for an empty list.
---@param samples table
---@return number?
function Stats.max(samples)
	local largest = nil

	for _, value in ipairs(samples) do
		if largest == nil or value > largest then
			largest = value
		end
	end

	return largest
end

return Tracer.instrument("Utility.Stats", Stats)

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["Utility/Tracer"] = [=[
--[[
	Utility/Tracer

	Call tracing and correlation ids. Tracer.instrument wraps every function on a module table so
	each call logs entry (arguments, caller from debug.info), exit (results, duration) and failure
	(traceback captured at the raise site). Tracer.withTrace runs one operation under a fresh trace
	id so every record it produces can be grouped. Tracer.step records the decision points inside a
	function body.
]]

local Tracer = {}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

-- Constants.
-- T5: pcall/xpcall return the success flag at index 1 and the callee's results from index 2.
local PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX = 2
-- T5: debug.info / debug.traceback level of the function one frame above the current one.
local CALLER_STACK_LEVEL = 2

-- State.
Tracer.traceCounter = 0
Tracer.currentTraceId = "none"
Tracer.frames = {}

---Current trace id and call depth, for Logger records.
---@return string, number
function Tracer.context()
	return Tracer.currentTraceId, #Tracer.frames
end

-- Publish the context provider so Logger records carry trace ids without requiring Tracer.
Logger.contextProvider = Tracer.context

---Run fn under a new trace id, restoring the previous id afterwards (also on failure).
---@param label string what the operation is, e.g. "frontline.cycle"
---@param callee function
---@return any
function Tracer.withTrace(label, callee, ...)
	Tracer.traceCounter += 1

	local previousTraceId = Tracer.currentTraceId
	Tracer.currentTraceId = string.format("trc_%s_%s", label, tostring(Tracer.traceCounter))

	---Log the failure with the stack at the raise site before the trace id is restored.
	local function onTraceError(failure)
		Logger.error("Utility.Tracer", "traceFailed", {
			label = label,
			message = tostring(failure),
		}, debug.traceback(tostring(failure), CALLER_STACK_LEVEL))

		return failure
	end

	local results = table.pack(xpcall(callee, onTraceError, ...))

	Tracer.currentTraceId = previousTraceId

	if not results[1] then
		return error(results[PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX], 0)
	end

	return table.unpack(results, PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX, results.n)
end

---Describe a value for a log record without yielding or dumping large payloads.
---@param value any
---@return any
function Tracer.describeValue(value)
	local valueType = typeof(value)

	if valueType == "Instance" then
		return { instance = value:GetFullName(), class = value.ClassName }
	end

	if valueType == "table" then
		local keyCount = 0
		for _ in pairs(value) do
			keyCount += 1
		end

		return { shape = "table", keys = keyCount, length = #value }
	end

	if valueType == "string" or valueType == "number" or valueType == "boolean" or valueType == "nil" then
		return value
	end

	return { type = valueType, text = tostring(value) }
end

---Describe a packed argument or result list.
---@param packed table
---@return table
function Tracer.describeValues(packed)
	local described = {}

	for index = 1, packed.n do
		table.insert(described, { index = index, value = Tracer.describeValue(packed[index]) })
	end

	return described
end

---Record a decision point inside a function body.
---@param qualifiedName string
---@param stepName string
---@param payload table
function Tracer.step(qualifiedName, stepName, payload)
	if not Logger.accepts("TRACE") then
		return
	end

	Logger.trace(qualifiedName, "step", {
		step = stepName,
		values = payload,
	})
end

---Wrap one function with entry/exit tracing, duration, and an error traceback.
---@param qualifiedName string
---@param callee function
---@return function
function Tracer.wrapFunction(qualifiedName, callee)
	---Log a wrapped call failure with the stack intact, then hand the message back.
	local function onWrappedError(failure)
		Logger.error(qualifiedName, "error", {
			message = tostring(failure),
		}, debug.traceback(tostring(failure), CALLER_STACK_LEVEL))

		return failure
	end

	return function(...)
		-- Skip every allocation when the record would be discarded anyway.
		if not Logger.accepts("TRACE") then
			return callee(...)
		end

		local callerSource, callerLine = debug.info(CALLER_STACK_LEVEL, "sl")
		local callStartClock = os.clock()

		table.insert(Tracer.frames, qualifiedName)

		Logger.trace(qualifiedName, "call", {
			caller = string.format("%s:%s", tostring(callerSource), tostring(callerLine)),
			arguments = Tracer.describeValues(table.pack(...)),
		})

		local results = table.pack(xpcall(callee, onWrappedError, ...))

		table.remove(Tracer.frames)

		-- The handler already logged the traceback; re-raise so callers still see the failure.
		if not results[1] then
			return error(results[PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX], 0)
		end

		Logger.trace(qualifiedName, "return", {
			durationSeconds = os.clock() - callStartClock,
			results = Tracer.describeValues(table.pack(table.unpack(results, PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX, results.n))),
		})

		return table.unpack(results, PCALL_RESULTS_START_INDEX, results.n)
	end
end

---Wrap every function on a module or class table so the whole module is traced.
---Metatable-backed instances inherit the wrapped methods through __index.
---@param scopeName string
---@param moduleTable table
---@return table
function Tracer.instrument(scopeName, moduleTable)
	local wrappedCount = 0

	for keyName, value in pairs(moduleTable) do
		if type(value) == "function" and keyName ~= "__index" then
			moduleTable[keyName] = Tracer.wrapFunction(scopeName .. "." .. keyName, value)
			wrappedCount += 1
		end
	end

	Logger.info("Utility.Tracer", "instrumented", { scope = scopeName, functions = wrappedCount })

	return moduleTable
end

return Tracer

]=]

MODULE_SOURCES["WarAI"] = [=[
--[[
	WarAI

	The orchestrator. Owns the session log, the settings, the menu and every feature. attach() brings
	the whole script up; detach() tears it down and reports what it released, so a re-execution never
	leaves a second copy running.

	Two lifetimes:
	- session: log sink, settings, menu, game modules, remotes check, executors, scheduler (the main
	  maid);
	- map: the tile graph, ownership, units and the tick detector, rebuilt when a new workspace.Map
	  arrives or the map attribute changes (the world maid).
	Our country can change within a round (annexed, then re-selected): every per-country memory is
	reset and the scheduler's event filters are refreshed.
]]

local WarAI = {
	attached = false,
}

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.FileSink
local FileSink = require("Utility/FileSink")

---@module Utility.Tracer
local Tracer = require("Utility/Tracer")

---@module Utility.Maid
local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")

---@module Utility.Profiler
local Profiler = require("Utility/Profiler")

---@module Utility.Formula
local Formula = require("Utility/Formula")

---@module Utility.Resolver
local Resolver = require("Utility/Resolver")

---@module Features.Core.Settings
local Settings = require("Features/Core/Settings")

---@module Features.Core.SettingsCatalog
local SettingsCatalog = require("Features/Core/SettingsCatalog")

---@module GUI.Linoria
local Linoria = require("GUI/Linoria")

---@module Menu
local Menu = require("Menu")

---@module Game.GameModules
local GameModules = require("Game/GameModules")

---@module Game.Remotes
local Remotes = require("Game/Remotes")

---@module Game.Session
local Session = require("Game/Session")

---@module Game.MapGraph
local MapGraph = require("Game/MapGraph")

---@module Game.Ownership
local Ownership = require("Game/Ownership")

---@module Game.Units
local Units = require("Game/Units")

---@module Game.Tick
local Tick = require("Game/Tick")

---@module Features.Economy.TechCatalog
local TechCatalog = require("Features/Economy/TechCatalog")

---@module Features.Economy.EconomyPlanner
local EconomyPlanner = require("Features/Economy/EconomyPlanner")

---@module Features.Orders.OrderExecutor
local OrderExecutor = require("Features/Orders/OrderExecutor")

---@module Features.Orders.ActionDispatcher
local ActionDispatcher = require("Features/Orders/ActionDispatcher")

---@module Features.Orders.ArmyControl
local ArmyControl = require("Features/Orders/ArmyControl")

---@module Features.Navy.NavyPlanner
local NavyPlanner = require("Features/Navy/NavyPlanner")

---@module Features.Air.AirPlanner
local AirPlanner = require("Features/Air/AirPlanner")

---@module Features.Review.Scorecard
local Scorecard = require("Features/Review/Scorecard")

---@module Features.Review.PlanOverlay
local PlanOverlay = require("Features/Review/PlanOverlay")

---@module Features.Core.AntiIdle
local AntiIdle = require("Features/Core/AntiIdle")

---@module Features.Core.Scheduler
local Scheduler = require("Features/Core/Scheduler")

-- Constants.
local LOG_FOLDER = "CTW_WarAI/logs"
-- T5: debug.traceback level of the function that raised, seen from inside an xpcall handler.
local RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL = 2
-- T5: milliseconds per second, for the latency status line.
local MILLISECONDS_PER_SECOND = 1000
-- Status lines shown on the menu's Status tab, in display order.
local STATUS_KEYS = { "state", "country", "enemies", "tick", "units", "plan", "orders", "reaction", "economy", "scorecard", "logLevel", "logRecords", "lastError" }

-- Services.
local runService = game:GetService("RunService")

-- State.
local maid = nil
local worldMaid = nil
local sessionSink = nil
local sessionTag = nil

---Declare the settings the orchestrator itself owns.
function WarAI.defineCoreSettings()
	Settings.define({
		key = "logLevel",
		tab = "Debug",
		section = "Logging",
		label = "Log level",
		kind = "dropdown",
		unit = "level",
		choices = Logger.LEVELS,
		default = "DEBUG",
		defaultSource = "first live run: TRACE wrote 245 MB in 78 s (1.1 M call records) and dropped frames; DEBUG keeps every formula, branch, decision and order",
		effect = "Lowest record level written to the session log. TRACE logs every call of every instrumented function with arguments and results; DEBUG logs formulas, branches and resolutions; INFO and above log lifecycle, orders, warnings and errors only.",
	})

	SettingsCatalog.defineAll()

	Logger.setLevel(Settings.get("logLevel"))
	Formula.logEveryEvaluation = Settings.get("formula.logEveryEvaluation")

	maid:give(Settings.onChanged("logLevel", function(level)
		Logger.setLevel(level)
		Menu.setStatus("logLevel", level)
	end))

	maid:give(Settings.onChanged("formula.logEveryEvaluation", function(value)
		Formula.logEveryEvaluation = value
	end))
end

---Refresh the status lines that describe the logger.
function WarAI.refreshLogStatus()
	local counts = Logger.counts

	Menu.setStatus("logRecords", string.format(
		"T%d D%d I%d W%d E%d",
		counts.TRACE,
		counts.DEBUG,
		counts.INFO,
		counts.WARN,
		counts.ERROR
	))

	local lastError = Logger.lastByLevel.ERROR
	if lastError ~= nil then
		Menu.setStatus("lastError", string.format("%s %s", lastError.scope, lastError.event))
	end
end

---Refresh the status lines after a cycle.
---@param world table?
---@param plan table?
---@param summary table?
function WarAI.refreshStatus(world, plan, summary)
	local period = Tick.periodSeconds()
	Menu.setStatus("tick", period and string.format("%.3f s (%d passes)", period, Tick.passCount) or string.format("measuring (%d passes)", Tick.passCount))

	if world ~= nil then
		local enemyNames = {}
		for enemyName in pairs(world.enemies) do
			table.insert(enemyNames, enemyName)
		end
		table.sort(enemyNames)

		Menu.setStatus("country", world.me)
		Menu.setStatus("enemies", #enemyNames > 0 and table.concat(enemyNames, ", ") or "none (peace)")
		Menu.setStatus("units", string.format("ours %d, enemy %d", #world.ourUnits, #world.enemyUnits))
	end

	if plan ~= nil then
		local planSummary = plan:summary()
		Menu.setStatus("plan", string.format("%d orders, %d halts, %d locks", planSummary.orders, planSummary.halts, planSummary.locks))
	end

	if summary ~= nil then
		local stats = OrderExecutor.stats
		Menu.setStatus("orders", string.format("sent %d, accepted %d, unaccepted %d, dry-run %d, unchanged %d", stats.sent, stats.accepted, stats.notAccepted, stats.dryRun, stats.unchanged))
	end

	local latency = Scheduler.latencyStats()
	if latency.count > 0 then
		Menu.setStatus("reaction", string.format("median %.1f ms, max %.1f ms (%d)", latency.median * MILLISECONDS_PER_SECOND, latency.max * MILLISECONDS_PER_SECOND, latency.count))
	end

	local score = Scorecard.summary()
	Menu.setStatus("scorecard", string.format("attacks won %d, lost %d, open %d; hit %.3f s", score.attacksWon, score.attacksLost, score.open, score.hitIntervalSeconds))

	local economy = EconomyPlanner.lastSummary
	if economy ~= nil then
		Menu.setStatus("economy", string.format("money %.0f; research %s", economy.money, economy.research and tostring(economy.research.chosen) or "-"))
	end

	WarAI.refreshLogStatus()
end

---Build (or rebuild) everything that belongs to the current map.
---@param reason string
function WarAI.buildWorld(reason)
	if worldMaid ~= nil then
		worldMaid:clean()
	end

	worldMaid = Maid.new("WarAI.world")

	Units.reset(reason)
	Tick.reset(reason)
	Resolver.invalidate(reason)

	Menu.setStatus("state", "building the map graph (" .. reason .. ")")

	local mapModel = Session.mapModel()
	MapGraph.build(mapModel, worldMaid)
	Ownership.attach(worldMaid)

	local country = Session.ourCountry()
	Units.attach(mapModel, country and country.Name or nil, worldMaid)
	Tick.attach(MapGraph, worldMaid)

	Scheduler.refreshContext(country and country.Name or nil)

	Logger.info("WarAI", "worldBuilt", { reason = reason, map = Session.mapName(), tiles = #MapGraph.tileOrder, units = Units.stats() })
end

---Our country changed (annexed, selected, switched).
---@param country Instance?
---@param reason string?
function WarAI.onCountryChanged(country, reason)
	local countryName = country and country.Name or nil

	Units.setOurCountry(countryName)
	OrderExecutor.reset("country changed")
	ActionDispatcher.reset("country changed")
	Resolver.invalidate("country changed")
	Scheduler.refreshContext(countryName)

	Menu.setStatus("country", countryName or ("none: " .. tostring(reason)))

	if countryName ~= nil then
		Scheduler.requestTick({ reason = "country changed" })
	end
end

---The per-tick work after the front plan.
---@param world table
---@param analysis table
---@param plan table
function WarAI.onTick(world, analysis, plan)
	Profiler.run("Tick_Economy", EconomyPlanner.run, world, analysis)
	Profiler.run("Tick_ArmyControl", ArmyControl.run, world, analysis)

	if Settings.get("navy.enabled") then
		Profiler.run("Tick_Navy", NavyPlanner.run, world)
	end

	Profiler.run("Tick_Air", AirPlanner.run, world, plan)
	Profiler.run("Tick_Scorecard", Scorecard.resolveStopped, world)
	Ownership.verifyCounts()
end

---Bring the script up.
function WarAI.attach()
	maid = Maid.new("WarAI")
	sessionTag = tostring(os.time())

	if not isfolder(LOG_FOLDER) then
		makefolder(LOG_FOLDER)
	end

	sessionSink = FileSink.new(LOG_FOLDER .. "/session_" .. sessionTag .. ".jsonl")
	Logger.addSink(sessionSink)

	-- One flush per frame: the frame boundary is the natural batching point for log writes.
	maid:give(runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		Logger.flush()
	end))

	WarAI.defineCoreSettings()

	local libraries = Linoria.load()
	Menu.build(libraries, STATUS_KEYS, WarAI.detach)
	maid:give(Menu.destroy)
	maid:give(Menu.startPump())

	Menu.setStatus("logLevel", Logger.activeLevel)
	Menu.setStatus("state", "loading game modules")

	if not Session.isCorrectPlace() then
		Menu.setStatus("state", string.format("stopped: not Conquer The World WW2 (PlaceId %s)", tostring(game.PlaceId)))
		Logger.error("WarAI", "wrongPlace", { placeId = game.PlaceId })
		WarAI.attached = true
		return
	end

	GameModules.loadAll()
	Remotes.verifyAll()
	TechCatalog.build()

	-- Module signals live for the whole session.
	for _, signal in ipairs({
		Session.countryChanged,
		Session.mapReplaced,
		MapGraph.tileColorChanged,
		MapGraph.tileAttributeChanged,
		Ownership.ownerChanged,
		Units.unitAdded,
		Units.unitMoved,
		Units.unitRemoved,
		Units.unitChanged,
		Tick.dayTick,
	}) do
		maid:give(signal)
	end

	maid:give(Session.countryChanged:connect(WarAI.onCountryChanged))
	maid:give(Session.mapReplaced:connect(function(reason)
		-- Building the graph yields (TileGraph), so it runs on its own thread owned by the maid.
		maid:give(task.spawn(function()
			local rebuildOk = xpcall(WarAI.buildWorld, function(failure)
				Logger.error("WarAI", "worldRebuildFailed", { reason = reason, message = tostring(failure) }, debug.traceback(tostring(failure), RAISE_SITE_STACK_LEVEL))
				return failure
			end, reason)

			if not rebuildOk then
				Menu.setStatus("state", "stopped: map rebuild failed, see log")
			end
		end))
	end))

	maid:give(function()
		if worldMaid ~= nil then
			worldMaid:clean()
			worldMaid = nil
		end
	end)

	WarAI.buildWorld("attach")

	OrderExecutor.attach(maid)
	ActionDispatcher.attach(maid)
	Scorecard.attach(maid)
	AntiIdle.attach(maid)

	Scheduler.attach(maid, {
		onVerdict = function(verdict)
			Menu.setStatus("state", verdict.reason or "?")
		end,
		onPlanned = function(world, plan, summary)
			Scorecard.recordLaunches(world, plan, summary.sent > 0)
			PlanOverlay.draw(plan, maid)
			WarAI.refreshStatus(world, plan, summary)
		end,
		onTick = WarAI.onTick,
	})

	Session.attach(maid)

	WarAI.attached = true
	WarAI.refreshStatus(nil, nil, nil)

	Logger.info("WarAI", "attach", {
		sessionTag = sessionTag,
		logPath = sessionSink.filePath,
		settings = #Settings.definitions,
		profile = Profiler.snapshot(),
	})
end

---Tear everything down and report what was released. The session sink is removed last so the
---final records reach the file.
function WarAI.detach()
	-- A half-finished attach still owns tasks, so cleanup keys on the maid, not on `attached`.
	if maid == nil then
		return
	end

	WarAI.attached = false

	local released = maid:clean()
	maid = nil

	Logger.info("WarAI", "detach", {
		sessionTag = sessionTag,
		released = released,
		logCounts = Logger.counts,
		orders = OrderExecutor.stats,
		actions = ActionDispatcher.stats,
		scorecard = Scorecard.summary(),
		profile = Profiler.snapshot(),
	})

	if sessionSink ~= nil then
		Logger.removeSink(sessionSink)
		sessionSink = nil
	end

	if shared.CTW_WarAI == WarAI then
		shared.CTW_WarAI = nil
	end
end

---Report the orchestrator's state for remote inspection.
---@return table
function WarAI.status()
	return {
		attached = WarAI.attached,
		sessionTag = sessionTag,
		logPath = sessionSink and sessionSink.filePath or nil,
		sink = sessionSink and sessionSink:stats() or nil,
		logCounts = Logger.counts,
		lastError = Logger.lastByLevel.ERROR,
		ownedTasks = maid and maid:count() or 0,
		units = Units.stats(),
		tickPeriod = Tick.periodSeconds(),
		orders = OrderExecutor.stats,
		actions = ActionDispatcher.stats,
		lastCycle = Scheduler.lastCycle,
		reaction = Scheduler.latencyStats(),
		scorecard = Scorecard.summary(),
		economy = EconomyPlanner.lastSummary,
		profile = Profiler.snapshot(),
	}
end

return Tracer.instrument("WarAI", WarAI)

]=]

---Source of a bundled module; an unknown path is a build error surfaced at runtime.
local function readModuleSource(modulePath)
	local source = MODULE_SOURCES[modulePath]
	if source == nil then
		return error(string.format("Loader: module '%s' is not in the bundle", modulePath))
	end
	return source
end

local moduleCache = {}
local loadingStack = {}
-- The executor's require, kept before modules shadow the name: game ModuleScripts load through it.
local nativeRequire = require

---Load a module by slash path, compiling it with its own chunk name so tracebacks name the file.
---A ModuleScript instance (a game module such as ReplicatedStorage.Common.ArmyCommon) is loaded
---with the executor's require instead.
---@param modulePath string | Instance
---@return any
local function requireModule(modulePath)
	if typeof(modulePath) == "Instance" then
		return nativeRequire(modulePath)
	end

	local cached = moduleCache[modulePath]
	if cached ~= nil then
		return cached.value
	end

	for _, loadingPath in ipairs(loadingStack) do
		if loadingPath == modulePath then
			return error(string.format("Loader: circular require of '%s' (stack: %s)", modulePath, table.concat(loadingStack, " -> ")))
		end
	end

	local source = readModuleSource(modulePath)
	-- The wrapper shares line 1 with the module so reported line numbers match the file.
	local chunk, compileError = loadstring("return function(require) " .. source .. "\nend", "=" .. modulePath)

	if chunk == nil then
		return error(string.format("Loader: '%s' failed to compile: %s", modulePath, tostring(compileError)))
	end

	table.insert(loadingStack, modulePath)

	local loadOk, moduleValue = xpcall(function()
		return chunk()(requireModule)
	end, function(failure)
		return debug.traceback(tostring(failure), 2)
	end)

	table.remove(loadingStack)

	if not loadOk then
		return error(string.format("Loader: '%s' failed to load:\n%s", modulePath, tostring(moduleValue)), 0)
	end

	if moduleValue == nil then
		return error(string.format("Loader: '%s' returned nil", modulePath))
	end

	moduleCache[modulePath] = { value = moduleValue }

	return moduleValue
end

return requireModule("Main")
